### [1] [$q$-Berezin sectorial operators with applications to $q$-Berezin number inequalities and $q$-Berezin ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21723)
*Saikat Mahapatra,Subhadip Halder,Riddhick Birbonshi,Arnab Patra,Kallol Paul*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文引入了一类新的算子——q-Berezin扇形算子，作为q-扇形算子的推广。通过在Hardy-Hilbert空间上给出例子，证明了存在q-Berezin扇形但非q-扇形算子的算子。文章还推导了与该类算子相关的q-Berezin数的新不等式，并研究了Bergman空间上各类算子（包括加权移位和特定合成算子）的q-Berezin值域的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展q-扇形算子类，引入更广泛的算子类别，并研究其在Berezin数框架下的性质，以丰富算子理论中扇形算子与Berezin数理论的研究。

Method: 定义q-Berezin扇形算子并证明其与q-扇形算子的包含关系；在Hardy-Hilbert空间上构造反例；推导q-Berezin数的新不等式；在Bergman空间上分析加权移位算子和合成算子的q-Berezin值域几何结构。

Result: 证明了q-Berezin扇形算子类严格包含q-扇形算子类（存在前者成立而后者不成立的算子）；获得了q-Berezin数的若干新不等式；刻画了Bergman空间上加权移位和某些合成算子的q-Berezin值域几何性质。

Conclusion: q-Berezin扇形算子构成一个比q-扇形算子更广泛的算子类别，其相关不等式和值域几何结构的建立为该理论后续发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a new class of operators, called $q$-Berezin sectorial operators, as an extension of the class of $q$-sectorial operators. By presenting examples on the Hardy-Hilbert space, we show that there exist operators which are $q$-Berezin sectorial but not $q$-sectorial. Several new inequalities for the $q$-Berezin number associated with this class of operators are also derived. In addition, we investigate the geometric structure of the $q$-Berezin range for various classes of operators on the Bergman space, including weighted shift and certain composition operators.

</details>


### [2] [On $φ$-Best Proximity Points and Proximal-type Algorithms in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22088)
*Priyanka Priyadarshini Behera,C. Nahak*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Banach空间中φ-最佳邻近点的存在性与收敛性问题，通过收缩投影算法建立了强收敛定理以计算邻近弱抑制映射的公共φ-最佳邻近点，并进一步提出了求解分裂φ-最佳邻近点与变分问题的投影方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非自映射的非线性问题，φ-最佳邻近点的存在性和收敛性分析是变分分析与非线性分析中的重要课题。现有的方法在处理非自映射时存在局限性，需要更有效的算法来求解公共φ-最佳邻近点及其与变分问题的耦合。

Method: 采用收缩投影算法（shrinking projection algorithm）来构造迭代序列，证明其在Banach空间中强收敛于邻近弱抑制映射的公共φ-最佳邻近点；同时设计了投影方案来处理分裂φ-最佳邻近点与变分问题的联合求解。

Result: 建立了计算公共φ-最佳邻近点的强收敛定理，并给出了求解分裂φ-最佳邻近点与变分问题的投影方案。

Conclusion: 本文的研究为求解涉及非自映射的非线性问题提供了新的分析工具和算法框架，拓展了最佳邻近点理论在Banach空间中的应用。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to study the existence and convergence of $φ$-best proximity points in Banach spaces. A strong convergence theorem is established by employing a shrinking projection algorithm designed to compute common $φ$-best proximity points of a proximally weakly suppressive mapping. Finally, we address a projection scheme to solve split $φ$-best proximity point and variational problem in a Banach space. These contributions provide new tools for solving nonlinear problems involving non-self mappings.

</details>


### [3] [Inner product bounds via the Moore-Penrose inverse with applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22220)
*A. Sheikhhosseini,Gh. Aghamollaei,M. Sababheh*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Hilbert空间算子的Moore-Penrose广义逆，提出了若干Cauchy-Schwarz型内积不等式，并以此推导出数值半径-范数界的新的精细化与推广版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cauchy-Schwarz不等式在内积空间和算子理论中具有基础性地位，而数值半径-范数界的估计是算子理论中的核心问题之一。现有结果在算子组合形式和应用范围上仍有局限性，因此需要借助广义逆工具来获得更精细、更一般化的不等式。

Method: 利用Hilbert空间算子的Moore-Penrose广义逆这一工具，构造并证明若干Cauchy-Schwarz型内积不等式；将这些不等式作为标准应用，推导出涉及不同算子组合的数值半径-范数界的精细化与推广版本。

Result: 获得了若干Cauchy-Schwarz型内积界，并在此基础上给出了包含不同算子组合的广义形式的数值半径-范数不等式，这些结果是对已有结论的精细化与推广。

Conclusion: Moore-Penrose广义逆为推导内积不等式及数值半径-范数界提供了有效工具，所得结果扩展了经典不等式在一般算子组合情形下的适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, by implementing the generalized inverse, known as the Moore-Penrose inverse, for Hilbert space operators, several inner product bounds that are of Cauchy-Schwarz type are presented. As a standard application, some new refined and generalized versions of numerical radius-norm bounds are also stated, in a generalized form that involves different combinations of operators.

</details>

### [5] [Fibre-to-Section Lifting over Measure-Preserving Dynamics: Variational Reciprocity and Memory-Reversal Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22433)
*Lei Luo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了定义在概率空间上的非线性与变分问题中，纤维到可测截面提升所产生的结构性障碍，涵盖依赖分解、Koopman记忆通道的可辨识性、时间反转不变性、邻近算子刚性以及分支粘贴序列的相对紧性等若干核心结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多非线性与变分问题以纤维形式定义在概率空间上，而动力学相关的对象是由保测变换耦合的可测截面。理解从纤维到截面的提升过程中产生的结构性障碍，对于刻画变分系统的可辨识性、记忆结构与遍历性质具有根本意义。

Method: 在实Hilbert空间中利用全局连续可微势函数的封闭子空间独立性互反性建立了有限正交分解的加性依赖分解；通过Koopman记忆通道的轨道分离条件逐通道解决经典的Hilbert空间势性准则，得到前向与反向记忆的移位伴随平衡；借助有限差分层次的梯度势条件推导关于时间反转的记忆不变性；利用凸势表示研究连续邻近算子的刚性，并通过不连续选择说明连续性不可移除；在点态非退化双分支条件下将分支粘贴残差消失等价于分支标签的渐近不变性，进而结合有限生成遍历保测作用建立强遍历性与分支粘贴近似零序列相对紧性之间的精确阈值关系。

Result: 得到了Hilbert空间中势函数依赖分解的加性结构；证明了有限Koopman记忆通道在尖锐轨道分离条件下恰好可辨识；建立了全局变分记忆的反转完备性及单侧记忆退化为当前状态局部性；给出连续邻近算子继承凸势刚性、不连续情形例外；证明强遍历性是分支粘贴近似零序列相对紧性的精确阈值，并辅以强紧性判别准则和非凸Wasserstein最小化步示例。

Conclusion: 纤维到截面提升在变分系统中产生本质的结构性约束：记忆结构具有时间反转完备性，单侧记忆不可全局变分实现，邻近算子的连续性与凸势刚性不可分离，而强遍历性精确刻画了分支粘贴序列的紧性行为。这些结果勾勒出可测选择提升理论中变分性与动力学约束之间深刻而系统的联系。

Abstract: Many nonlinear and variational problems are posed fibrewise over a probability space, whereas the dynamically relevant objects are measurable sections coupled by measure-preserving transformations. We study structural obstructions created by this fibre-to-section lifting. First, for a global continuously differentiable potential on a real Hilbert space, absence of influence between closed subspaces is reciprocal; for finite orthogonal decompositions this yields an additive decomposition over dependency components. Second, finite Koopman memory channels are identifiable exactly under a sharp orbit-separation condition. Resolving the classical Hilbert-space potentiality criterion channel by channel then gives a shifted-adjoint balance between forward and reverse memories. At the global finite-difference level, if a section map with arbitrary finite dynamical memory is the gradient of a global continuously differentiable potential, its genuinely active lags are invariant under time reversal. Thus globally variational memory is reversal-complete, and one-sided memory collapses to present-state locality. Continuous proximity operators inherit the same rigidity through their convex-potential representation; a discontinuous proximal selection shows that continuity cannot generally be removed. Finally, under pointwise nondegeneracy of two coherent branches, vanishing branchpasting residual is equivalent to asymptotic invariance of the branch labels. For finitely generated ergodic probability-preserving actions, strong ergodicity is therefore the exact threshold for relative compactness of all branch-pasting approximate-zero sequences. We also record a strong-compactness criterion for full measurable-selection lifts and a nonconvex Wasserstein minimizing-step illustration.

</details>


### [6] [Linear dynamics of composition operators on Schwartz spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22450)
*Javier Henriquez-Amador,Brian Rojas*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了S(R)上可逆复合算子的动力学性质，证明它们从不具有广义双曲性，当符号有不动点时也不具有正跟踪性；同时给出了符号使复合算子具有正拓扑扩张性的充分条件，包括仿射符号的完全刻画、奇数阶多项式符号的扩张性，以及偶数阶多项式符号不能产生扩张性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局部凸空间中研究线性算子的拓扑扩张性等动力学性质是一个新兴方向，作者希望通过复合算子这一自然桥梁，建立符号映射动力学与诱导线性算子动力学之间的深刻联系，既提供新的正例也提供障碍性结果。

Method: 通过分析S(R)上复合算子C_phi的结构，研究其广义双曲性、正跟踪性（shadowing）和正拓扑扩张性。利用符号函数的动力学性质（不动点、奇偶度多项式等）来建立充分条件，并对仿射符号给出完全分类。

Result: 证明了：1) S(R)上的可逆复合算子绝不具有广义双曲性；2) 符号有不动点时算子不满足正跟踪性；3) 给出符号满足充分条件时算子正拓扑扩张的判据；4) 仿射符号被完全刻画；5) 奇数次多项式符号可产生扩张性，而偶数次多项式不能。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了符号动力学与诱导复合算子线性动力学之间的强关联，为局部凸空间中拓扑扩张性提供了新的实例与障碍。

Abstract: In this note, we show that invertible composition operators on S(R) are never generalized hyperbolic and, when the symbol has a fixed point, the corresponding operator fails to have the positive shadowing property. We then establish sufficient conditions on the symbol under which the associated composition operator is positively topologically expansive. In particular, we obtain a complete characterization for affine symbols and prove expansivity for broad classes of odd-degree polynomial symbols, while showing that polynomial symbols of even degree cannot generate topologically expansive operators. Our results reveal a strong connection between the dynamics of the symbol and the linear dynamics of the induced composition operator, and provide new examples and obstructions for topological expansivity in locally convex spaces.

</details>


### [7] [Observation Schemes and Irregularity in Linear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22616)
*Manuel Saavedra*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于观测方案空间Υ的结构化框架，统一了线性动力学中的不规则性概念（如Li-Yorke混沌、平均Li-Yorke混沌和分布混沌），并建立了保证大线性结构存在的抽象准则，获得稠密可线性化与空间化结果，以及全局的不规则性三分律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性动力学中各种混沌/不规则性概念（Li-Yorke混沌、平均Li-Yorke混沌、分布混沌）看似独立发展，缺乏统一的理论框架。研究者希望将动力学行为与观测机制分离，从而在统一框架下理解这些概念之间的内在联系。

Method: 引入观测方案空间Υ，将不规则性分为绝对(μ_m)-不规则性与分布(μ_m)-不规则性两大类别。通过Dirac测度与Cesàro平均分别还原经典混沌概念。建立两条抽象准则以刻画具有大线性结构（稠密子空间与闭无限维子空间）的不规则向量集，并在自然密度假设下推导全局三分律。

Result: 获得了绝对(μ_m)-不规则向量和分布(μ_m)-不规则向量的稠密可线性化与空间化结果；在密度假设下建立了关于Υ上不规则性全局行为的三分律，并揭示了各类观测方案生成混沌行为的刚性现象（rigidity phenomena）。

Conclusion: 本文提供的统一框架表明，各种经典的混沌/不规则性概念本质上对应于不同的观测方案，通过结构化的抽象准则可以系统性地导出大线性结构存在性结果，并揭示了观测方案类别与混沌行为类型之间的深层次对应关系。

Abstract: We develop a structural framework for irregularity in linear dynamics centered on the space $Υ$ of observation schemes. This approach separates the underlying dynamical behavior from the observation mechanism and provides a unified setting in which classical notions such as Li--Yorke chaos, mean Li--Yorke chaos, and distributional chaos arise as particular cases corresponding to Dirac measures and Cesàro averages. We establish two abstract criteria ensuring the existence of large linear structures, leading to dense-lineability and spaceability results for both absolutely $(μ_m)$-irregular and distributionally $(μ_m)$-irregular vectors. Furthermore, under a natural density assumption, we obtain a trichotomy describing the global behavior of irregularity across $Υ$, together with rigidity phenomena for the classes of observation schemes generating each type of chaotic behavior.

</details>


### [8] [The Douglas question for functions of the form $\overline{z}+h$ with $h$ in the disk algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22811)
*Jiawei Wang,Xianfeng Zhao*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在开单位圆盘上，如果$|\overline{z}+h|$被某个正常数从下方界定（其中$h$属于圆盘代数），则Bergman空间上的Toeplitz算子$T_{\overline{z}+h}$是可逆的。这为Douglas问题在Bergman空间上的肯定回答提供了一类广泛的调和函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Douglas问题（Douglas conjecture）是算子理论中的一个重要开放性问题，它关注在何种条件下，由某个符号函数诱导的Toeplitz算子具有可逆性。作者旨在寻找Bergman空间上更多类符号函数，使得对应的Toeplitz算子可逆，从而为Douglas问题提供更多肯定答案的情形。

Method: 作者选取形如$\overline{z}+h$的调和符号函数，其中$h$属于圆盘代数（即$h$在开单位圆盘上解析且在闭单位圆盘上连续）。关键假设是$|\overline{z}+h|$在开单位圆盘上有正的下界，在此条件下证明Toeplitz算子$T_{\overline{z}+h}$的逆存在性。

Result: 成功证明了当$|\overline{z}+h|$在开单位圆盘上被某正常数从下方界定且$h$属于圆盘代数时，Bergman空间上的Toeplitz算子$T_{\overline{z}+h}$可逆。这一结果涵盖了一类广泛的调和符号函数。

Conclusion: 该结果扩展了Douglas问题在Bergman空间上已知可逆的符号函数类别，为调和符号函数的Toeplitz算子可逆性提供了新的充分条件，进一步推进了对Douglas问题的研究。

Abstract: We prove that the Toeplitz operator $T_{\overline{z}+h}$ is invertible on the Bergman space provided that
  $|\overline{z}+h|$ is bounded below by some positive constant on the open unit disk,
  where $h$ is in the disk algebra. This provides a general class of harmonic functions for which the answer to the Douglas question on the Bergman space is affirmative.

</details>


### [9] [Helson Forms and Integral Operators on Bergman Spaces of Dirichlet Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22882)
*Tengfei Ma,Yufeng Lu,Chao Zu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究Dirichlet级数的Hilbertian Bergman空间上的一族算子T_α（α>1），确定了其谱类型：本质谱和绝对连续谱均为[0,Λ_α]（绝对连续重数为1），奇异连续谱为空，无嵌入特征值，仅在Λ_α之上存在有限个简单特征值。文中还证明了存在唯一的α_*∈(1,2)，使得1<α<α_*时Λ_α之上无特征值，而α>α_*时总有特征值存在。作为证明的一部分，作者建立了一类带权积分Hankel算子的谱定理，并研究了相应的加权Helson形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经典乘法Hilbert矩阵出发，将其推广到带有广义除数系数d_α的加权乘法Hilbert矩阵，系统研究这类算子在Bergman空间上的谱结构，并探讨加权Helson形式的有界性与紧性。

Method: 将算子T_α表示为自然正交基下的加权乘法Hilbert矩阵，利用广义除数系数进行加权；通过建立一般类带权积分Hankel算子（核为w(x)b(x+y)w̄(y)）的谱定理，借助本质谱、绝对连续谱、奇异连续谱与特征值的系统分析完成谱型判定；对Helson形式的有界性、紧性及有限正测度诱导形式的有界性给出刻画。

Result: 确定了T_α的完整谱型：本质谱与绝对连续谱为[0,Λ_α]且绝对连续重数为1，奇异连续谱为空，无嵌入特征值，Λ_α之上仅有有限个简单特征值。证明存在唯一临界值α_*∈(1,2)区分Λ_α之上有无特征值两种情形。此外，给出加权Helson形式的有界性与紧性充分条件，并完全刻画了有限正测度诱导形式的有界性。

Conclusion: 完整地刻画了算子族T_α的谱结构，揭示了α=α_*处发生定性转变的临界现象，并将结果推广至更一般的加权Hankel算子与Helson形式框架中。

Abstract: We study a family of operators $T_α$, $α>1$, on Hilbertian Bergman spaces of Dirichlet series. In the natural orthonormal basis, these operators are represented by weighted multiplicative Hilbert matrices involving the generalized divisor coefficients $d_α$. We determine their spectral type. The essential and absolutely continuous spectra are $[0,Λ_α]$, with absolutely continuous multiplicity one; the singular continuous spectrum is empty, and there are no embedded eigenvalues. There are only finitely many eigenvalues above $Λ_α$, and all of them are simple. We also prove that there is a unique $α_*\in(1,2)$ such that no eigenvalues occur above $Λ_α$ for $1<α<α_*$, whereas such eigenvalues exist for every $α>α_*$. As part of the proof, we establish a spectral theorem for a general class of weighted integral Hankel operators with kernels $w(x)b(x+y)\overline{w(y)}$. Finally, we study the corresponding weighted Helson forms, give sufficient conditions for boundedness and compactness, and characterize boundedness for forms induced by finite positive measures.

</details>


### [10] [The pointwise multiscale texture operator: analytical foundations and functional characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23042)
*Carlos Fernández García,Zulima Fernández Muñiz*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文从高斯尺度空间演化的视角研究纹理的泛函分析结构，提出了一个多尺度纹理算子，证明了其在L^p空间上的有界性、希尔伯特空间中的无穷阶光滑效应及谱特征（局部高斯带通滤波器）。通过r-adic Difference-of-Gaussians离散化，建立了Littlewood-Paley型稳定性估计和重建公式，并证明由此定义的纹理范数等价于经典Besov和Sobolev空间范数，从而建立了纹理持存性与经典函数正则性之间的严格桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纹理通常通过统计描述符、滤波器组响应或多尺度表示来刻画，但其泛函分析结构尚未被深入探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，从高斯尺度空间演化的数学视角为纹理提供严格的泛函分析基础，并揭示纹理持存性与经典函数空间（Besov、Sobolev）之间的内在联系。

Method: 作者将纹理解释为局部结构在高斯扩散下的尺度持存性，由此定义多尺度纹理算子。在理论层面，证明了该算子在L^p上的有界性、希尔伯特空间中的无穷阶光滑效应，并给出谱特征（等价于局部高斯带通滤波）。在离散化层面，受高斯尺度对数组织的启发，引入r-adic DoG离散化并证明Littlewood-Paley型稳定性估计及显式重建公式。基于此多尺度表示定义纹理范数，诱导一族Banach空间（含希尔伯特情形），并证明其与经典Besov和Sobolev尺度等价。

Result: 建立了纹理持存性在Gaussian尺度演化下的首个泛函分析刻画，证明经典Besov和Sobolev正则性可由多尺度纹理持存性等价描述，同时在高斯尺度空间理论、调和分析与基于纹理的变分模型之间建立了严格联系。

Conclusion: 该研究为纹理分析提供了全新的泛函分析框架，揭示纹理持存性与经典函数正则性的数学等价性，为调和分析、尺度空间理论与纹理变分建模提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Texture is commonly described through statistical descriptors, filter-bank responses, or multiscale representations, yet its functional-analytic structure remains largely unexplored. In this work we investigate texture from the viewpoint of Gaussian scale-space evolution, interpreting it as the persistence of local structures across scales. This perspective naturally leads to a multiscale texture operator that quantifies the evolution of local image structures under Gaussian diffusion.
  We establish the fundamental analytical properties of this operator, proving its boundedness on $L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, its infinite-order smoothing effect in the Hilbert setting, and an explicit spectral characterization showing that it acts as a localized Gaussian band-pass filter. Motivated by the logarithmic organization of Gaussian scales, we introduce an $r$-adic Difference-of-Gaussians discretization and prove Littlewood--Paley-type stability estimates together with an explicit reconstruction formula.
  Building on this multiscale representation, we define multiscale texture norms that quantify the persistence of local structures across scales. These norms induce a natural family of Banach spaces, with a distinguished Hilbertian case, and we prove that they provide an equivalent characterization of the classical Besov and Sobolev scales. Consequently, classical functional regularity can be described intrinsically through the persistence of texture under Gaussian scale evolution.
  These results establish the first functional-analytic characterization of texture persistence under Gaussian scale evolution, revealing that classical Besov and Sobolev regularity admit an equivalent description in terms of multiscale texture persistence. This provides a rigorous bridge between Gaussian scale-space theory, harmonic analysis, and variational models based on texture.

</details>

Abstract: Factorization for positive semidefinite matrix-valued polynomials over a nonsingular compact affine real surface is established. An explicit example shows a conclusion of [Dri25, Theorem, p. 519] that arises organically from its proof need not hold. The difficulty is traced to [Dri25, Theorem 3.7].

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is twofold. In particular, we give a complete answer to a question raised by Makoto Masumoto. Second, motivated by Aikawa's work, we study the boundedness of the operator norm of $P_α$, where $P_α[\varphi]$ is the Dirichlet solution of such equation for the boundary data $\varphi$. By using alternative proof techniques, we obtain an equivalent characterization of the boundedness of the operator norm of $P_α$. Finally, we show that the Girela-Peláez conjecture holds positively for more general classes of functions induced by the Möbius invariant Laplacian operator.

Abstract: We establish a dynamic generalization of Nevanlinna-Pick interpolation for functions invariant under the action of a finite Blaschke product $f$, reducing global bounded holomorphic extension on orbit spaces to structured block-kernel positivity. Furthermore, we demonstrate that membership in $H^\infty(\mathbb{D})$ is universally detectable via deformations by any finite Blaschke product. These results are proved via an underlying operator-theoretic lifting framework for covariant operator pairs in the spirit of Sarason. Finally, in the setting of non-commutative function theory, we show that despite the universal validity of the matrix-valued von Neumann inequality over the free polydisk, Arveson-type complete spectral set representations break down for non-commutative inner varieties.

Abstract: Motivated by scattering theory, this paper proves general results about the distribution of zeros in logarithmic neighborhoods of the real axis for a certain class of functions holomorphic in the closed lower half-plane. We define a new \emph{indicator function} that measures the growth of the holomorphic function along logarithmic curves, and connect this to the distribution of zeros of the function. This is related to classical results on the distribution of zeros in sectors for entire functions of completely regular growth. These results can be applied to the determinant of the scattering matrix of a Schrodinger operator on odd-dimensional Euclidean space, yielding bounds on resonance counting functions for logarithmic neighborhoods of the real axis. As a further application, we study a certain family of potentials in one dimension having jump-like singularities. Using our complex-analytic results we show that the singularities can lead to many -- and even infinitely many -- strings of resonances along logarithmic curves. We connect the properties of these strings of resonances, including their location and linear density, both to the parameters describing the singularities of the potential and to the asymptotic behavior of the scattering determinant.

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of the singular spectrum of self-adjoint operators under families of Hermitian perturbations, even non-compact ones. Under mild assumptions we have the ``shift'' of the singular spectrum for almost all values of the parameter. Moreover, if we consider trace class perturbations, we observe the ``shift'' outside a countable set of the values of the parameter. While similar results were known for finite rank perturbations, the extensions to trace class perturbations are far from easy, and the proofs require significant new ideas.
  In addition, some of our results hold (surprisingly enough!) even for all positive and bounded perturbations. This is a consequence of our generalized Aleksandrov disintegration theorem established in this paper. We use some advanced techniques, involving Sz.-Nagy--Foia\c s theory and the operator ${\bf A}_2$ condition.

Motivation: 该问题的动机是证明任意紧致光滑平面区域的Steklov等谱族在C∞拓扑下具有紧性，这是对逆谱问题中谱决定几何性质的紧性刻画的重要一步，此前该问题一直悬而未决。

Result: 成功证明了紧致光滑平面区域的Steklov等谱族在C∞拓扑下的紧性，完整回答了Colbois、Girouard、Gordon和Sher提出的开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过将Steklov谱紧性问题分解为固定共形类的紧性和退化排除两部分，并综合运用迹比较原理、Steklov zeta分析、双曲曲面行列式展开以及几何非塌缩估计，完成了平面区域Steklov等谱族紧性的证明。

Abstract: We prove that every Steklov isospectral family of compact smooth planar domains is compact in the $C^\infty$ topology, answering an open question of Colbois, Girouard, Gordon, and Sher. The proof has two main parts. First, a trace comparison principle for Dirichlet-to-Neumann operators yields monotonicity of negative Steklov zeta values and compactness within a fixed conformal class for genus-zero flat surfaces. Second, we analyze the normalized Steklov determinant on degenerating hyperbolic surfaces with geodesic boundary. Its asymptotics are expressed in terms of shrinking boundary components and small Neumann and Dirichlet eigenvalues, which in genus zero are compared with weighted graph Laplacians. This rules out degeneration under a bound on the total hyperbolic boundary length. We finally establish this bound for planar domains by geometric non-collapse estimates.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we study $L^p$-estimates for maximal averaging operators $\mathcal M$ along hypersurfaces $S$ in $\mathbb{R}^{3}$ which are the graph of a mixed homogeneous function $Φ$ which is analytic away from the origin. The closure of such a surface will pass through the origin, so that the usual transversality condition that had been imposed in many previous works on maximal averages along hypersurfaces will not hold even when $Φ$ is analytic at the origin.
  As our main result, under mild assumptions which are satisfied for instance for every mixed homogeneous polynomial $Φ,$ we determine the critical Lebesgue exponent $p_c$ for which $\mathcal M$ is $L^p$-bounded for every $p>p_c,$ but unbounded for $p<p_c,$ in terms of multiplicities of the real roots of the Hessian determinant of $Φ.$
  It turns out that the study of the contributions by neighborhoods of a certain type of roots is closely related to recent work by Dendrinos, Ikromov and the first and third author on sharp estimates for a maximal averaging operator along a transversal hypersurface of an ``exceptional'' class, whose $L^p$-boundedness had been an open problem for a long time and which has recently been established by means of their new theory of FIO-cone multipliers.

Abstract: We prove a critical two-depth form of Journé's covering lemma. Nevertheless, \[ \int_ΩH_s(x) dx =\sum_{I\in\mathcal U}(e_1(I;Ω)+1)^{-s}(e_2(I;Ω)+1)^{-(1-s)}|I| \lesssim\frac{1}{s(1-s)}|Ω|. \] The exponent sum $s+(1-s)=1$ is sharp, which is why we call this estimate critical. In fact, for a sufficiently small absolute constant $c>0$, the height $H_s$ satisfies \[ \int_Ωe^{c s(1-s)H_s(x)} dx\lesssim|Ω|. \] The $s(1-s)$-dependence in both estimates is also sharp.
  The critical packing estimate fails for unrestricted tri-parameter rectangles, but exactly the same $L^1$ estimate holds for dyadic Zygmund rectangles, by which we mean rectangles $I=I^1\times I^2\times I^3$ with $\ell(I^3)=\ell(I^1)\ell(I^2)$.

Abstract: The asymptotic phase for the zeros of a Jacobi polynomial contains additive constants $κ_r$ that are not determined by the phase equation. We study their denominators as polynomials in $A=α^2$ and $B=β^2$. We prove that the odd part of $\denκ_r$ divides $\operatorname{lcm}(1,3,\ldots,2r-1)$ and that $2^{E_r}κ_r$ is $2$-adically integral, where $E_r=3r-1+ν_2((r-1)!)$. The extremal coefficient is governed by the valuation law \[
  ν_2\!\left(\sum_{j=0}^{m}\binom mj\frac1{2j+1}\right) =m+ν_2(m+1), \] which follows from the identity $\sum_{k\ge0}k!/(2k+1)!!=0$ in $\mathbb Q_2$. Abstract: We define the notion of regular parabolic (bi-)Lipschitz images as the parabolic (bi-)Lipschitz maps from $n$-dimensional space time which, up to translation, fix the $t$ variable and whose spatial components are each regular parabolic Lipschitz functions. Abstract: We establish quantitative versions of the law of rare events and binomial approximations in non-commutative probability settings, including the free, Boolean, and monotone convolution frameworks. Abstract: Given a real-valued function $f$, let $\mathcal{N}_f(δ, Q)$ be the number of rational points with denominators at most $Q\ge 1$ in the $(δ/Q)$-tubular neighbourhood of the graph of the function $f$. A heuristic predicts that the number of such points grows like the area of the neighbourhood provided that $δ$ is big enough (in a suitable sense).
  Considerable efforts have been committed to prove this heuristic for regular curves. This culminated in the works by Vaughan \& Velani~(2006) and by Huang~(2015) establishing an asymptotic expansion for $\mathcal{N}_f(δ, Q)$ provided that $δ\gg Q^{-1+ε}$ for some $ε>0$ when the map $f$ is, among other assumptions, twice continuously differentiable.
  The present work deals with the thus-far unexplored regime where minimal regularity conditions are imposed on the curve. More precisely, it is concerned with the case where the map $f$ is an a.s. realisation of the graph of Brownian motion.
  The main result establishes the existence of an almost sure asymptotic expansion for the counting function for all values of $δ$, with the exception of a critical regime, thereby going well beyond the theory currently available for regular curves. A key ingredient in the proof is the derivation of the area heuristic, which relies on establishing the a.s. asymptotics of the area of the tubular neighbourhood of the graph of Brownian motion.
  This result has two main consequences: firstly, it completes the counting aspect of the theory of Diophantine approximation on the graph of Brownian motion initiated by Sprindžuk (1979). Secondly, it hints at the existence of a theory unifying the analysis of rational points near a curve on the one hand and, on the other, its local Hölder regularity and fine-scale oscillations. It thus builds a seemingly new bridge between Number Theory and Multifractal Analysis.

</details>

Let $g(n)$ be the largest integer $k$ such that every set $A\subseteq[n]$ with $|A|\ge n/2$ contains an ascending wave of length $k$. Alon and Spencer proved that \[

Abstract: Write P(N) for the assertion that every principal minor of the Fourier matrix of order N is nonzero. Abstract: Determining the properties of a random polynomial has fuelled significant investigation over the past century. One driving force of this research is a 1936 paper of Van der Waerden. Fix $n \geq 3$ and let $E_n(B)$ be the number of monic, irreducible, non-$S_n$ polynomials $f = X^n + a_1 X^{n-1} + \cdots + a_n$ with $|a_j| \leq B$ for all $j$. A recent breakthrough of Bhargava bounds $E_n(B) \ll B^{n-1}$. This spectacularly resolves a conjecture of Van der Waerden, but leaves open its stronger form, namely that $E_n(B) = o(B^{n-1})$. Inspired by recent progress, we now address this strong form.

Assuming a twisted function field version of Chowla's conjecture, we prove this claim. This not only connects two active and challenging areas of research, but also conditionally resolves the strong Van der Waerden conjecture for all $n \geq 7$.

Our methods also apply to the analogous problem of counting square discriminants of polynomials that are not necessarily monic. In addition to describing our new contributions, we briefly elaborate on the various conjectures appearing in Van der Waerden's paper and some of the exciting recent work of others in this area of arithmetic statistics.

Abstract: Motivated by the classical Lagrange spectrum and the reciprocal formulation of the Lagrange spectrum in continued-fraction theory, we introduce a normalized approximation function $\mathcal{N}(α)$ for approximation by multiple zeta-star values. At each depth, the approximation error is minimized over all admissible indices and normalized by the binary scale determined by their weights. The function $\mathcal{N}(α)$ is then obtained by taking the limit inferior as the depth tends to infinity.

We prove that the depthwise approximation functions are upper semicontinuous, that $\mathcal{N}$ is Borel measurable, and that its zero set is a dense $G_δ$ subset of $(1,+\infty)$. We also derive a natural-prefix approximation estimate showing that a large next digit produces an exceptionally good normalized approximation. Combined with the author's metric resultson Diophantine approximation of multiple zeta-star values, this gives a full-measure theorem under a logarithmically reinforced divergence condition and, in particular, proves that $\mathcal{N}(α)=0$ for Lebesgue almost every $α>1$. Abstract: A curious property of integers, first observed by D.~R. Kaprekar in 1949, is as follows: write the digits of a 4-digit number in both descending and ascending order, and then find the positive difference between these integers. In general, if we start with any integer and iterate this process, it either ends at a fixed point (as with 4 digits) or enters a cycle of numbers, called a Kaprekar cycle. Then $c^{-1}d\equiv 1$ mod $Π^{n+1}Δ$ hence, setting $d'=c^{-1}d$, has $nr(d')=1$ and level $n'>n$. Repetition ends in $[Δ^\times,Δ^\times]$ when the level gets sufficiently large.

Method: 通过相位归一化的二次高斯和恢复乘性及局部求值，使得结果对所有奇素数幂q成立（无需Florea假设的q≡1 mod 4）；分析平方对偶母函数在次圆上的极点结构。

Result: 完整二次项为q^{2g/3}(α_{g mod 3}g + β_{g mod 3})，系数为实数，依赖g模3且最小周期恰为3；在三个剩余类中至少有两个类中非实极点贡献gq^{2g/3}阶的项；q=3的精确有限系综矩验证了三个剩余类趋势。

Abstract: Let $q$ be an odd prime power. We revisit Florea's asymptotic formula for the first moment of quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions over the odd-degree hyperelliptic ensemble $\mathcal H_{2g+1}$, and show that its secondary term is not the complete one. The square-dual generating function has three double poles of the same modulus on the secondary circle: the positive real one reproduces Florea's polynomial, while the two nonreal conjugate poles contribute at the same order. The complete secondary term is $q^{2g/3}(α_{g\bmod3}g+β_{g\bmod3})$, with real coefficients whose dependence on the genus has minimal period exactly three, and on at least two of the three residue classes the nonreal poles contribute a term of order $gq^{2g/3}$. We also prove the formula for every odd prime power $q$, whereas Florea assumes $q\equiv1\bmod4$ throughout; this is achieved by a phase-normalized quadratic Gauss sum that restores the multiplicativity and the local evaluations her argument uses. Exact finite-ensemble moments for $q=3$ exhibit the three residue-class trends.

</details>

Abstract: We study congruences between special values of zeta functions and modular $L$-functions in cyclotomic towers. The underlying mechanism is an integral norm congruence for finite-level Iwasawa-theoretic elements, which becomes stronger as one ascends the tower. In the $\mathrm{GL}_1$ setting, this gives congruences for Dedekind zeta values over totally real fields, with applications to higher $K$-groups, generalized Bernoulli numbers, and Euler-Poincaré characteristics. In the $\mathrm{GL}_2$ setting, we use Mazur-Tate elements and their distribution relations to control the primitive twisted special values appearing at each cyclotomic level. Via Artin formalism, these give congruences for the corresponding base-change $L$-values over successive cyclotomic fields.

</details>


### [60] [Logarithmic Chowla Correlations Uniformly over Fixed Polylogarithmic Shift Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23500)
*Jizhou Guo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Liouville函数的对数和加权两点相关估计：对任意固定A>0，存在绝对常数c>0，使得1≤h≤(log x)^A范围内，∑_{n≤x} λ(n)λ(n+h)/n 一致地有O_A((log x)^{1-c})的上界，即在所有足够大的外部尺度上获得单次幂节省，且在整个固定多对数位移范围内一致成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Liouville函数的符号振荡行为，特别是其在短位移下的两点相关性的上界估计。该问题与Chowla猜想密切相关，是解析数论中关于乘法函数符号变化的核心问题之一。

Method: 将Pilatte的块分解与Menon的短指数和论证的灵活形式相结合。核心方法包括：(1)对所有参数依赖重演小弧(次要弧)部分；(2)通过同余类和特征扭曲短区间局部化主弧(主要弧)；(3)采用单边典型因子分解截断以去除筛补集；(4)利用灵活的圆截面消除h^{1/5}的损失；(5)在不相交的dyadic除数块上进行Hölder求和以保留功率节省；(6)中心项由Tao和Teräväinen的任意区间解耦定理处理。

Result: 获得了对任意固定A>0，1≤h≤(log x)^A范围内一致成立的(log x)^{1-c}上界，并且该估计对固定多对数盒子中任意两个不同位移也一致成立。结果是对数加权的，并未证明普通的Chowla猜想。

Conclusion: 该结果在Liouville函数两点相关的对数加权版本上首次获得了在所有足够大的外部尺度下的单次幂节省，为Chowla猜想的进一步研究提供了重要进展。但注意指数A需在x趋于无穷前固定，且结果带有对数权重，不涉及普通（非加权）的Chowla猜想证明。

Abstract: Let $λ(n)=(-1)^{Ω(n)}$ be the Liouville function. We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that, for every fixed $A>0$, $\displaystyle \sup_{1\le h\le(\log x)^A}\left|\sum_{n\le x}\frac{λ(n)λ(n+h)}{n}\right|\ll_A(\log x)^{1-c}.$ Thus a single power saving holds at every sufficiently large outer scale, uniformly throughout every fixed polylogarithmic shift range. The proof combines Pilatte's block decomposition with a flexible form of Menon's short-exponential-sum argument. The latter is proved here for every fixed power-logarithmic circle cutoff: we replay the minor arcs with all parameter dependencies exposed and localize the major arcs through residue classes and character-twisted short intervals. A one-sided typical-factorization truncation removes the sieve complement before Fourier inversion and hence introduces no loss in the shift. The remaining $h^{1/5}$ loss is cancelled by the flexible circle cutoff; a Hölder summation across the disjoint dyadic divisor blocks retains the resulting power saving. The centred term then follows from the arbitrary-interval decoupling theorem of Tao and Teräväinen. As a consequence, the same estimate holds uniformly for every pair of distinct shifts in a fixed polylogarithmic box. The exponent $A$ must be fixed before $x$ tends to infinity. The result is logarithmically weighted and does not prove the ordinary two-point Chowla conjecture.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 设d=2g+1>4，考虑系数空间A^{d-1}参数化的超椭圆族C_a: y^2=x^d+a_{d-1}x^{d-1}+...+a_1x+t。利用Zariski一般位置的系数向量，结合算术统计与代数几何方法，对素数p的约化性质进行分析；采用分圆扩张Q(a,ζ_d)进行基变换以实现完整的对称性。

Result: (1) 对Zariski一般的系数向量a，所有充分大的素数p处的每个素理想上，C_a均具有普通约化；(2) 对任意系数向量a，普通素数的自然密度至少为1/[Q(a,ζ_d):Q(a)]；(3) 基变换到Q(a,ζ_d)(t)后，普通素数的自然密度恰好为1。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了亏格g≥2超椭圆族在模素数约化下的普通性密度，证明了在足够大的素数处普通约化是"普遍"现象，且分圆基变换可以消除障碍使密度达到1，推广了椭圆曲线情形的经典结果。

Abstract: Let d=2g+1>4 and let A^{d-1} be the coefficient space parametrizing hyperelliptic families
  C_a: y^2 = x^d + a_{d-1}x^{d-1} + ... + a_1x+t
  over the t-line. We show that for a Zariski-generic coefficient vector a=(a_1,...,a_{d-1}) in \bar{Z}^{d-1}, for every prime p large enough, C_a has generically ordinary reduction at every prime above p.
  For arbitrary a in \bar{Z}^{d-1}, the set of ordinary primes of the hyperelliptic curve C: y^2=x^d+a_{d-1}x^{d-1}+...+a_1x+t over Q(a)(t) has natural density at least 1/[Q(a,ζ_d):Q(a)]; after base change to Q(a,ζ_d)(t), it has natural density 1.

</details>

Abstract: Let $D_n$ denote the supremum of the orthogonality defects over all $n$-dimensional Hermite--Korkine--Zolotarev (HKZ)-reduced bases. This paper shows that $\lim_{n\to\infty} \log D_n/(n\log n)=2$. We also determine the exact value $D_4=4375/1024$.

Abstract: Let $C\subset X(1)\times X(1)$ be a smooth curve defined over $\mathbb{C}_p$ with irreducible reduction. We study the intersection loci of $C$ with the modular subvarieties $Y_0(n)$. If the curve $C$ avoids points where both coordinates have supersingular reduction, or if a sequence $Y_0(a_n)$ is taken where the numbers $a_n$ have increasing divisibility by $p$, then these loci equidistribute to the unique canonical point of the analytification, $C^\text{Berk}$. If neither condition is satisfied, we expect equidistribution to fail and give a family of examples whose behaviour is believed to be typical. We also study the accumulation points of the set $C\cap\bigcup_nY_0(n)$ and show that this set is always non-discrete.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数表示理论是数论中的经典问题，而k-广义Fibonacci数作为自然数系的重要推广，其上的整数表示结构尚未被充分研究。特别地，有符号表示（Zeckendorf表示的一种变体）和重数分布问题联系着组合学、概率论与动力系统，探索其中的递推关系及极限分布有助于揭示数字系统与自相似测度之间的深层联系。

Result: 有符号零表示计数序列满足线性递推且特征多项式可显式确定；Fibonacci与Tribonacci情形下表示计数的递推分别与Tribonacci和Fibonacci序列产生意外的交叉联系。Tribonacci情形下表示重数由乘积公式Q_n(x)=∏_{k=2}^{n-1}(1+x^{T_k})编码，归一化后依分布收敛到参数为Tribonacci常数ρ的Bernoulli卷积，该极限分布满足自相似关系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了k-广义Fibonacci数表示理论中计数递推与经典序列（Fibonacci/Tribonacci）之间的双向联系，并通过概率方法将离散表示问题与连续自相似测度（Bernoulli卷积）统一起来，揭示了Tribonacci缩放下表示重数的极限分布的自然自相似结构。

Abstract: We study representations of integers using $k$-generalized Fibonacci numbers. For $k\geq 2$, we first consider signed representations of zero with coefficients in $\{-1,0,1\}$ and give a recursive description of their number. The resulting counting sequences satisfy linear recurrences whose characteristic polynomials are determined explicitly. In the Fibonacci and Tribonacci cases, these recurrences reveal unexpected connections between the corresponding representation counts and Tribonacci and Fibonacci sequences, respectively.
  We then consider representations with coefficients in $\{0,1\}$ in the Tribonacci case. Using a random inhomogeneous Tribonacci recurrence, we construct a binary-tree model in which representation multiplicities are encoded by a family of polynomials satisfying the product formula $Q_n(x)=\prod_{k=2}^{n-1}(1+x^{T_k})$. This product admits a probabilistic interpretation in terms of weighted Bernoulli sums. After normalization by $T_n$, these sums converge in distribution to the Bernoulli convolution $\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\varepsilon_jρ^{-j}$, where $ρ$ is the Tribonacci constant and the $\varepsilon_j$ are independent Bernoulli random variables. The limiting distribution satisfies a natural self-similarity relation, linking the representation problem to self-similar measures associated with Tribonacci scaling.

</details>

### [67] [A deterministic sin^2-type algorithm for complex cubic irrationalities with exact periodicity certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23281)
*Ludovic Tagnon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究并实现了一个确定性算法，用于解决Hermite在1848年提出的问题（共签名(1,1)情形的立方无理数周期表示，即Karpenkov问题4）。该算法实现了Karpenkov提出的解析扩展：得分表达式在(1,1)数据上严格为负（已给出闭式证明），选择最负得分，并通过声明顺序解决精确平局。在205个复三次多项式的样本上，所有运行均以精确单位证书投影闭合；在$[-3,3]^3$全盒上的穷举运动194/194全部闭合；跨457个变形基，终循环是标记格的\textbf{不变量}。为四个域计算了经认证的有限转移图；塑性情形在Lean 4中经机器验证（仅内核）。所有数据都配有公共存档和便携验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermite 1848年提出要求实数的表示，其周期性质刻画三次无理数。全实情形已由Karpenkov的$\sin^2$-算法解决，但复情形（签名(1,1)）即Karpenkov问题4仍未解决。本文旨在解决这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 实现一个确定性算法：(1)采用Karpenkov建议的解析扩展；(2)证明得分表达式在(1,1)数据上严格为负（闭式证明）；(3)选择最负得分；(4)通过声明顺序解决精确得分平局；(5)每一步转换通过$\mathbb{Q}(\alpha)$中的精确比较进行认证；(6)在Lean 4（内核仅）中机器验证。

Result: 在205个复三次多项式样本上，所有运行均以投影闭合和精确单位证书结束。在$[-3,3]^3$全盒上的穷举运动194/194全部闭合。跨457个变形基，终循环被证明是标记格的\textbf{不变量}。为四个数域计算了经认证的有限转移图。

Conclusion: 该算法成功解决了Karpenkov问题4（Hermite问题在复（签名(1,1)）情形下的解）。终循环是标记格的不变量这一性质为问题的解决提供了强有力的理论基础。全部数据公开可用，并配有便携验证器，保证了研究的可复现性。

Abstract: Hermite asked in 1848 for a representation of real numbers whose eventual periodicity characterizes cubic irrationals. The totally real case was solved by Karpenkov's $\sin^2$-algorithm; the complex case, signature (1,1), is his Problem 4. We study a deterministic algorithm implementing his suggested analytic extension: the score expression is strictly negative on (1,1) data (closed form proved), the most negative score is selected, and exact score ties are resolved by a declared ordering. On a sample of 205 complex cubic polynomials, every run closes projectively with an exact unit certificate, each transition certified by exact comparisons in $\mathbb{Q}(α)$. An exhaustive campaign over the full box $[-3,3]^3$ closes 194/194. Across 457 deformed bases, the terminal cycle is an invariant of the marked lattice. Certified finite transition graphs are computed for four fields; the plastic case is machine-checked in Lean 4, kernel-only. All data ship in a public archive with a portable verifier.

</details>

Abstract: In the recent work by T. H. Chan, it was shown that there are no three consecutive powerful numbers with the middle term a perfect cube and each of the other two having only a single prime factor raised to an odd power. In this paper, with results given by Nagell-Ljunggren, Lebesgue, Ko and some elementary methods, we extend the result to similar cases, whose middle term can be perfect powers for infinitely many powers. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mordell方程y^2=x^3+k的整数解上界问题自Stark以来50多年未有突破性的幂次节省改进，同时整数平方与立方数的分离问题也长期停留在Stark 1973年的结果。本文旨在通过改进三次Thue-Mahler方程的有效界，在多个相关数论问题中取得幂次节省的进展。

Method: 通过深化三次Thue-Mahler方程的有效求解理论，建立对regulator具有幂次节省依赖的新有效上界，并将该界应用于Mordell方程、整数平方与立方分离问题以及Frey高度猜想。

Result: 将Mordell方程整数解界的指数从1+ε降至1/2+ε；将整数平方与立方的分离下界从(log T)^{1-o(1)}提升到(log T)^{2-o(1)}；在Frey高度猜想的conductor方向获得幂次节省改进。

Conclusion: 本文通过新的三次Thue-Mahler方程有效界，在多个经典数论问题上取得了50年来首次的幂次节省突破，特别是在无限制条件下显著提升了Mordell方程和分离问题的已知结果。

Abstract: We prove a new effective bound for cubic Thue--Mahler equations with power-saving dependence on the regulator. This has some applications. First, we improve Stark's bound $\log \max\{|x|,|y|\} \ll_ε|k|^{1+ε}$ for the integer solutions of Mordell's equation $y^2=x^3+k$ ($k$ a non-zero integer) by reducing the exponent $1+ε$ to $1/2+ε$; this is the first power-saving improvement without restrictions on $k$ in more than 50 years. Secondly, for integer squares and cubes of size $O(T)$ we improve the known unconditional separation lower bound $(\log T)^{1-o(1)}$ obtained by Stark in 1973 to $(\log T)^{2-o(1)}$. Finally, we obtain a power-saving improvement in the conductor aspect of the strongest currently available bounds for Frey's height conjecture (a strengthening of Szpiro's conjecture) in the case of elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}$ with integral $j$-invariant.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是解决平面三角剖分在红蓝顶点着色下的差异（discrepancy）界问题，具体而言是验证Asayama和Matsumoto提出的猜想，并进一步探索更紧的界。通过穷举验证小规模三角剖分，可以确认理论结果并揭示尚未解决的剩余情形。

Method: 主要证明方法是将两组现有结果（Loyola等人2026和Kawarabayashi等人2026）相结合，推导出一般情形下差异的上界。对于更紧的界，利用模6的性质进行分类讨论（排除n≡5 mod 6的情形）。最后，通过对4≤n≤12范围内全部9,150个非同构球面三角剖分进行穷尽式普查（exhaustive census）来实证验证理论界的正确性。

Result: 证明了任意n顶点平面三角剖分存在差异至多⌊n/3⌋的多色红蓝顶点着色，解决了Asayama和Matsumoto的猜想。对于n≥6且n≢5 (mod 6)的情况，获得了更紧的界n-2⌈(n+2)/3⌉。穷举验证覆盖了4≤n≤12的所有三角剖分，确认了所有界在n=11时也成立——这是第一个需验证更紧估计的剩余情形。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了平面三角剖分多色红蓝顶点着色的差异界猜想，同时在绝大多数情况下给出了更紧的界。唯一尚未从理论上解决的剩余情形是n≡5 (mod 6)的顶点数，但在n=11（该剩余类的最小非平凡情形）通过穷举验证已确认更紧界成立，为后续研究提供了证据支持。

Abstract: Every $n$-vertex plane triangulation admits a polychromatic red--blue vertex coloring with discrepancy at most $n-2\ceil{n/3}\le\floor{n/3}$, resolving a conjecture of Asayama and Matsumoto. The proof follows by combining Loyola et al. 2026 and Kawarabayashi et al. 2026. For $n\ge6$ and $n\not\equiv5\pmod6$, we prove the sharper bound $n-2\ceil{(n+2)/3}$. An exhaustive census of all $9{,}150$ non-isomorphic sphere triangulations with $4\le n\le12$ verifies the bounds and also confirms the sharper estimate at $n=11$, the first order in the remaining open residue class.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $G=(V,E)$ be a finite loopless directed graph. Each directed two-step diamond identifies its two pairs of opposite edges, and the connected components of the resulting auxiliary graph on $E$ define a canonical partition $Π_{\mathrm{opp}}(G)$. We ask whether a finite relational certificate built from the blocks of an edge partition identifies this canonical partition.
  We prove the functoriality and a universal coarsening property of $Π_{\mathrm{opp}}(G)$, count its complete fixed-profile fibre and its exact radius-one transposition neighbourhood, and reduce the parity condition in the certificate to an odd closed walk in a directed $\mathbb Z_2$-gain graph. In a fixed catalogue, four labelled target-positive rows, representing three isomorphism types, each admits a noncanonical positive partition obtained by a single cross-block transposition. In one row a stronger positive comparison partition also preserves every per-vertex, fixed-role incoming and outgoing count; it arises from a 12-edge alternating trade. The corresponding incidence fibre is a singleton in the other three rows.
  A deterministic 64-index family of comparison partitions yields 78 bounded positives among 256 nonidentity partitions. On the same profile fibre, one member has a complete 885-element relation semigroup and no write-preserve-use certificate, giving an exact negative. Thus the certificate has genuine but intermediate selectivity: it is neither determined by block sizes nor specific to the canonical target. The claims are finite statements in computational combinatorics and are supported by explicit, independently checkable certificates.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格定理建立了无限图和有向图中末端结构与网格状子图存在性之间的基本联系。具体而言，需要确定在任意有向图中，一个末端达到多大的入度才能保证其中包含宽度为n的有向六边形网格的细分。Hamann和Heuer此前提出了精确值k(n)的开放问题，本文旨在解决该问题。

Result: 精确确定了k(n)的值：对n≥4，k(n)=⌊3n/2⌋-1；且k(1)=1, k(2)=2, k(3)=4。同时确定了对偶极值参数W(d)：对d≥4，W(d)=⌊2d/3⌋+1；且W(1)=1, W(2)=W(3)=2。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了Hamann和Heuer提出的开问题，给出了末端入度与有向六边形网格细分存在的精确阈值，并补充了对偶极值参数的完整刻画，深化了对无限有向图末端结构的理解。

Abstract: Grid theorems provide a fundamental link between the structure of ends and the existence of grid-like subgraphs in infinite graphs and digraphs. For every fixed width $n$, let $k(n)$ denote the least positive integer such that every digraph with an end of in-degree at least $k(n)$ contains a subdivision of the directed hexagonal grid of width $n$. Hamann and Heuer asked the exact vaule of $k(n)$. We solve this problem by proving that $$ k(n)=\left\lfloor\frac{3n}{2}\right\rfloor-1 \qquad\text{for every }n\geq4, $$ while $k(1)=1$, $k(2)=2$, and $k(3)=4$. For the upper bound, we establish a finite vacancy theorem for labelled token slides, convert it into a closed directed schedule on an auxiliary ray digraph, and lift the schedule through fresh clean linkages. For the matching lower bound, we use alternating orientations of products of a ray with a three-armed tree and prove a no-passing property for disjoint directed paths.
  We also determine the dual extremal parameter. Let $W(d)$ denote the largest width that is always forced by an end of in-degree $d$, with all branch rays remaining in that end, then $$W(d)=\left\lfloor\frac{2d}{3}\right\rfloor+1 \qquad\text{for every }d\geq4,$$ with $W(1)=1$ and $W(2)=W(3)=2$.

</details>


### [75] [Nonnesting permutations avoiding 123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21680)
*Eric Cowan*

TL;DR: 本文解决了Elizalde和Luo提出的开放问题：对多重集{1,1,2,2,...,n,n}上避免模式123的非嵌套排列进行计数。作者通过双核方法构造生成树，证明了生成函数C(z)的代数表达式，并给出渐近公式c_n ~ (9+6√3)/(8√π)·6^n·n^(-3/2)，且C为4次代数函数。

Motivation: Elizalde和Luo在此前工作中枚举了避免每类两个及以上长度为3模式组合的非嵌套排列，但对避开单个模式123的情况未解决，并将其列为他们论文中的Problem 1。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Result: 证明了C(z) = 1 + z(1+U)/(2 - z(1+U)(1+V))，其中V满足V=1+zV²，U满足U=1+3z+zU²。由此推出C是次数恰为4的代数函数，且渐近公式为c_n ~ (9+6√3)/(8√π)·6^n·n^(-3/2)。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了Elizalde和Luo开放问题，揭示了避免123的非嵌套排列计数具有代数生成函数（次数为4），并收敛阶行为为典型的有根物体计数形式（常数因子·6^n·n^(-3/2)）。

Abstract: A nonnesting permutation of the multiset {1,1,2,2,...,n,n} is a word containing no subsequence abba with a distinct from b. Elizalde and Luo enumerated nonnesting permutations avoiding each set of two or more patterns of length three, and left open the enumeration of those avoiding the single pattern 123 (Problem 1 of their paper). We solve this problem. Writing $c_n$ for the number of nonnesting permutations of {1,1,...,n,n} avoiding 123 and $C(z)=\sum_{n\ge 0}c_nz^n$, we prove $C(z) = 1 + z(1+U)/(2 - z(1+U)(1+V))$, where $V = 1+zV^2$ and $U = 1+3z+zU^2$ are the formal power series branches with constant term 1. In particular $C$ is algebraic of degree exactly 4 over $\mathbb{Q}(z)$, and $c_n \sim \frac{9+6\sqrt{3}}{8\sqrtπ}\, 6^n n^{-3/2}$. The proof constructs a generating tree for these objects whose labels consist of two nonnegative integers, and applies the kernel method twice.

</details>

Method: 作者采用显式代数方法构造无s-团且无t-独立集的图。该构造在非对角情形对参数s的依赖精确体现为log s / log(log s + 1)，在s固定时给出t的指数下界；在对角情形通过改进构造细节，将Frankl–Wilson多胞形/线性代数方法中的常数从1/4提升到1。

Conclusion: 作者通过一个简洁的显式代数构造同时改进了非对角和对角Ramsey下界，在非对角情形优化了对s的依赖，在对角情形将经典常数翻了四倍，且构造的简洁性几乎与Frankl–Wilson原始方法相当。

Abstract: We provide an explicit algebraic construction showing that, uniformly for integers $3 \leq s \leq t$, as $t \to \infty$, \[
  R( s,t ) \geq t^{(1-o(1)) \log s / \log(\log s + 1) }. \] For large fixed $s$, this improves the dependence on $s$ in the general off-diagonal construction of Alon and Pudlák. In particular, $R(33, t) \geq t^{2.1-o(1)}$, to our knowledge, the first explicit construction showing $R(s, t) \geq t^c$ for some fixed $s$ and some $c > 2$. In the diagonal case, it improves the leading constant in the exponent of the classical Frankl--Wilson bound from $1/4$ to $1$, while being almost as simple to describe.

</details>


### [78] [B-coloring of grid graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21774)

TL;DR: 本文完全确定了圆柱网格图和环面网格图的B-着色色数q_B(G)。对环面网格C_m□C_n，证明了当m,n均为偶数且G非同构于C_4□C_{4k+2}时q_B(G)=4=Δ(G)；否则q_B(G)=5=Δ(G)+1。对圆柱网格C_s□P_m，q_B(C_{2n}□P_m)=4，而q_B(C_{2n+1}□P_m)在2≤m≤n时为4，在m≥n+1时为5。此外还讨论了高维离散环面和ℓ-圆柱网格的B-着色，给出了精确结果与界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-着色是一种要求每个4-圈为彩虹色的正常边着色，Gyárfás和Sárközy已确定了矩形网格P_m□P_n的B-着色色数。本文旨在进一步推进该研究，完全确定圆柱网格和环面网格这两类重要网格图（包括圆柱、环面及高维推广）的B-着色色数，填补已有工作的空白。

Method: 本文采用构造性的着色方法，针对不同奇偶性情形分别构造达到色数下界（Δ(G)或Δ(G)+1）的B-着色方案。通过对环面网格C_m□C_n中原胞（2×2网格）对应4-圈的着色约束进行细致分析，区分m,n奇偶性的所有情况；对圆柱网格则依据周长s的奇偶性及柱体长度m与n的大小关系分类讨论，并在高维情形中推广相关构造以给出精确值和上下界。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 环面网格C_m□C_n（m,n≥3）：当m,n均为偶数且G≇C_4□C_{4k+2}时q_B(G)=4=Δ(G)；当m,n中至少一个为奇数或G≅C_4□C_{4k+2}时q_B(G)=5=Δ(G)+1。(2) 圆柱网格C_{2n}□P_m（m,n≥2）：q_B=4；C_{2n+1}□P_m（m≥2,n≥1）：当2≤m≤n时q_B=4，当m≥n+1时q_B=5。(3) 对高维离散环面和ℓ-圆柱网格获得了部分精确结果和上下界。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了二维圆柱和环面网格图的B-着色色数问题，揭示了色数由图的基本参数（周长奇偶性、柱体长度及两者大小关系）完全决定，且色数仅取Δ(G)或Δ(G)+1两个值。高维情形的部分结果为进一步研究提供了方向。

Abstract: A B-coloring of a graph $G$ is a proper edge-coloring in which every $4$-cycle is rainbow. Let $q_B(G)$ be the minimum number of colors in such a coloring. Gyárfás and Sárközy (2023) determine $q_B(G)$ when $G=P_m\square P_n$ is a rectangular grid. In this paper, we completely determine $q_B(G)$ for cylindrical and torus grid graphs $G$. For a torus grid $G=C_m\square C_n$, where $m,n\ge3$ are integers, we prove that $q_B(G)=4=Δ(G)$ if both $m$ and $n$ are even and $G\not\cong C_4\square C_{4k+2}$ for any integer $k\ge1$, and that $q_B(G)=5=Δ(G)+1$ if at least one of $m,n$ is odd or $G\cong C_4\square C_{4k+2}$ for some integer $k\ge1$. For a cylindrical grid $G=C_s\square P_m$, $q_B(G)$ also depends on the parity of $s$ and the length of $P_m$. For integers $m\ge2$ and $n\ge2$, we have $q_B(C_{2n}\square P_m)=4$. For integers $m\ge2$ and $n\ge1$, we have $q_B(C_{2n+1}\square P_m)=4$ if $2\le m\le n$, whereas $q_B(C_{2n+1}\square P_m)=5$ if $m\ge n+1$. In higher dimensions, we discuss the B-coloring of discrete torus and $\ell$-cylindrical grid, obtaining some exact results and certain bounds.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t元平衡设计是组合设计理论中的核心对象，而图形化设计因其在对称群作用下的不变性具有特殊的结构美感和理论价值。Chouinard猜想长期以来是一个悬而未决的问题，证明该猜想需要对图形化设计的结构进行深入分析。

Method: 作者通过分析完全图边集上在顶点对称群诱导作用下不变的块多重集的结构，利用对称性约束和代数组合方法，将所有设计参数（点数、块大小、指标等）用λ的显式多项式加以界定，从而证明有限性。

Result: 成功证明了Chouinard猜想对t-设计（即所有块等大小的t元平衡设计）成立：固定指标λ时，仅存在有限多个非平凡图形化t元平衡设计（t>1），且无需简单性假设，所有参数均被λ的显式多项式所界定。

Conclusion: 该结果推广并加强了Chouinard猜想在t-设计情形下的结论，为完全证明一般情形提供了重要的方法论基础和技术路径。

Abstract: A $t$-wise balanced design on the edge set of a complete graph is graphical if its block multiset is invariant under the induced action of the symmetric group on the vertices. Chouinard conjectured that, for each fixed index $λ$, there are only finitely many nontrivial simple graphical $t$-wise balanced designs with $t>1$. We prove the conjecture for $t$-designs, which are the $t$-wise balanced designs whose blocks all have the same size. Our theorem does not require simplicity, and we bound all parameters of the designs by explicit polynomials in $λ$.

</details>

*Chaochao Zhu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究树的广义谱刻画问题，证明若两棵树具有相同的邻接谱和总游走序列且匹配数至多为4，则它们必同构；并证明了该界是紧的——对任意正整数m，都能构造匹配数为5的非同构树对，它们具有相同的邻接谱和完全相同的总游走序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的谱刻画是谱图理论中的核心问题。此前已知并非所有树都能由其谱唯一确定，本文旨在精确刻画在树的集合中，哪些树可以通过其广义谱（邻接谱加上总游走序列）被唯一重建，并确定匹配数这一参数下的精确阈值。

Method: 采用邻接谱结合总游走序列W_k(G)=1^T A(G)^k 1的等价表述。证明分为两部分：正方向基于有限核约简（finite-core reduction）和代数重建可能的悬挂附着结构，证明匹配数不超过4时可由谱确定；反方向通过构造一族匹配数为5的非同构树对，它们具有相同的邻接谱和相同的总游走序列，从而证明匹配数4的界是紧的。

Result: 证明了若两棵树匹配数均不超过4且具有相同邻接谱和相同总游走序列，则它们同构；且此范围内只需检查3≤k≤8的总游走序列相等即可。同时还证明了匹配数5时结论不成立，即界是紧的。

Conclusion: 树在树集合中的广义谱确定性问题在匹配数4处存在精确的相变：匹配数不超过4的树可由广义谱（邻接谱加总游走序列）唯一确定，而匹配数为5时存在反例。这给出了该重建问题的精确匹配数阈值。

Abstract: The spectral characterization of graphs is a central problem in spectral graph theory. In this paper we study when a tree is determined, among trees, by its generalized spectrum. We use the equivalent formulation given by the adjacency spectrum together with the total-walk sequence $W_k(G)=\mathbf 1^{\mathsf T}A(G)^k\mathbf 1$. We determine the exact matching-number threshold for this tree-level reconstruction problem. If $T$ and $T'$ are trees with matching number at most 4 and have the same adjacency spectrum and the same total-walk sequence, then $T\cong T'$. Moreover, in this range it is enough to require equality of $W_k$ for $3\le k\le8$. The bound is sharp: for every positive integer $m$ we construct a pair of non-isomorphic trees with matching number 5 having the same adjacency spectrum and identical total-walk sequences. The proof of the positive result is based on a finite-core reduction and an algebraic reconstruction of the possible pendant attachments.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文给出了一般连通图加权Kirchhoff指标的组合解释，将其表示为适当加权细分图及其子图中匹配权重之和，从而肯定地回答了Li、Li和Yan提出的关于加权Kirchhoff指标组合解释的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权Kirchhoff指标是图论中的一个重要不变量，其组合解释具有重要意义。此前Li, Li和Yan在2022年的论文中提出了一个问题：能否用加权细分图中的匹配及其子图来组合解释一般连通图的加权Kirchhoff指标。本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 作者给出了一般连通图G的加权Kirchhoff指标的组合解释，将K(G;x1,x2,...,xn)表示为适当加权的细分图G中匹配权重的和，以及从该加权细分图中删除对应于G的2-正则子图的细分子图后所得子图中匹配权重的和。

Result: 本文推导出的通用公式作为特例，可以恢复已知的树和单圈图的加权Kirchhoff指标的公式，以及任意连通图的普通Kirchhoff指标的已知公式。

Conclusion: 本文成功给出了任意连通图加权Kirchhoff指标的组合解释，肯定地回答了Li, Li和Yan提出的公开问题，并且统一了已有关于树和单圈图的加权Kirchhoff指标公式以及普通Kirchhoff指标公式。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected graph with vertex set $V(G)=\{v_1,v_2,\ldots,v_n\}$, and let $ω:V(G)\to \mathbb R^+$ be a positive vertex-weight function satisfying $ω(v_i)=x_i$ for each $v_i \in V(G)$. The weighted Kirchhoff index of $G$ is defined by $K(G;x_1,x_2,\ldots,x_n)=\sum_{1\le i<j\le n}x_i x_j r_G(v_i,v_j)$, where $r_G(v_i,v_j)$ denotes the resistance distance between $v_i$ and $v_j$. In this paper, we give a combinatorial interpretation of the weighted Kirchhoff index of an arbitrary connected graph. More precisely, we express $K(G;x_1,x_2,\ldots,x_n)$ in terms of the sums of weights of matchings in an appropriately weighted subdivision graph of $G$, and in the subgraphs obtained from this weighted subdivision graph by deleting the subdivision graphs corresponding to \(2\)-regular subgraphs of $G$. This gives an affirmative answer to a question posed by Li, Li and Yan [Discrete Math. 345 (2022) 113109] concerning a combinatorial explanation of the weighted Kirchhoff index of a general graph by using matchings in weighted subdivision graphs and their subgraphs. As special cases, our formula recovers the known formulas for the weighted Kirchhoff index of trees and unicyclic graphs, as well as the known formula for the ordinary Kirchhoff index of an arbitrary connected graph.

</details>

Conclusion: 每个无穷小有限可强制的有界对称实核都是有限可强制的，这建立了两个可强制概念之间的等价性，并展示了谱方法与压缩技巧在核极限理论中的有效结合。

Abstract: We characterize infinitesimal finite forcibility for bounded symmetric real kernels. We prove that the graph-density gradients at a kernel span a finite-dimensional space if and only if the kernel is a step kernel. Combined with known finite-forcing results for step kernels, this gives a positive answer to a question of Lovász and Szegedy on whether every infinitesimally finitely forcible kernel is finitely forcible. The proof combines spectral methods with a compression argument based on book graphs.

</details>


### [83] [Generalized Steinhaus triangles generated by canonical basis vectors: periodicity and weight formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22010)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Steinhaus三角形在组合数学和数论中有重要应用，但广义Steinhaus s-三角形的周期性和计数结构尚未被系统研究。理解由规范化基向量生成的三角形，能够揭示其周期、权重分布和渐进行为，为广义Steinhaus三角形提供统一的描述框架。

Method: 通过双s项式系数（bi^s-nomial coefficients）表达三角形条目，分析截断系数轮廓的周期性并确定精确周期。利用该周期性推导幂次二的导数恒等式和有限块分解，进而构造1的个数的精确递推关系，推导有理生成函数并分析渐进增长率。

Result: 证明了截断系数轮廓的纯周期性并给出精确周期；得到幂次二导数恒等式和相同有限块的分解；建立了1的个数在每条剩余类上的仿射函数表达式和精确递推；推导出有理生成函数、渐进增长率，并证明了规范化基向量生成三角形的1的密度为零；给出了前两个规范化向量及经典情形块权重的显式公式。

Conclusion: 规范化基向量生成的广义Steinhaus三角形具有完全确定的周期和计数结构，其1的个数呈仿射增长且密度趋于零，为广义Steinhaus三角形的周期与枚举性质提供了统一且完整的描述。

Abstract: A generalized Steinhaus $s$-triangle is obtained from a binary sequence by repeatedly replacing each block of $s$ consecutive entries by its sum modulo $2$. We study the triangles generated by canonical basis vectors. Expressing their entries through bi$^{s}$nomial coefficients, we prove that the truncated coefficient profiles of the successive rows are purely periodic and determine their exact period. This periodicity yields a power-of-two derivative identity and a decomposition into identical finite blocks. We consequently obtain an exact recurrence for the number of ones and show that, on every residue class, this weight is an affine function of the length. We also derive a rational generating function, determine the asymptotic growth rate, and prove that these canonical triangles have zero density of ones. Explicit formulas are obtained for the first two canonical vectors and for the block weight in the classical case. These results provide a unified description of the periodic and enumerative structure of generalized Steinhaus triangles generated by canonical basis vectors.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者利用固定密度随机图G(n,p)的结构特性，通过分析k-令牌图中公共邻居计数来恢复Johnson图J(n,k)中的邻接关系。具体地，利用k-令牌图顶点（未标记的k-子集）之间的公共邻居数量差异来区分Johnson邻接与非Johnson邻接，从而重建原图结构。证明采用概率方法，对每个固定p∈(0,1)建立高概率重构结论。

Result: 对于每个固定p∈(0,1)且G_n~G(n,p)，图G_n对任意1≤k≤n-1可由F_k(G_n)重构的概率至少为1-(1+o(1))n(1-p)^{n-1}。因此渐近几乎所有的标记n顶点图G对所有非平凡秩2≤k≤n-2可由F_k(G)重构。此外，同构类层面的同密度结论在Remark中概述。

Conclusion: 固定密度随机图几乎必然具有从k-令牌图重构原图的能力，该结论对标记图的所有非平凡秩成立，并在同构类层面亦有相应推广。这为k-令牌图与图重构问题建立了重要联系。

Abstract: Let $F_k(G)$ denote the $k$-token graph of a finite graph $G$. The graph $F_k(G)$ is a spanning subgraph of $J(n,k)$, but its vertices are not given their $k$-subset labels. We show that, for fixed-density random graphs, the Johnson adjacency relation can be determined from common-neighbor counts. For every fixed $p\in(0,1)$ and $G_n\sim G(n,p)$, the probability that $G_n$ is reconstructible from $F_k(G_n)$ for every $1\le k\le n-1$ is at least $1-(1+o(1))n(1-p)^{n-1}$. In particular, asymptotically almost every labeled $n$-vertex graph $G$ is reconstructible from $F_k(G)$ for every nontrivial rank $2\le k\le n-2$. The same density-one conclusion for isomorphism classes is outlined in Remark.

</details>

*Anders Claesson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在虚拟species环中证明了区间序species ℐ 等于一个由E的乘法逆E⁻¹构成的交错无穷积展开式，即ℐ = Σ_{m≥0} (-1)^m ∏_{i=1}^m ((E⁻¹)^i - 1)，该等式右侧即Claesson和Hannah引入的带符号选票矩阵虚拟species。作者分别用代数方法和基于自然符号反转对合的构造性双射方法证明了这一species恒等式，并进一步推导了区间序自同构群的Young子群结构、刚性区间序species的分解ℐ = ℛ∘E₊，以及Glaisher T数与24着色区间序的计数联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Claesson和Hannah已通过带符号基数证明了带符号选票矩阵虚拟species的基数计数标号区间序，但仅是基数层面的结果。作者旨在将其强化为完整的species恒等式，从而在组合结构层面（而非仅计数层面）建立区间序与带符号选票矩阵之间的等价关系，以获得更丰富的结构信息和进一步的组合推论。

Method: 作者给出两种证明：(1) 代数证明，在虚拟species环中直接操作E、E⁻¹及相关运算推导恒等式；(2) 双射证明，构造自然符号反转对合来建立区间序与带符号选票矩阵之间的符号保持双射。此外利用cycle index级数将结果推广到标号和未标号生成级数，分析区间序自同构群作为Young子群的结构，并证明ℐ = ℛ∘E₊的分解。

Result: 建立了虚拟species恒等式ℐ = Σ_{m≥0} (-1)^m ∏_{i=1}^m ((E⁻¹)^i - 1)，将Claesson-Hannah的基数结果强化为物种级恒等式；其cycle index级数统一给出标号和未标号区间序的生成函数；区间序自同构群被刻画为Young子群；得到分解ℐ = ℛ∘E₊；并证明了Glaisher T_n恰好计数[n]上无24号颜色的孤立元素的24着色区间序数。

Conclusion: 作者成功地以两种独立方式（代数和双射）建立了区间序species与带符号选票矩阵虚拟species之间的精确恒等式，为区间序的组合结构提供了新的species理论视角，并由此推导出若干有趣的结构性结论（自同构群分解、刚性区间序分解以及与Glaisher T数的联系）。

Abstract: We show that, in the ring of virtual species, \[
  \mathcal{I}=\sum_{m\geq 0}(-1)^m\prod_{i=1}^{m}\bigl((E^{-1})^i-1\bigr), \] where $\mathcal{I}$ is the species of interval orders and $E^{-1}$ is the multiplicative inverse of the species $E$ of sets. The right-hand side is the virtual species of signed ballot matrices introduced by Claesson and Hannah. They showed that its signed cardinality counts labeled interval orders. We strengthen this to a species identity, which we prove twice: first algebraically and then bijectively, using a natural sign-reversing involution. The cycle index series of $\mathcal{I}$ specializes to the generating series for labeled and unlabeled interval orders. We describe the automorphism group of an interval order as a Young subgroup and prove the identity $\mathcal{I}=\mathcal{R}\circ E_+$, where $\mathcal{R}$ is the species of rigid interval orders. We also show that Glaisher's T-number $T_n$ counts the $24$-colored interval orders on $[n]$ in which no isolated element has color $24$.

</details>


### [87] [Rank-Average Degree Bound for Graph Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22139)
*Seyed Ahmad Mojallal*

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意n≥5的简单图G的能量满足E(G) ≥ r(G) + d̄(G) − 1，其中r(G)为邻接矩阵的秩，d̄(G)为平均度，并完整刻画了所有极值图。该结果作为推论同时解决了五个此前关于非奇异图能量下界的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的能量（邻接矩阵特征值绝对值之和）是图论中的一个重要不变量，长期以来研究者关注其下界的估计。此前有多个关于非奇异图能量的下界猜想尚未被证明，本文旨在通过一个统一的不等式框架来证明或解决这些猜想。

Method: 本文通过建立能量与邻接矩阵秩、平均度之间的不等式关系，证明了一般简单图满足E(G) ≥ r(G) + d̄(G) − 1，并运用极值图论方法刻画了所有达到等号的极值图。

Result: 证明了上述统一下界不等式并完整刻画了极值图；作为推论，解决了五个先前提出的关于非奇异图能量的下界猜想，包括包含n−1+d̄(G)、Δ(G)+δ(G)、2√(d̄(G)(n−1))、M₁(G)/m以及M₁(G)/(2m)+2m/n等形式的能量下界。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了一般简单图能量关于秩和平均度的统一下界，不仅解决了多个猜想，还通过极值图的完整刻画加深了对图能量极值行为的理解。

Abstract: We prove that the energy ${\mathcal E}(G)$ of any simple graph $G$ of order $n\ge5$ satisfies \[
  {\mathcal E}\ge r(G)+\bar d(G)-1, \] where $r(G)$ and $\bar d(G)$ denote, respectively, the rank of the adjacency matrix and the average degree of $G$. We also characterize all extremal graphs. As consequences, our result settles five previously conjectured lower bounds for the energy of nonsingular graphs in their stated ranges, namely \[
  \begin{gathered}
  {\mathcal E}(G)\ge n-1+\bar d(G),\qquad
  {\mathcal E}(G)\geΔ(G)+δ(G),\qquad
  {\mathcal E}(G)\ge2\sqrt{\bar d(G) (n-1)},\qquad
  {\mathcal E}(G)\ge\frac{M_1(G)}{m},\qquad
  {\mathcal E}(G)\ge\frac{M_1(G)}{2m}+\frac{2m}{n},
  \end{gathered} \] where $m$ is the number of edges, $Δ(G)$ and $δ(G)$ are the maximum and minimum degrees, and the first Zagreb index $ M_1(G)$ is the sum of degree squares.

</details>


### [88] [Log-concavity of subsequence counts of words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22147)
*Vincent Vatter*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限字母表上单词的不同子序列计数序列的对数凹性。Chase在1976年证明了该序列是对数凹的，但其证明依赖于三角阵列和对若干比值的细致分析。本文提出了一种更简洁的证明方法，按首字母进行分解，将证明简化为一个加权平均问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chase于1976年证明了单词的不同子序列计数序列具有对数凹性，但其原始证明方法繁琐，需要对三角阵列进行细致分析并计算多个和式的比值。作者希望找到一种更简洁、更直观的证明方法。

Method: 作者放弃基于单词前缀的三角阵列方法，转而按照单词的首字母进行分解，将对数凹性的证明归约为一个加权平均问题，从而简化了证明过程。

Result: 通过按首字母分解的方法，作者成功地将对数凹性证明简化为加权平均的形式，为Chase的定理提供了一种更简洁的替代证明。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种比Chase原始证明更简洁的方法来证明单词子序列计数序列的对数凹性，通过首字母分解将复杂证明归约为加权平均，体现了方法上的简洁性优势。

Abstract: Given a word over a finite alphabet, consider the sequence that counts, for each length, the number of distinct subsequences of that length. In 1976, Chase proved that this sequence is log-concave. His proof uses a triangular array indexed by the prefixes of the word together with a meticulous analysis of ratios of several sums. We instead decompose according to the first letter, which reduces the proof to a weighted average.

</details>


### [89] [Schur positivity from signed elementary expansions: clique-spiders and spiders $S(a,b,2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22184)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 色对称函数（chromatic symmetric function）的Schur正性和e-正性研究是代数组合学的核心问题，但判断给定图的色对称函数是否Schur正性通常困难。作者希望通过建立新的判定准则来系统化这一验证过程，并解决特定图族（尤其是蜘蛛图）上的正性问题。

Result: 得出S^{000}_{rst}恰在r≥st-1时Schur正；S^{100}_{rst}和S^{010}_{rst}对所有图均Schur正；S^{001}_{rst}在s=t时Schur正，在s>t时其Schur正性成员落入四个显式参数区域。证明了所有蜘蛛S(a,b,2)是Schur正的，并证明S(a,b,2)（a≥b≥2，3∤b）的e-正性当且仅当(a,b)∈{(6,4),(12,4),(9,7)}，推进了Tom猜想。

Conclusion: 本文通过提出新的Schur正性判定准则，为色对称函数的正性研究提供了系统工具，并在多类蜘蛛图上取得了完整分类，显著推进了Tom猜想的研究进展。

Abstract: We prove a Schur alpha-omega lemma for chromatic symmetric functions. It bounds the partitions indexing nonzero Schur coefficients in terms of higher independence numbers, higher clique numbers, and the chromatic number. We then establish three equivalent dominance-matching criteria: matrix, Hall, and order-ideal, that certify Schur positivity from a fixed signed $e_I$-expansion.
  As applications, we obtain complete classifications of $e$-positivity and Schur positivity for four basic families of $3$-clique-spiders. Here $S^{ghk}_{rst}$ is formed by joining a common center to one vertex of each of $K_r$, $K_s$, and $K_t$ by internally disjoint paths of lengths $g$, $h$, and $k$, respectively. As a result, $S^{000}_{rst}$ is Schur positive exactly when $r\ge st-1$, and every graph $S^{100}_{rst}$ and $S^{010}_{rst}$ is Schur positive. When $s=t$, the graph $S^{001}_{rst}$ is Schur positive; when $s>t$, its Schur-positive members fall into four explicit parameter regimes. We also introduce a path-clique bootstrap and use it to prove that every spider $S(a,b,2)$ is Schur positive. Finally, we prove that the spider $S(a,b,2)$ for $a\ge b\ge2$ with $3\nmid b$ is $e$-positive if and only if $(a,b)\in\{(6,4),(12,4),(9,7)\}$, which advances the study of Tom's conjecture concerning the $e$-positivity of spiders $S(a,b,2)$.

</details>


### [90] [Cocycles of determinantal hypertrees with small support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22255)
*András Mészáros*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机二维行列式超树T_n上coefficient在F_p中的1-cocycle的小支撑结构。结果表明：当p>2时，不存在任何连通图G能以正概率作为cocycle的支撑出现；当p=2时，连通图G具有此性质当且仅当它恰有一个奇长度的唯一圈，且若该圈为三角形，则三角形各顶点度数至少为3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机行列式超树（determinantal hypertree）的代数拓扑结构中，理解小支撑的cocycle的典型形态是理解这类随机复形同调/上同调结构的重要一步。作者希望严格刻画哪些连通图能以正概率作为Z^1(T_n, F_p)中某个cocycle的支撑出现，从而揭示不同素数p下该随机复形上同调空间的局部结构差异。

Method: 作者对素数p分情况讨论。对于p=2的情况，利用F_2上cocycle与图结构的关系，刻画支撑的连通分支必须满足"恰有一个奇数长度的唯一圈"这一特征，并额外处理唯一圈为三角形时的度数条件。对于p>2，通过构造或反证说明不存在任何连通图能作为小支撑cocycle以正概率出现，从而得到否定的完整分类。

Result: 给出了完整的分类结果：p>2时，不存在任何连通图G满足性质；p=2时，连通图G满足性质当且仅当G恰有一个唯一圈且该圈长度为奇数，且若该圈是三角形，则三角形上所有顶点度数至少为3。

Conclusion: 该结果精确刻画了随机二维行列式超树在F_p系数下的1-cocycle小支撑的典型拓扑结构，揭示了p=2与p>2之间本质的行为差异，为理解随机行列式超树的上同调结构提供了新的洞见。

Abstract: Let ${T}_n$ be a random $2$-dimensional determinantal hypertree on $n$ vertices. Given any prime $p$, we answer the following question: If a cocycle in $Z^1({T}_n,\mathbb{F}_p)$ has small support, what does the support typically look like? More precisely, we characterize all the finite connected graphs $G$ for which there is a constant $c_G>0$ with the following property: For all large enough $n$, with probability at least $c_G$, we have a cocycle $f\in Z^1({T}_n,\mathbb{F}_p)$ such that after removing all the isolated vertices, the support of $f$ is isomorphic to $G$. We prove that for $p>2$, we do not have any such graph. For $p=2$, a connected graph has the property above if and only if it has a unique cycle such that this unique cycle has odd length, moreover, if the unique cycle is a triangle, then we also need to require that all the vertices of the triangle have degree at least $3$.

</details>


### [91] [Pfaffian--Toeplitz identities, Schur positivity, and the $q$-log-convexity of Baxter polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22258)
*Yanxin Liu,Jianxi Mao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Pfaffian小求和公式与Jacobi-Trudi Toeplitz矩阵，推导出斜Schur函数中的Pfaffian展开，进而为多个涉及斜Schur函数乘积的生成函数提供了显式Schur展开，并证明了Baxter多项式的$q$-log-凸性以及$q$-refined Baxter数的$q$-log-凹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究组合恒等式的Schur正性，特别是与Narayana多项式的$q$-log-凸性相关的恒等式，以及Baxter多项式和$d$-Hoggatt数相关的log-凸/凹性质，需要新的代数工具来给出统一且严格的证明。

Method: 结合Pfaffian小求和公式与Jacobi-Trudi Toeplitz矩阵来推导斜Schur函数中的Pfaffian展开；利用稀疏斜对称矩阵给出Schur正性恒等式的Pfaffian证明；将$q$-refined Baxter数实现为矩形Schur函数的主特殊化以研究其$q$-log-凹性。

Result: 证明了Baxter多项式构成$q$-log-凸序列；证明了Baxter变换保持log-凸性；证明了$q$-refined Baxter数阵列在每一行和每一列上都是$q$-log-凹的；这些结果自然推广到$d$-Hoggatt数的$q$-模拟。

Conclusion: Pfaffian方法与Schur函数理论的结合为解决组合序列的log-凸/凹性质提供了有力的统一框架，不仅验证了已知恒等式的Schur正性，还建立了Baxter数与Hoggatt数序列的新的$q$-log-凸/凹性质。

Abstract: We use the Pfaffian minor summation formula together with Jacobi--Trudi Toeplitz matrices to derive Pfaffian expansions in skew Schur functions. This yields explicit Schur expansions for several generating functions involving products of skew Schur functions. In particular, using sparse skew-symmetric matrices, we provide a Pfaffian proof of a Schur-positive identity arising in the study of the $q$-log-convexity of the Narayana polynomials. As the main application, we prove that the Baxter polynomials form a $q$-log-convex sequence. We further show that the Baxter transformation defined by the refined Baxter numbers preserves log-convexity. Finally, by realizing the $q$-refined Baxter numbers as principal specializations of rectangular Schur functions, we prove that the array of $q$-refined Baxter numbers is $q$-log-concave both along each row and along each column. Both $q$-log-concavity results extend naturally to the $q$-analogues of the $d$-Hoggatt numbers.

</details>


### [92] [The Partition Pairing Theorems I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22291)
*George Andrews,Manosij Ghosh Dastidar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文为整数分拆引入了配对理论（pairing theory），定义配对指标（pairing index）与配对秩（pairing rank）两个统计量，并证明其与分拆的若干经典统计量（如部分数、最大部分）的联合等分布性质。文章进一步引入图式配对（diagrammatic pairing），通过折叠Durfee方块两翼形成对角块，给出具有非负连续秩的分拆集合的布尔分解，并联系到overpartitions与Frobenius表示，最后研究负配对秩的简单配对分拆，得到涉及奇除数、overpartitions的恒等式、配对秩-2的奇偶定理及一个Toeplitz行列式的系数极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为整数分拆建立一套新的配对理论框架，通过引入新的统计量（配对指标与配对秩）揭示分拆结构的深层组合性质，并建立其与经典统计量、Durfee方块几何结构、overpartitions及数论对象（如Kummer进位定理、MacMahon乘积）之间的内在联系。

Method: 定义配对指标与配对秩两个统计量，证明其等分布性及联合精化性质；引入配对宽度并证明联合等分布。通过有限高斯生成函数的环分圆因式分解导出Kummer定理。随后引入图式配对：折叠Durfee方块两翼，将未配对单元分解为对角块，通过独立反射给出布尔分解。利用符号特化建立配对秩奇偶性与自共轭分拆的关系，并联系overpartitions与Frobenius表示。最后研究负配对秩情形，借助Toeplitz行列式与分析技巧得出极限结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套系统的整数分拆配对理论，将组合统计量、生成函数、几何（Durfee方块折叠）、数论（Kummer定理、同余式）与分析工具（Toeplitz行列式）融为一体，不仅统一了若干已知结果，还揭示了分拆、overpartitions与Frobenius表示之间的深层联系，为后续研究提供了新的框架与开放问题。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to introduce pairing theory for partitions. We begin with two statistics on integer partitions, the \emph{pairing index} and the \emph{pairing rank}. The pairing index is equidistributed with the number of parts, while a joint refinement identifies its two constituents with the numbers of even and odd parts. We further introduce the \emph{pairing width} and prove that pairing index and pairing width are jointly equidistributed with the number of parts and the largest part. The resulting finite Gaussian generating function has a cyclotomic factorization from which Kummer's famous carry theorem for binomial coefficients follows. We also prove a mod-$5$ congruence for the excess of unpaired parts congruent to $1$ modulo $4$ over those congruent to $3$ modulo $4$ in the partitions of $5n+4$. A signed specialization exhibits that the parity of the pairing rank is governed by self-conjugate partitions. Motivated by this, we go on to introduce a second, diagrammatic pairing: after the two wings of the Durfee square are folded together, the unpaired cells break into connected \emph{diagonal blocks}. These blocks may be reflected independently, giving a Boolean decomposition of the set of partitions with a unique representative having all successive ranks nonnegative. We then relate our theory to overpartitions and Frobenius representations, obtaining as a corollary a geometric realization of overpartitions in terms of partitions whose principal hooks are all even. Finally, we study simply paired partitions of negative pairing rank, obtaining identities involving odd divisors and overpartitions, a parity theorem for pairing rank $-2$, and a Toeplitz determinant whose coefficientwise limit is an explicit infinite product related to MacMahon's product for plane partitions.

</details>

Result: (1) 在两个赋值问题设定下，H_J和H̄_J均为对应基础图的生成树。(2) 对平面点配置的欧氏p次幂距离权重，p=1时H_J非交叉，p=2时H̄_J非交叉。(3) 提出猜想：p=2时树H̄_J在对应设定下渐近分布为具有自由/有线边界条件的均匀生成树，相应树路径及平面对偶上的路径渐近为SLE_κ（κ=2）。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了最小匹配问题并集图在特定设定下具有生成树和非交叉结构，并建立了这些组合结构到统计力学中均匀生成树和SLE理论的桥梁，为随机欧氏赋值问题的深入研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: The \emph{Minimum Matching Problem} consists of finding an independent edge set of minimum weight $M_{\star}(G)$ in a given edge-weighted graph $G$. When $G$ is bipartite, this reduces to the \emph{Assignment Problem}. We consider a variant of this problem defined by taking the union of optimal matchings across various slightly modified versions of the base graph: $H_{\mathcal{J}}(G)=\bigcup_{U \in \mathcal{J}} M_{\star}(G_{U})$. We establish two families of results: (1) In two distinct settings for the Assignment Problem, we prove that the resulting graphs $H_{\mathcal{J}}$, as well as certain associated graphs $\bar{H}_{\mathcal{J}}$, are spanning trees on the relevant base graphs $G$ and $\bar{G}$. (2) In these same settings, assuming the edge weights are given by the $p$-th power of Euclidean distances for point configurations in the plane, we show that for $p=1$ the tree $H_{\mathcal{J}}$ is non-crossing (i.e., its planar embedding has no crossing edges), whereas, remarkably, for $p=2$ the associated tree $\bar{H}_{\mathcal{J}}$ is non-crossing. Finally, we introduce novel conjectures in Statistical Mechanics, to be explored in future work: in the Random Euclidean Assignment Problem (where points are i.i.d.\ on a planar domain), we conjecture that for $p=2$ the trees $\bar{H}_{\mathcal{J}}$ are asymptotically distributed as Uniform Spanning Trees with free and wired boundary conditions in the two respective settings. In particular, suitable paths on the tree in the second setting, and on its planar dual in the first setting, are asymptotically distributed as $\text{SLE}_κ$ with $κ=2$.

</details>

Abstract: For $2 \leq a \leq d+1$, what is the largest integer $H_{a,d} (n)$ such that every set of $n$ points in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with no $a$ points on a common $(a-2)$-flat contains a subset of $H_{a,d} (n)$ points whose determined $(a-1)$-dimensional simplices have pairwise distinct $(a-1)$-dimensional volumes? We construct $n$-point sets that improve the best known upper bounds for $H_{a,d}(n)$ in several cases of $a$ and $d$.
  We also study a dual version of the problem. Let $D_d(n)$ the maximum number such that for any arrangement of $n$ hyperplanes in general position in $\mathbb{R}^d$, we can always find a subset of $D_d(n)$ hyperplanes for which all the $d$-dimensional simplices that they define have distinct $d$-dimensional volumes. We improve the current known upper bound for $D_d(n)$ and give the first nontrivial lower bound for $D_2(n)$ and $D_3 (n)$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文证明了长S环（长度至少为ℓ且包含指定集合S中顶点的环）满足诱导版本的Erdős-Pósa型对偶性：对于任意整数k≥1和ℓ≥3，每个图要么包含k个两两顶点不相交且诱导的S环，要么存在一个大小为f(k,ℓ)（多项式函数）的顶点集，其闭邻域与所有长度至少为ℓ的S环相交。证明基于一种新的"脆弱耳"（fragile ears）分解技术，并对每个固定ℓ给出多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Erdős-Pósa定理是图论中的基本对偶性结果，已被推广到长环、S环等多种情形。然而，当打包（packing）本身要求是诱导的（即不同环之间不仅顶点不相交且无连边）时，已知结果非常少。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立长S环在诱导框架下的Erdős-Pósa型对偶性。

Method: 引入了一种新的基于"脆弱耳"（fragile ears）的耳分解技术。该技术用于构造诱导打包或找到有界大小的顶点集（其闭邻域击中所有目标环）。证明通过归纳构造，并利用函数f(k,ℓ)给出多项式上界，同时对每个固定ℓ提供多项式时间算法。

Result: 证明了存在多项式函数f(k,ℓ)，使得对任意k≥1和ℓ≥3，每个图要么包含k个长度为至少ℓ的诱导S环打包，要么存在至多f(k,ℓ)个顶点的集合，其闭邻域与所有长度至少为ℓ的S环相交。对每个固定ℓ，该构造可在多项式时间内完成。

Conclusion: 长S环在诱导打包的意义下具有Erdős-Pósa型对偶性，推广了经典Erdős-Pósa定理到诱导且带长度约束的S环情形。文中提出的脆弱耳分解技术是证明的核心新工具，且算法上对固定ℓ是多项式时间可计算的。

Abstract: The Erdős-Pósa theorem states that for every integer $k\geq1$, every graph contains either $k$ vertex-disjoint cycles or a set of $\mathcal{O}(k\log k)$ vertices meeting all cycles. This fundamental min-max duality has been extended to numerous settings, including long cycles, $S$-cycles, that is, cycles containing a vertex in a prescribed set $S$, and cycles satisfying various additional constraints. In contrast, much less is known when the packing itself is required to be induced, namely, when distinct cycles are vertex-disjoint and have no edges between them.
  We prove that long $S$-cycles admit an induced version of the Erdős-Pósa-type duality. More precisely, we show that there exists a polynomial function $f(k,\ell)$ such that for all integers $k\geq1$ and $\ell\geq3$, every graph contains either an induced packing of $k$ $S$-cycles of length at least $\ell$ or a set of at most $f(k,\ell)$ vertices whose closed neighbourhood intersects all $S$-cycles of length at least $\ell$. The proof introduces a new ear-decomposition technique based on fragile ears and yields a polynomial-time algorithm for every fixed $\ell$.

</details>


### [96] [Ramsey-type results for threshold graphs and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22350)
*Xihe Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阈值图是一类结构简单但重要的图类，可由单顶点图通过反复添加支配顶点或孤立顶点构造而成。由该图类的诱导Ramsey型问题驱动，研究任意n顶点图中必然包含的诱导阈值图的最大规模，并希望通过边染色视角（有序染色）建立与规范Ramsey理论的联系。

Method: 定义有序染色，将阈值图诱导子图问题转化为2-边染色完全图K_n中必含有序K_s的问题。建立指数级上下界，对小规模s利用穷举或构造确定精确值。利用强正则图、Hadamard矩阵和会议矩阵构造下界，结合渐近分析给出完全二部图的精确渐近公式。

Result: 建立了r'_2(s)的指数级上下界，确定了s∈{3,4,5,6}的精确值；确定了无序规范Ramsey数CR(s,3)对所有s≥3的精确值；证明了r'_2(K_{s,t})=CR(K_{s,t},K_3)=(2^s/(s+1)+o(1))t；对s∈{2,3}给出了无穷多个t的精确值。

Conclusion: 将阈值图诱导Ramsey数通过有序染色与经典Ramsey和规范Ramsey理论建立了统一联系，给出了完全二部图情形下的精确渐近结果，并展示了组合构造（强正则图、Hadamard矩阵、会议矩阵）在确定精确值中的关键作用。

Abstract: A {\it threshold graph} is a graph that can be constructed from the one-vertex graph by repeatedly adding either a dominating vertex or an isolated vertex. Motivated by an induced Ramsey-type problem for this class, we define $r'_2(s)$ to be the minimum integer $n$ such that every $n$-vertex graph contains an induced threshold graph on $s$ vertices. We establish exponential upper and lower bounds for $r'_2(s)$ and determine its exact values for $s\in\{3,4,5,6\}$. To study this problem from an edge-coloring perspective, we use the notion of an orderable coloring, introduced by Richer [{\it J. Combin. Theory Ser. B}, 80(1) (2000), 172--177]. An edge-colored graph is {\it orderable} if its vertices can be ordered so that, for each vertex, all edges from it to later vertices have the same color. Equivalently, $r'_2(s)$ is the minimum $n$ such that every $2$-edge-coloring of $K_n$ contains an orderable $K_s$. We also determine the exact value of the unordered canonical Ramsey number $CR(s, 3)$ for all $s \ge 3$, where $CR(s,3)$ denotes the minimum integer $n$ such that every edge-coloring of $K_n$ contains either an orderable $K_s$ or a rainbow $K_3$. More generally, for graphs $G$ and $H$, we study $r'_2(G)$, the corresponding $2$-color Ramsey number for an orderable $G$, and $CR(G,H)$, where the alternative is a rainbow $H$. For complete bipartite graphs, we prove that for every fixed $s$, $r'_2(K_{s,t}) = CR(K_{s,t}, K_3)= \left(\frac{2^s}{s+1}+o(1)\right)t$ as $t\to\infty$. For $s\in \{2,3\}$, we further determine the exact values of these parameters for infinitely many $t$, using constructions arising from strongly regular graphs, Hadamard matrices and conference matrices.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
