Abstract: We establish a commutator estimate for the Yukawa modulated energy associated with overdamped particle systems with repulsive interactions in every fixed dimension $d\ge2$, with constants uniform for $0<κ\leκ_*$. The main difficulty is specific to the modified Helmholtz operator: truncating the Yukawa potential produces both a surface charge measure and a positive volume charge density, while the resulting smeared charge has total charge $1-κ^2r^2/(2d)+o(κ^2r^2)$ as $κr\downarrow0$. We restore neutrality by rescaling the reference density and use a stress-energy identity whose interface term involves the arithmetic mean of the one-sided gradient traces. This yields an additive finite-$N$ correction of order $N^{-2/d}$ for $d\ge3$ and $(1+\log N)/N$ for $d=2$, without an $N$-independent lower bound on interparticle distances. As consequences, we obtain quantitative weak--strong stability for the mean-field limit, control of the $k$-particle marginals for fixed $k$, and a time-integrated mean-square force discrepancy. For smooth compactly supported initial data, regular mean-field solutions exist on a time interval uniform in $0\leκ\leκ_*$. The same uniformity yields the Coulomb limit: the squared Wasserstein distance between the Yukawa and Coulomb mean-field solutions is $O(κ^4)$ in $d\ge3$ and $O(κ^2)$ in $d=2$; in $d=3$ we also obtain a direct simultaneous mean-field/Coulomb estimate at the particle level.

</details>


### [140] [Nonuniqueness of solutions to the Lagrangian mean curvature equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22114)
*Arunima Bhattacharya,W. Jacob Ogden*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文解决了Harvey和Lawson提出的一个问题：在单位球上具有连续边界数据的Lagrangian平均曲率方程Dirichlet问题中，连续粘性解的唯一性不成立。对于任意维数n≥2，作者构造了一个连续的相位函数ψ和连续边界数据g，使得该Dirichlet问题存在连续统（无穷多个）不同的连续粘性解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harvey和Lawson曾提出关于Lagrangian平均曲率方程Dirichlet问题连续粘性解唯一性的疑问。该方程在几何测度论和变分法中具有重要意义，弄清其解的唯一性是理解方程整体性质的关键问题。

Method: 作者通过构造具体的反例来证明唯一性的不成立：对于任意维数n≥2，构造一个连续相位函数ψ定义在闭单位球上并取值于(-nπ/2, nπ/2)，同时构造连续边界数据g，使得相应的Dirichlet问题存在一族（连续统）不同的连续粘性解。

Result: 成功构造了反例，证明了当相位函数和边界数据都仅假设连续时，Lagrangian平均曲率方程的Dirichlet问题不具有唯一性——存在无穷多个不同的连续粘性解，从而否定了该情形下解的唯一性。

Conclusion: 在连续的相位函数和连续边界数据的假设下，Lagrangian平均曲率方程Dirichlet问题的连续粘性解的唯一性一般不成立，回答了Harvey和Lawson提出的问题。

Abstract: We resolve a question posed by Harvey and Lawson concerning the uniqueness of continuous viscosity solutions to the Dirichlet problem for the Lagrangian mean curvature equation on the unit ball with continuous boundary data.
  For every dimension $n\geq 2$, we construct a continuous phase $ψ\colon \overline{B_1}\to(-n\fracπ{2},n\fracπ{2})$ and continuous boundary data $g \colon \partial B_1\to \mathbb{R}$ for which the Dirichlet problem \[
  \sum_{i=1}^n \arctanλ_i(D^2u)=ψ(x)\quad\text{in }B_1,
  \qquad u=g\quad\text{on }\partial B_1, \] admits a continuum of distinct continuous viscosity solutions.

</details>


### [141] [Interior $C^{1,α}$ estimates for the linearized Monge--Ampère equation in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22180)
*Ling Wang,Bin Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明二维情形下齐次线性化Monge-Ampère方程解的C^1,α内部估计，仅需Monge-Ampère密度满足上下界假设，无需连续性假设，是经典Morrey-Nirenberg C^1,α估计的仿射不变类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维中建立线性化Monge-Ampère方程解的内部C^1,α正则性估计，且不要求Monge-Ampère密度的连续性，推广经典椭圆正则性理论到仿射不变框架下。

Method: 核心方法是部分Legendre变换。变换后，解的一阶导数变为一致椭圆非散度型方程的伴随解之比；利用Bauman的Harnack不等式控制商函数的Hölder正则性，通过部分Legendre变换的Jacobi恒等式和Caccioppoli估计获得局部有界性。

Result: 在det D^2φ有正上下界的假设下，建立了二维齐次线性化Monge-Ampère方程解的C^1,α内部估计；并作为应用，证明了至多线性增长的整解满足Liouville定理。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了二维线性化Monge-Ampère方程在无连续性假设下的最优正则性，凸显了部分Legendre变换与经典Morrey-Nirenberg估计之间的深刻联系，并为整体解的分类提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove an interior $C^{1,α}$ estimate for solutions of the homogeneous linearized Monge--Ampère equation in dimension two under the assumption \[ 0<λ\leq \det D^2\varphi\leqΛ<+\infty. \] No continuity assumption on the Monge--Ampère density is required. Our result is an affine-invariant analogue of the classical Morrey--Nirenberg $C^{1,α}$ estimate in two dimensions. The core of the proof is the partial Legendre transform. After the transform, the first derivatives of the solution are quotients of adjoint solutions for a uniformly elliptic non-divergence form equation. Bauman's Harnack inequality gives the Hölder control of the quotient, while the Jacobian identity of the partial Legendre transform and a Caccioppoli estimate give its local boundedness. As an application, we prove a Liouville theorem for entire solutions with at most linear growth.

</details>


### [142] [Asymptotics of the principal eigenvalue of an elliptic operator on closed and orientable Riemannian manifolds: small diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22181)
*Xin Xu,Kexin Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究闭可定向黎曼流形上椭圆特征值问题在扩散系数趋于零时的主特征值渐近行为，发现其极限值完全由Morse函数f的临界点及黎曼Hessian特征值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究小扩散极限下主特征值的渐近行为，揭示其与流形上Morse函数临界点结构的深层联系，为理解退化扩散过程中的谱性质提供理论依据。

Method: 针对闭可定向黎曼流形上的椭圆特征值问题，在f为Morse函数的假设下，通过分析临界点处的c值及黎曼Hessian特征值，刻画主特征值在小扩散极限下的极限值。

Result: 建立了lim_{D→0}λ(D)的完整刻画，表明该极限完全由f的临界点以及这些点上c和黎曼Hessian特征值所决定。

Conclusion: 主特征值的小扩散极限与流形上Morse函数的拓扑结构（临界点）及黎曼几何（Hessian）密切相关，为黎曼流形上退化椭圆算子的谱分析提供了新的洞见。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the asymptotic behavior of the principal eigenvalue $λ(D)$ of the elliptic eigenvalue problem \[ -DΔ_{M}u - a\langle \nabla_M f, \nabla_M u\rangle_g + c u = λ(D)u, \] posed on a closed orientable Riemannian manifold $(M,g)$, in the small-diffusion limit $D \to 0^+$. Under the assumption that $f$ is a Morse function on $M$, we establish that the limiting value $\lim_{D\to 0}λ(D)$ is completely characterized by the critical points of $f$ and the associated Riemannian Hessian, specifically through the values of $c$ and the Riemannian Hessian eigenvalues at those points.

</details>


### [143] [Sharp Barron Regularity Results for Coulombic Many-Electron Wave Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22252)
*Pingbing Ming,Hao Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了库仑多电子波函数在提取普适截断Jastrow因子后的尖锐Barron正则性结果。对于库仑本征函数ψ，通过逐次商构造φ和φ₃，证明它们属于Barron空间Bˢ(R³ᴺ)对所有s<2成立，且该范围在普适分解中是最优的。文章还给出了精确的端点增长估计：当s=2-ε时范数以ε⁻²速率发散，并通过对未微扰双电子原子的精确分析证明了该二次速率在|φ₃(0,0)|≠0时是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barron空间（即谱范数有限的空间）在机器学习与高维逼近理论中具有核心地位，而量子多体波函数的Barron正则性直接决定了神经网络波函数（如费米子神经网络、PauliNet等）逼近库仑波函数的近似率。此前对波函数Barron正则性的研究未达到最优，本文旨在确立库仑多电子波函数在普适因子分解下的最佳Barron正则性并给出精确的端点行为。

Method: 本文采用Fournais等人在2005年提出的分解框架：首先提取两电子截断Jastrow因子e^{F_{2,cut}}，再进一步提取三电子截断Jastrow因子e^{F_{3,cut}}，定义逐次商φ和φ₃。通过分析这些商的正则性，利用解析/微局部方法结合Barron空间的工具，证明其对任意s<2属于Bˢ，并通过构造反例和精确的渐近分析（对双电子原子）证明端点s=2时的发散率为ε⁻²且不可改进。

Result: 主要结果是：(1) φ,φ₃∈Bˢ(R³ᴺ)对所有s<2成立，且s<2的最优性通过构造不依赖本征函数的普适因子无法将商提升到B²来证明；(2) 端点增长率精确估出：‖u‖_{B^{2-ε}} ≤ (M/ε²)‖u‖_{B¹}；(3) 对未微扰双电子原子，\|φ₃\|_{B^{2-ε}} = 32πZ|φ₃(0,0)|/ε² + O(1/ε)，其中基态满足|φ₃(0,0)|≠0，从而证明二次发散率是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 本文确立了库仑波函数在普适Jastrow因子分解下的最优Barron正则性为任意s<2（而非s=2），并精确刻画了端点s→2时的发散行为（ε⁻²上界为最优）。这一结果对神经网络量子波函数的逼近理论具有重要指导意义：它表明从Barron正则性角度，普适因子的提取已无法进一步改进s<2的上界，而四电子以上交互（或更高阶核）效应的精确处理可能需要额外的非普适结构。

Abstract: We establish sharp Barron regularity for Coulombic many-electron wave functions after extraction of the universal cut-off Jastrow factors. Following the factorization of Fournais et al.~\cite[Definition~1.4]{FournaisEtAl2005}, for a Coulombic eigenfunction $ψ$ we define the successive quotients by \[ φ=e^{-F_{2,\mathrm{cut}}}ψ\quad\text{and}\quad φ_3=e^{-F_{3,\mathrm{cut}}}φ=e^{-(F_{2,\mathrm{cut}}+F_{3,\mathrm{cut}})}ψ. \] Then \[ φ,φ_3\in\mathcal{B}^s(\mathbb{R}^{3N}) \qquad\text{for every }s<2. \] This range is optimal among universal factorizations. No factor depending only on the particle number and the nuclear data, but not on the eigenfunction or its eigenvalue, can make every corresponding quotient belong to $\mathcal{B}^2$.
  We also determine the exact endpoint growth. Writing $\varepsilon=2-s$, we prove that, for either $u=φ$ or $u=φ_3$, there is a computable constant $M$ independent of $\varepsilon$ such that \[ \left\|u\right\|_{\mathcal{B}^{2-\varepsilon}}\leq\frac{M}{\varepsilon^2}\left\|u\right\|_{\mathcal{B}^1}. \] For the unperturbed two-electron atom we prove, with a constant independent of $\varepsilon$, \[ \left|\left\|φ_3\right\|_{\mathcal{B}^{2-\varepsilon}}-\frac{32πZ\lvertφ_3(0,0)\rvert}{\varepsilon^2}\right|\leq\frac{C}{\varepsilon}. \] Hence the quadratic rate in the upper bound is sharp whenever $\lvertφ_3(0,0)\rvert\neq0$, as is the case for the ground state.

</details>


### [144] [Boundary regularity for Oseen-Frank minimizers with arbitrary positive splay, twist, and bend constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22287)
*Zhiyuan Dai,Haotong Fu,Huaijie Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维修正Oseen-Frank能量在强锚定边界条件下的任意全局极小元在边界附近全邻域内是光滑的，适用于任意正展曲、扭曲和弯曲常数，无需小各向异性假设，解决了Lin和Liu问题(c)的边界正则性部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oseen-Frank模型是液晶理论的基本变分模型，但其全局极小元的边界正则性长期以来是开放问题。Lin和Liu提出的问题(c)关注强锚定条件下能量极小元的正则性，此前仅在某些各向同性或特殊常数假设下取得进展，缺乏对任意正弹性常数的一般性结果。

Method: 通过解析变分方法与PDE正则性理论相结合的技术路线，针对Oseen-Frank能量密度（包含展曲、扭曲、弯曲三项）的全局极小元，在任意正弹性常数假设下建立边界附近的完全正则性证明，同时涵盖无磁场扰动和有光滑磁场扰动两种情形。

Result: 成功证明纯Oseen-Frank问题及带光滑磁场扰动情形下，全局极小元在边界∂Ω的完整邻域内是光滑的，突破了此前需要小各向异性假设的限制。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了Lin和Liu问题(c)中关于纯Oseen-Frank问题的边界正则性部分，为液晶变分模型的严格数学理论提供了关键进展。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^3$ be a smooth bounded domain and let $g:\partialΩ\to\mathbb S^2$ be smooth. We prove that any global minimizer of the Oseen--Frank energy subject to the strong anchoring condition $n=g$ is smooth in a full neighborhood of $\partialΩ$. The result holds for arbitrary positive splay, twist, and bend constants, without any small-anisotropy assumption. It resolves the boundary-regularity part of Lin and Liu's Problem~(c) for the pure Oseen--Frank problem and for its prescribed smooth magnetic-field perturbation.

</details>


### [145] [The parabolic Dini-$β$ condition and absolute continuity of surface and caloric measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23240)
*Simon Bortz,Moritz Egert,Sandra Ferris,Olli Saari*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了抛物型Lipschitz图边界上，抛物面测度关于热测度绝对连续当且仅当满足（平方）Dini-β条件。该条件等价于边界可以被可数多个"正则"Lipschitz图覆盖，这为抛物型PDE背景下定性抛物可求长性提供了合适的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究抛物型偏微分方程背景下边界测度与正则性的关系，特别是抛物面测度与热测度的绝对连续性等价条件，以及与之对应的几何可求长性概念。作者希望建立抛物型情形下与椭圆情形类似的Jonesβ-数理论基础，并论证"正则Lipschitz图覆盖"是定性抛物可求长性的正确概念。

Method: 引入抛物版本的Jones (L^2) β-数（β̂），定义（平方）Dini-β条件为 ∫₀¹ β̂(X,t,r)² dr/r < ∞（σ-几乎处处成立）。通过建立该条件与"正则Lipschitz图覆盖"之间的等价关系来刻画边界的几何性质。正则Lipschitz图指在抛物BMO空间中具有半阶时间导数额外规则性的图。

Result: (1) 抛物面测度σ关于热测度绝对连续当且仅当（平方）Dini-β条件成立（σ-a.e.）；(2) 该Dini-β条件等价于边界能被可数多个正则Lipschitz图覆盖；(3) 若Dini-β条件在热测度零集之外成立，则热测度关于面测度绝对连续（反向绝对连续性）。

Conclusion: 研究支持"正则Lipschitz图覆盖"作为抛物型PDE背景下定性抛物可求长性的正确概念，并将Dini-β条件与抛物面测度和热测度之间的绝对连续性建立了精确的等价关系。

Abstract: We show if $\partial Ω$ is the graph of a parabolic Lipschitz function, then parabolic surface measure $σ$ of $\partial Ω$ is absolutely continuous with respect to its caloric measure if and only if a (square) Dini-$β$ condition is satisfied. More specifically, the (square) Dini-$β$ condition is that \[\int_0^1 \hatβ(X,t,r)^2 \frac{dr}{r} < \infty, \quad \text{$σ$-a.e. } (X,t) \in \partial Ω.\] Here $\hatβ$ is a parabolic version of the Jones ($L^2$) $β$-numbers. We show that these conditions are satisfied if and only if the graph is covered by a countable collection of {\it regular} Lipschitz graphs, that is, graphs with additional in-time regularity in the form of a half order time derivative in the parabolic BMO space. This supports the view that covering by {\it regular} Lipschitz graphs is the right notion for qualitative parabolic rectifiability in the context of parabolic PDEs.
  We also show that if \[\int_0^1 \hatβ(X,t,r)^2 \frac{dr}{r} < \infty\] up to a set of caloric measure zero then the caloric measure is absolutely continuous with respect to surface measure.

</details>


### [146] [A Free Boundary Problem for quasilinear Systems with mixed variable exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22305)
*Somayeh Khademloo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究单位球内由变指数(p,q)-拉普拉斯型混合算子控制的Alt-Phillips型自由边界半线性系统，在特定结构条件下建立了非负径向C^1解的存在性，其范数依赖于结构数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Alt-Phillips型自由边界问题在变指数(p,q)-拉普拉斯混合算子框架下的可解性，拓展经典自由边界理论到更一般的非线性算子情形。

Method: 在单位球中考虑由变指数(p,q)-拉普拉斯型混合算子控制的半线性系统，施加特定的结构条件，通过适当的分析工具建立非负径向C^1解的存在性。

Result: 在结构条件下，证明了非负径向C^1解的存在性，解的存在性与范数估计均依赖于系统所涉及的结构数据。

Conclusion: 研究结果为变指数(p,q)-拉普拉斯混合算子框架下的Alt-Phillips型自由边界问题提供了存在性理论，并给出了依赖于结构数据的解范数控制。

Abstract: In the unit ball, we study a semilinear system that gives rise to a free boundary of Alt-Phillips type, where the equation is governed by a mixed variable-exponent operator of $(p,q)$-Laplacian type.
  Under certain structural conditions, we establish the existence of nonnegative radial solutions that are $C^1$, with their norms depending on the structural data.

</details>


### [147] [Quasiconvexity of the Burkholder function on symmetric matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23388)
*André Guerra*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用Morse理论证明了Burkholder函数在对称矩阵上是拟凸的，这推出了Beurling–Ahlfors变换作用于实值函数时的若干Sharp不等式；更一般地，还证明了Székelyhidi引入的分隔函数在对称矩阵上也是拟凸的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burkholder函数和Székelyhidi分隔函数在调和分析（特别是奇异积分算子的Sharp估计）中扮演关键角色。证明这些函数在对称矩阵上的拟凸性是推导Beurling–Ahlfors变换Sharp不等式的重要途径，但此前缺乏基于Morse理论的系统性证明方法。

Method: 采用Morse理论作为核心分析工具，通过分析对称矩阵流形上函数的临界点结构、Morse指标等拓扑性质，来验证Burkholder函数以及更一般的Székelyhidi分隔函数满足拟凸性条件，进而获得所需的函数不等式。

Result: 成功证明了Burkholder函数在对称矩阵上是拟凸的，并将其推广到更一般的Székelyhidi分隔函数；由此推导出Beurling–Ahlfors变换在实值函数上的若干Sharp不等式。

Conclusion: 论文建立了Morse理论与调和分析中Sharp估计之间的新联系，为Burkholder函数和分隔函数的拟凸性提供了统一的证明框架，并深化了对Beurling–Ahlfors变换Sharp不等式理论的理解。

Abstract: We use Morse Theory to prove that the Burkholder function is quasiconvex on symmetric matrices, which implies several sharp inequalities for the Beurling--Ahlfors transform when acting on real-valued functions. More generally, we prove that the separating functions introduced by Székelyhidi are quasiconvex on symmetric matrices.

</details>


### [148] [A small rigid body immersed in a three-dimensional viscous fluid and the vanishing viscosity limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22380)
*Xiaoguang You*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维粘性不可压缩流体中浸泡的刚体系统，当刚体尺寸和流体粘度同时趋于零时的渐近行为，证明极限速度场由全空间不可压缩欧拉方程控制，且流体对刚体运动的影响在该极限下消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究流体-刚体耦合系统的极限行为，理解当物体尺寸和粘性同时消失时，系统在宏观尺度上如何收敛到理想流体（欧拉方程）的描述，这对于多相流、稀悬浮液等应用场景中简化模型具有重要意义。

Method: 通过考虑刚体尺寸和流体粘度同时趋于零的极限过程，利用偏微分方程分析（估计、紧性论证等）证明极限速度场收敛到全空间不可压缩欧拉方程的解，同时分析流体对刚体运动影响的消失机制。

Result: 证明了极限速度场满足全空间不可压缩欧拉方程（对应理想流体极限），且在该极限下流体对刚体运动的作用力（影响力）趋向于零。

Conclusion: 当刚体尺寸和流体粘度同时趋于零时，耦合系统收敛到无粘理想流体模型，刚体运动趋向自由运动（不受流体影响），这为小尺度颗粒在低粘流体中的动力学建模提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the asymptotic behavior of a coupled fluid--rigid body system, consisting of a rigid body immersed in a three-dimensional viscous incompressible fluid, as the body size and the fluid viscosity vanish. We prove that the limiting velocity field is governed by the whole-space incompressible Euler equations. Moreover, the influence of the fluid on the rigid body motion is shown to vanish in this limit.

</details>


### [149] [A simple derivation of Anderson-Hartree equations with harmonic potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22437)
*Samaël Mackowiak*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何将Knowles和Pickl推导Hartree方程的方法推广到一维和二维Anderson-Hermite算子以及三维形式Anderson-Hermite算子的情形。通过证明极限方程解的能量估计（在噪声和界集上一致），实现了均值场极限与噪声极限的双重极限交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Knowles和Pickl的Hartree方程推导方法从标准单粒子哈密顿量推广到带有随机噪声的Anderson-Hermite算子情形，从而研究在无序系统（如Anderson模型）中均值场极限与噪声极限是否可以交换的问题。

Method: 本文采用解析方法：首先将Knowles和Pickl推导Hartree方程的技术适配到Anderson-Hermite算子框架下；其次证明极限方程解的能量估计在噪声的有界集上一致成立，利用该一致性最终证明双重极限（均值场极限和噪声极限）可以交换。

Result: 在维度1和2中，对于Anderson-Hermite算子（维度3中为形式Anderson-Hermite算子），成功实现了均值场极限与噪声极限的交换，即双重极限可以按任意顺序取。

Conclusion: 本文证实了在具有Anderson型随机势的量子多体系统中，均值场极限与噪声极限可以交换。这为理解无序量子系统中的均值场近似有效性提供了理论依据，并拓展了Hartree方程推导方法的适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we show how to adapt a derivation of Hartree equations due to Knowles and Pickl in the case where the one-particle hamiltonian is an Anderson-Hermite operator in dimension 1 and 2 or a formal Anderson-Hermite operator in dimension 3. By proving energy estimates for solution of the limit equation, uniform in the noise in bounded sets, we achieve a double limit. Namely, we show that we can commute the mean-field limit with a limit in noise.

</details>


### [150] [No bubble trees for the $1$-equivariant harmonic map heat flow and the radial energy-critical nonlinear heat equation in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22447)
*Taegyu Kim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维调和映射热流（HMHF）到S²的1-等变解，以及三维到五维径向能量临界非线性热方程（NLH）的每一个有限能量解至多只有一个气泡：有限时间爆破恰好一个气泡，全局解要么无气泡要么在无穷时刻有一个气泡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡树（bubble tree）现象是几何流和色散方程中常见但复杂的奇异性模式，此前关于是否可能形成多气泡叠合的结构仍缺乏一般性结论。作者旨在证明在特定对称性设定下，气泡树的形成被排除，从而简化解的渐近结构分类。

Method: 作者从孤子分解（soliton resolution）出发，利用调制分析（modulation analysis）来排除气泡树。具体地，通过提取两个最内层尺度的相对动力学，推导出矛盾，从而证明不可能存在多个气泡的嵌套结构。此方法本质上是能量方法，不依赖于极大值原理，因此对气泡符号没有限制。

Result: 对1-等变HMHF的所有有限能量解和NLH的所有H¹有界解，证明了至多单气泡性质：有限时间爆破时仅有一个气泡形成，而全局解在无穷时间最多出现一个气泡。

Conclusion: 在所述对称性框架下，气泡树结构被完全排除，解的动力学行为比一般情形更简单。该能量方法的广泛适用性（不依赖极大值原理、不限气泡符号）为进一步处理更一般情形提供了技术基础。

Abstract: We consider the $1$-equivariant harmonic map heat flow (HMHF) from $\mathbb R^2$ to $\mathbb S^2$ and the radial energy-critical nonlinear heat equation (NLH) in dimensions $d=3,4,5$. We prove that every finite-energy solution of (HMHF) and every $\dot H^1$-bounded solution of (NLH) has at most one bubble: every finite-time blow-up has exactly one bubble, whereas every global solution has either no bubble or one bubble at infinite time. Starting from the soliton resolution, we exclude bubble trees by modulation analysis, deriving a contradiction from the relative dynamics of the two innermost scales. This energy method does not rely on maximum principle and, in particular, applies without restriction on the bubble signs.

</details>


### [151] [The Global Weak-Lorentz Vorticity Endpoint in the Stationary Navier--Stokes Liouville Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22471)
*Wangzhe Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明三维稳态Navier–Stokes流若涡度属于弱Lorentz空间L^{9/5,∞}(R³)，则流动必然为零，这是Chae–Wolf的L^{9/5}涡度准则及Kozono–Terasawa–Wakasugi逐点准则的端点推广，无需有限Dirichlet能量假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展稳态Navier–Stokes方程的刘维尔型定理：此前Chae–Wolf证明了L^{9/5}涡度条件下的平凡性准则，而Kozono–Terasawa–Wakasugi的临界逐点衰减准则需要相对小性条件。本文旨在将这些结果的边界情形统一，去除小性假设并推广至弱Lorentz空间。

Method: 证明分为四个步骤：(1) 利用端点Biot–Savart映射及Seregin–Wang临界环形Lorentz估计，从弱L^9/5涡度推断有限Dirichlet能量；(2) 通过对L^{9/5}累积质量的对数界选取blow-down尺度，得到稳态Euler极限；(3) 该Euler极限在外部区域满足Bernoulli伴随律，结合继承的弱端点估计迫使极限能量通量为零；(4) 运用调和截断恒等式将零能量通量传递回原始尺度，得到零Dirichlet能量，从而v≡0。

Result: 建立了端点刘维尔型定理：ω∈L^{9/5,∞}(R³)蕴含v≡0。该条件在远场重缩放下不变（对应临界涡度衰减|ω(x)|=O(|x|^{-5/3})），严格推广了Chae–Wolf的L^{9/5}准则，并去除了Kozono–Terasawa–Wakasugi临界逐点准则中的相对小性条件，且无需假设有限Dirichlet能量。

Conclusion: 该结果表明在临界弱Lorentz正则性条件下，三维稳态Navier–Stokes流在无穷远处消失时必然是零解，为FEM临界涡度情形的刘维尔问题提供了端点答案，也展示了blow-down分析与能量通量消失论证相结合的新技术路线。

Abstract: Let $(v,p)$ be a smooth stationary Navier--Stokes flow in $\mathbb R^3$ that vanishes at infinity, and set $ω:=\operatorname{curl}v$. We prove the endpoint implication \[
  ω\in L^{9/5,\infty}(\mathbb R^3)
  \quad\Longrightarrow\quad v\equiv0. \] This weak-Lorentz condition is invariant under the far-field rescaling associated with the borderline vorticity decay $|ω(x)|=O(|x|^{-5/3})$ and strictly extends the $L^{9/5}$ vorticity criterion of Chae--Wolf. It also removes the relative smallness condition from the critical pointwise criterion of Kozono--Terasawa--Wakasugi: the decay $|ω(x)|=O(|x|^{-5/3})$ alone implies $v\equiv0$. No finite Dirichlet energy is assumed. In our proof, we firstly use the endpoint Biot--Savart mapping and the critical annular Lorentz estimate of Seregin--Wang to yield finite Dirichlet energy. A logarithmic bound for the cumulative $L^{9/5}$ mass then selects blow-down scales whose stationary Euler limit satisfies Bernoulli companion laws on the exterior region. Together with the inherited weak endpoint bounds, these laws force the limiting energy flux to vanish. A harmonic-cutoff identity transfers this vanishing to the original scale and yields zero Dirichlet energy.

</details>


### [152] [Uniqueness for the Degenerate Monge-Ampère Equation on Arbitrary Bounded Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22519)
*Yang Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于n维空间中任意有界开凸集Ω，当p>n时，Dirichlet问题det D²u = (-u)^p在Ω内、u=0在边界上、u>0在Ω内的凸Alexandrov解至多唯一。证明基于Monge-Ampère能量的仿射行为和沿Legendre路径的L^{p+1}质量的幂凹性。当p=n时，同一论证表明任意两个非零同系数解互为正常数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Monge-Ampère型方程解的唯一性问题，特别是幂非线性Dirichlet问题在凸Alexandrov解类中的唯一性。这是一个关于退化/奇异椭圆型偏微分方程解的唯一性的基础性问题，对理解Monge-Ampère方程的结构具有重要意义。

Method: 证明方法结合了两个关键工具：(1) Monge-Ampère能量的仿射行为性质；(2) 沿连接两个解的Legendre路径上L^{p+1}质量的幂凹性（power-concavity）性质。通过这两者的结合来证明解的唯一性。

Result: 对于p>n的情形，在任意有界开凸集Ω上，该Dirichlet问题的凸Alexandrov解至多有一个。在临界指数p=n的情形，任意两个非零的同系数解必定互为正常数倍（即正比例关系）。

Conclusion: 该结果表明在超临界指数（p>n）下，凸Alexandrov解是唯一的；在临界指数（p=n）下，解的结构被简化为其在正倍数意义下唯一。这为Monge-Ampère方程的幂非线性Dirichlet问题的解空间结构提供了完整的刻画。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$ and let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^n$ be an arbitrary bounded open convex set. The author prove that, for $p>n$, the Dirichlet problem \[
  \det D^2u=(-u)^p\quad\text{in }Ω,
  \qquad u=0\quad\text{on }\partialΩ,
  \qquad u>0\quad\text{in }Ω\] has at most one convex Alexandrov solution. The proof is based on the affine behavior of the Monge--Ampère energy and on a power-concavity property of the $L^{p+1}$ mass along the Legendre path connecting two solutions. At the homogeneous exponent $p=n$, the same argument shows that any two nonzero solutions with the same coefficient are positive multiples of one another.

</details>


### [153] [Classification of local $(-1)$-homogeneous axisymmetric solutions of the $3$D stationary Navier-Stokes equations with logarithmic singular-ray behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22558)
*Xukai Yan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维不可压稳态Navier-Stokes方程在奇异射线附近局部(-1)-齐次轴对称解的（Type II行为）存在性与分类问题。作者构造了一个四参数族的局部(-1)-齐次轴对称解，其具有至多对数增长，并以递推系数收敛级数表示；同时证明了满足|u|=o(dist(x,P)^{-1})条件的局部(-1)-齐次轴对称解都属于该族。该族包含非零涡量的Type II解，这与R^3\{x'=0}中无涡量的全局(-1)-齐次轴对称Type II解形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Navier-Stokes方程在奇异射线附近的解的结构是理解方程整体正则性理论的关键。由于Landau解是已知的最奇异类型解，本文旨在探索除Landau解之外"次奇异"的(-1)-齐次轴对称解，以分类奇异射线附近可能的解形态，并揭示局部解与全局解在涡量上的本质差异。

Method: 作者采用构造性分析方法，通过将局部解表示为收敛级数并递推确定其系数，构造四参数族的局部(-1)-齐次轴对称解。同时利用匹配估计和ODE/偏微分方程方法证明逆命题——即所有满足给定渐近条件的局部解均落入该族，并建立导数估计及分布意义下奇异力的识别。

Result: 成功构造了四参数族的局部(-1)-齐次轴对称Type II解（具有对数增长），其中包含具有非零涡量的解；证明了满足|u|=o(dist(x,P)^{-1})的任意局部(-1)-齐次轴对称解属于该族；建立了解的导数估计，并在分布意义下识别了这些解穿越奇异射线时产生的奇异力。

Conclusion: 本文给出了奇异射线附近局部(-1)-齐次轴对称Type II解的完整分类（在适当渐近条件下），揭示了与全局解的重要差异：局部允许有涡量，而全局解无涡量。这为深入理解Navier-Stokes方程解的奇异性结构提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: We study the existence and classification of local $(-1)$-homogeneous axisymmetric solutions $(u, p)$ of the three-dimensional incompressible stationary Navier-Stokes equations near a singular ray. We focus on such solutions with Type II behavior, which satisfy $0<\limsup_{x\in\bS^2, x\to P}|u|/|\ln \text{dist}(x, P)|<\infty$, where $P$ is the south or north pole. Apart from the Landau solutions, these are the least singular nontrivial $(-1)$-homogeneous axisymmetric solutions. We construct a four-parameter family of local $(-1)$-homogeneous axisymmetric solutions with at most logarithmic growth near the singular ray and represent them as convergent series whose coefficients are determined recursively. Conversely, we prove that every local $(-1)$-homogeneous axisymmetric solution satisfying $|u|=o(\text{dist} (x, P)^{-1})$ as $x\to P$ on $\bS^2$ belongs to this family. In particular, this family contains Type II solutions with nonzero swirl, in contrast to the global $(-1)$-homogeneous axisymmetric Type II solutions in $\R^3\setminus\{x'=0\}$, which have no swirl. We also establish derivative estimates for the constructed solutions and identify the singular force generated by these solutions across the singular ray in the sense of distributions.

</details>


### [154] [Singularity formation and self-shrinkers for the parabolic Bernoulli problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22574)
*Hunter Liu,Sebastian Munoz,Zihui Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究抛物型Bernoulli自由边界问题在奇异点处的奇性形成，引入Type I爆率假设证明切向流为自收缩解，分类所有径向自收缩解（球、外部和唯一的环形剖面），证明球的动态稳定性与环的动态不稳定性，并借助计算机辅助推理验证边界谱符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Ricci流和平均曲率流中奇性分析的启发，自由边界问题中的奇性形成和自相似剖面缺乏系统性的理解。本文旨在建立类似于几何流领域中的切向流分类理论，以理解Bernoulli自由边界问题中奇异点的局部结构。

Method: 引入Type I爆率假设保证抛物rescaling的紧性和切向流的非平凡性；利用紧性论证恢复Bernoulli边界条件；在次临界区域证明切向流为双平面；通过分类所有径向自收缩解（包括新发现的环状剖面），获得内外半径的维数一致界和尖点渐近；通过线性谱符号分类建立稳定/不稳定判据；对极端维度使用区间算术的计算机辅助严格验证。

Result: （1）Type I假设下切向流收敛且满足Bernoulli条件；（2）次临界区域切向流为双平面；（3）径向自收缩解仅有球、外部和唯一环状三类，环状剖面内外半径具有精确渐近及向外偏置；（4）球动态稳定（模对称群），环动态不稳定，具有角阶0,1,2,3的真实不稳定模态；（5）谱非退化性保证切向流唯一性；（6）维度2≤n≤4000的边界面谱符号由区间算术严格验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了自由边界问题奇性分析的系统框架，完整分类了径向自收缩谱系，澄清了球与环状解的稳定性差异，并展示了计算机辅助证明在该领域的可行性与必要性。

Abstract: We study singularity formation for classical solutions of the parabolic Bernoulli free boundary problem and the associated self-similar profiles. Inspired by Ricci and mean curvature flows, we introduce a Type I assumption on the blow-up rate at a singular free boundary point, under which the parabolic rescalings are compact and every tangent flow is a non-trivial self-shrinking solution of the elliptic profile equation. The proof establishes convergence of the positivity sets and recovers the Bernoulli condition in the limit, despite the absence of a minimizing structure. In the subcritical regime, every tangent flow is a double plane. We next classify all radial self-shrinkers. Besides the known ball and exterior profiles, there is a unique annular profile. We obtain dimension-uniform bounds and sharp asymptotics for its inner and outer radii, including a quantitative outward bias of its midpoint. These delicate estimates yield a complete classification of the signs of the linear spectrum for the annular solution in every dimension. As a consequence, the ball is dynamically stable modulo ambient symmetries, whereas the annulus is dynamically unstable, with genuine unstable modes of angular degrees $0,1,2,3$. We finally prove, from the spectral nondegeneracy of the compact radial profiles, that whenever one tangent flow is a ball or an annular solution, the tangent flow is unique. One borderline spectral sign in dimensions $2\leq n \leq 4000$ is verified by a rigorous computer-assisted argument using interval arithmetic.

</details>


### [155] [Uniqueness for the spatially homogeneous Boltzmann equation in critical Sobolev spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22579)
*Tianhao Dong,Shuchen Guo,Jie Ji*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了无角截断的空间均匀Boltzmann方程在逆幂律关系γ+4s=1（极软势）下的临界Sobolev空间中的适定性与正则化估计，并结合Fisher信息单调性将解延拓到全局时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极软势（γ+4s=1）情形下，Boltzmann方程的非线性碰撞算子具有最弱的耗散性和最强的非局部性，经典的适定性理论难以直接适用。作者旨在填补该临界情形下解的适定性与全局存在性的理论空白。

Method: 采用相空间与频率空间的同时二adic局部化能量估计，配合碰撞算子与局部化算子之间的尖锐交换子估计；结合Fisher信息单调性建立解的全局延拓。

Result: 在临界Sobolev空间H^{-(γ+2s+3/2)}（含对数修正）中建立了解的存在性、唯一性、稳定性与正则化估计，并借助Fisher信息单调性将解全局延拓至所有时间。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在临界正则性框架下完整解决了极软势情形Boltzmann方程的适定性与全局存在性问题，标志着对无角截断Boltzmann方程在临界情形的理解达到了新的深度。

Abstract: We study the spatially homogeneous Boltzmann equation without angular cutoff for very soft potentials satisfying the inverse power law relation $γ+4s=1$. Our main results establish the existence, uniqueness, stability and regularization estimates of solutions in the critical Sobolev space $ H^{-(γ+ 2s + \frac{3}{2})} $ with a logarithmic correction. Combined with the recently established monotonicity of the Fisher information, the solutions extend globally in time. Our primary tools are energy estimates based on a simultaneous dyadic localization in the phase and frequency variables, together with sharp commutator estimates between the collision operator and the localization operators.

</details>


### [156] [The Monge--Ampère equation on graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22580)
*Ahmed Alkhozaae,Julio D. Rossi,Aelson Sobral,José Miguel Urbano*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了有限图上的Monge–Ampère方程，其算子通过邻居顶点函数值的局部序统计量定义离散Hessian特征值的乘积。文章推导了等价Bellman型公式，建立了严格凸类中的比较原理与唯一性，通过Perron方法研究存在性并识别图论障碍，同时研究了齐次方程并提出了数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作受非线性图插值和半监督学习的驱动，旨在将经典偏微分方程中的Monge–Ampère算子推广到图结构上，以处理非欧几里得数据上的非线性插值问题。

Method: 通过离散Hessian特征值（基于相邻顶点函数值的局部序统计量）的乘积定义图上的Monge–Ampère算子；利用Bellman型动态规划公式等价刻画非齐次Dirichlet问题；采用比较原理建立严格图凸类中的唯一性，并通过Perron方法分析存在性；针对齐次与非齐次问题分别设计数值格式。

Result: 建立了非齐次Dirichlet问题的Bellman等价形式，证明了严格图凸类中的比较原理和唯一性；通过Perron方法揭示了存在性中某些图论意义上的障碍；齐次方程归结为涉及最小离散特征值的非线性插值规则；提出了两类问题的数值方案。

Conclusion: 本文为图上的非线性偏微分方程（特别是Monge–Ampère方程）提供了系统的理论基础，包括适定性分析和数值方法，为图插值与半监督学习等应用提供了新的数学框架。

Abstract: We introduce a version of the Monge--Ampère equation on finite graphs, motivated by nonlinear graph-based interpolation and semi-supervised learning. The operator is defined as the product of discrete analogs of the Hessian eigenvalues, obtained via local order statistics of function values at neighboring vertices. We derive an equivalent Bellman-type formulation of the inhomogeneous Dirichlet problem, establish a comparison principle and uniqueness in the strictly graph-convex class, and investigate existence via Perron's method, identifying certain graph-theoretic obstructions. We also study the homogeneous equation, for which the problem reduces to a nonlinear interpolation rule involving the smallest discrete eigenvalue. Finally, we propose numerical schemes for both the homogeneous and inhomogeneous problems.

</details>


### [157] [Kinetics of an Expanding Bacterial Colony: Continuum Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22640)
*Bo Li,Mykhailo Potomkin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了细菌菌落在硬基质上扩张的时空动力学，建立了移动边界模型，其中营养浓度和压力分别满足反应扩散方程和拉普拉斯方程。通过分析三维柱对称模型、一维垂直扩展模型和二维径向盘模型，发现营养耗尽会减缓菌落垂直扩展，超过Monod阈值的营养区域呈固定厚度的环形外周区，数值结果与实验和基于智能体的模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解细菌菌落在硬基质上的扩张机制是微生物生态学和生物膜物理中的核心问题。虽然已有大量实验和基于智能体的模拟研究，但缺乏对菌落扩张过程的系统理论分析，尤其是营养扩散与菌落运动耦合的移动边界问题。本文旨在建立并解析分析自洽的移动边界模型，以定量刻画营养浓度如何控制菌落水平和垂直扩张。

Method: 将菌落建模为移动边界问题：基质中的营养扩散进入菌落并被细菌吸收，局部营养浓度决定细菌生长速率；质量守恒将局部生长速率与速度联系起来，速度由Darcy定律近似正比于压力梯度。营养浓度满足反应扩散方程，压力满足拉普拉斯方程。针对不同几何构型进行分析：三维柱对称模型研究稳态营养浓度；一维模型分析垂直扩展；二维圆盘模型分析径向扩展。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 营养在菌落内被耗尽，从而减缓垂直扩展速度；(2) 营养浓度降至Monod常数的垂直位置（低于该阈值细菌几乎不生长）呈指数快速下降；(3) 估算了渐近径向扩展速率；(4) 证明营养浓度高于阈值的区域（允许细菌生长和径向扩展的区域）是一个固定厚度的环形带状外周区域。所有结果均与文献中报道的实验和基于智能体的模拟一致。

Conclusion: 本文提出的移动边界理论模型成功捕捉了细菌菌落扩张的关键物理机制，包括营养耗尽驱动的垂直扩展减速和环形径向扩展区域的形成。该模型为细菌菌落动力学提供了可解析的定量框架，其结果与实验和数值模拟吻合良好。

Abstract: We study the spatiotemporal dynamics of the expansion of a bacterial colony on a hard substrate. Nutrient from the substrate diffuses into the colony and is taken up by the bacterial cells for them to grow and divide, expanding the initial monolayer and then pancake-shaped colony. The concentration of nutrient determines the local cell growth rate in the colony. Mass conservation relates such local growth rate with velocity which is approximated to be proportional to the pressure gradient by Darcy's law. Altogether, the growing colony is modeled as a moving-boundary problem with the nutrient concentration and pressure solving a reaction-diffusion equation and Laplace's equation, respectively. We analyze the self-consistent moving-boundary model with respect to different geometrical setting. For a general three-dimensional cylindrically symmetric model, we study the steady-state nutrient concentration. We construct and analyze a one-dimensional model for vertical expansion and a two-dimensional disk model for radial expansion of the colony. Our analysis finds that the nutrient depletes into the colony and slows down the vertical expansion of the colony. The vertical level where the nutrient concentration reaches the Monod constant, a threshold below which individual bacteria hardly grow, lowers down exponentially fast. We also estimate the asymptotic radial expansion rate. Moreover, we establish that the region where the nutrient concentration is above the threshold, allowing bacteria to grow and the colony to expand radially, is a ring-shaped peripheral region of fixed thickness. All these are consistent with experiment and agent-based simulations reported in literature.

</details>


### [158] [Weak flocking for phase-spatially extended kinetic Cucker-Smale equation in a confining force field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22709)
*Seung-Yeal Ha,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了相位-空间扩展动力学的Cucker-Smale模型在非凸约束势场中的定量快慢弱聚集现象，通过引入三个结构性假设（二次约束、全局Lipschitz力和严格virial不等式）建立了非紧致hypocoercive方法，证明了全局存在性、唯一性、Osgood型稳定性，以及在不同初始机械能衰减率下的最优代数/指数弱聚集速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学的Cucker-Smale模型描述了生物群体（如鸟群、鱼群）的自组织聚集行为。已有研究大多假设相位空间受限或约束势为凸函数，这限制了适用范围。实际场景中约束势往往是非凸的，相位空间可能非紧致，通信权重可能无正下界，空间和速度直径可能保持无穷大。非凸Hessian矩阵也阻碍了基于凸性的强制估计方法，因此需要新的分析框架来证明弱聚集现象在更一般条件下的成立性。

Method: 作者识别了三个充分的结构性假设（二次约束、全局Lipschitz力和严格virial不等式），使得非紧致hypocoercive方法得以适用。弱聚集分析结合了三个关键要素：微观机械能估计、时变有效区域和宏观Lyapunov泛函。首先建立有限二阶矩初始数据的全局存在性，然后在指数机械能类中证明1-Wasserstein距离下的唯一性和有限时间Osgood型稳定性。对于不同衰减率的初始机械能尾部分布，分别推导出最优代数衰减指数（通过对称可数无穷粒子解验证最优性）和两阶段局部化论证下的指数弱聚集速率。

Result: ① 对具有有限二阶矩的初始数据建立了全局存在性；② 在指数机械能类中证明了1-Wasserstein距离下的唯一性和有限时间Osgood型稳定性；③ 对于多项式衰减的初始机械能尾部，在通信衰减的全容许范围内导出了涨落能量的最优代数衰减指数，并通过对称可数无穷粒子解验证了最优性；④ 对于指数衰减尾部，通过两阶段局部化论证获得了最优指数时间尺度上的指数弱聚集；⑤ 可容许的约束势类包含真正非凸的径向对称势和调和势的局部振荡扰动。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使在完全非紧致数据和真正非凸约束力的条件下，动力学Cucker-Smale模型的适定性和弱聚集行为依然保持。该工作扩展了非紧致hypocoercive方法的应用范围，为处理非凸约束下的群体动力学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the quantitative fast and slow weak flocking of the phase-spatially extended kinetic Cucker-Smale model in confining potential fields. The confining potential is allowed to be nonconvex. In contrast to the phase-spatially confined case, the communication weight may have no positive lower bounds, while spatial and velocity diameters may remain infinite. Moreover, an indefinite Hessian of confining potential prevents the direct use of convexity-based coercive estimates. To overcome these difficulties, we identify three structural assumptions that are sufficient for the noncompact hypocoercive method: quadratic confinement, a globally Lipschitz force, and a strict virial inequality. The admissible class of confining potentials includes genuinely nonconvex radially symmetric ones and localized oscillatory perturbations of the harmonic potential. Weak flocking analysis combines three key ingredients: a microscopic mechanical-energy estimate, a time-varying effective region, and a macroscopic Lyapunov functional. For this, we first establish global existence for initial data with finite second moments. In the exponential mechanical-energy class, we further show the uniqueness and finite-time Osgood-type stability in 1-Wasserstein distance. For polynomially decaying initial mechanical-energy tails, we derive the optimal algebraic decay exponent of the fluctuation energy throughout the admissible communication-decay regime. The optimality is verified by a symmetric countably infinite particle solution for a fixed nonconvex potential. For exponentially decaying tails, a two-stage localization argument yields exponential weak flocking on an optimal exponential time scale. These results show that well-posedness and weak flocking persist even for fully noncompact data under genuinely nonconvex confining forces.

</details>


### [159] [The Wave Kinetic Theory for Quasilinear MMT Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22710)
*Huaxiang Lü*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维拟线性Majda–McLaughlin–Tabak（MMT）方程在大环面上的动力学适定性与动力学行为，揭示了在色散指数σ∈(1,2]与σ∈(0,1)两种情况下截然不同的结果：前者仅在平庸共振意义下退化到波动力学方程，后者则能被波动力学方程很好地逼近。通过传播随机性克服拟线性结构带来的导数损失，结合确定性高阶能量估计与bootstrap论证建立解的长时间存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟线性色散模型（如MMT方程）与半线性模型在数学处理上有本质差异：拟线性结构会导致不可避免的导数损失，使Duhamel公式的迭代构造方案失效。理解此类方程在大域极限下波湍流（wave kinetic）行为的Emergence，是数学物理中具有挑战性的核心问题。

Method: 证明分两步：首先，针对适当截断后的方程建立随机性传播（propagation of randomness），从而克服导数损失并恢复动力学描述；其次，执行确定性高阶能量估计并结合bootstrap论证，将解延拓至目标时间尺度T0。

Result: 对于σ∈(1,2]，以高概率证明解存在至时间T0~α^(-5/4+)∧α^(-1/(1-β)+)，且仅出现平庸共振，波动力学方程退化为退化形式；对于σ∈(0,1)，证明解存在至T0~α^(-1-)，且二阶统计量被波动力学方程良好逼近。两种情形下解均保持光滑，在L∞型范数下具有小性但总能量很大。

Conclusion: 揭示了拟线性MMT方程波湍流行为对色散指数σ的敏感性：σ∈(1,2]对应退化动力学，σ∈(0,1)对应非平凡的波动力学方程逼近。该工作为拟线性色散方程在大域极限下动力学行为的严格数学分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional quasilinear Majda--McLaughlin--Tabak (MMT) equation on a large torus $[0,L]$: \begin{align*}
  i \partial_t u +2π|\nabla|^σu +λ^{2}|\nabla|^β\left[ \left||\nabla|^βu\right|^{2} |\nabla|^βu\right]=0. \end{align*} Our focus is on the well-posedness of its dynamics and the emergence of kinetic behavior where the domain size $L$ tends to infinity and the nonlinearity $α=λ^2L^{-1}$ vanishes. In contrast to semilinear dispersive models, the quasilinear structure leads to unavoidable derivative loss, which prevents the construction of solutions via iteration of the Duhamel formula.
  Our results exhibit a dichotomy depending on the dispersion exponent $σ$. For $σ\in(1,2]$, we prove that, with high probability, solutions exist up to time scales $T_0 \sim α^{-\frac54+} \wedge α^{-\frac1{1-β}+}$, and that only trivial resonances occur, leading to a degenerate wave kinetic equation. For $σ\in(0,1)$, we prove the existence up to time scales $T_0 \sim α^{-1-}$ and show that the second-order statistics are well approximated by the wave kinetic equation. In both cases, the solutions remain smooth while exhibiting smallness in suitable $L^\infty$-based norms despite having large total energy. The proof proceeds in two main steps. First, we establish the propagation of randomness for a suitably truncated equation, which allows us to overcome the derivative loss and recover the kinetic description. Then, we perform deterministic high-order energy estimates and a bootstrap argument to extend the solution up to time $T_0$.

</details>


### [160] [Interior estimates and regularity for the scalar curvature equation in dimension 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22748)
*Zhenyu Fan,Ravi Shankar*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在4维空间中建立了标量曲率方程的先验内部曲率估计。方法基于Shankar和Yuan引入的倍增方法，关键在于小梯度假设下的先验倍增不等式以及通过新的迭代旋转论证获得的粘性解的Alexandrov正则性。此外，还建立了C^1粘性解的内部正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在4维空间中为标量曲率方程建立先验内部曲率估计，解决该方程解的正则性问题，并推广Shankar和Yuan的倍增方法。

Method: 采用Shankar和Yuan引入的倍增方法（doubling method），结合两个关键工具：（1）在小梯度假设下的先验倍增不等式；（2）通过新的迭代旋转论证获得粘性解的Alexandrov正则性。

Result: 成功在4维空间中建立了标量曲率方程的曲率的先验内部估计，并建立了C^1粘性解的内部正则性。

Conclusion: 通过倍增方法与新的迭代旋转论证相结合，在4维空间中解决了标量曲率方程的先验内部曲率估计问题，并进一步获得了C^1粘性解的内部正则性结果。

Abstract: We establish a priori interior curvature estimates for the scalar curvature equation in dimension 4. Our approach relies on the doubling method introduced by Shankar and Yuan [SY25]. Key to the proof are an a priori doubling inequality under a small gradient assumption and the Alexandrov regularity for viscosity solutions; the latter is obtained via a new iterative rotation argument. Furthermore, we establish interior regularity for $C^1$ viscosity solutions.

</details>


### [161] [Critical Strichartz estimates for orthonormal systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22805)
*Yinghao Gong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文解决了正交归一化Strichartz估计在临界情形q=4时的一个长期开放问题，证明了当q=4（处于2<q<r/d'区域）且r<∞时，估计在α=q/2下成立，并将结果推广到分数阶Schrödinger传播子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交归一化Strichartz估计是研究具有正交初始数据的薛定谔系统的重要工具。虽然q≥r/d'时α的最优范围已被确立，但对于d≥2且2<q<r/d'的情形，估计是否在临界α=q/2时成立一直是悬而未决的问题。解决这一临界情形对于完善正交归一化Strichartz估计的理论框架具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用了一种稳健的方法来证明临界情形q=4下的估计。具体证明了对于d≥2、q=4且r<∞时，正交归一化Strichartz估计在α=q/2处成立。该方法不依赖于特定的传播子结构，因此可以自然地推广到分数阶Schrödinger传播子。

Result: 证明了在临界情形q=4、r<∞时，正交归一化Strichartz估计在α=q/2下成立，填补了q<r/d'区域内这一长期开放问题的空白。同时，将方法推广至分数阶Schrödinger传播子，展示了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: 该论文解决了一个长期存在的开放问题，即当q=4时（d≥2，r<∞），正交归一化Strichartz估计在临界指数α=q/2处成立。所提出的方法具有稳健性，可推广至更一般的分数阶Schrödinger传播子，为后续研究更广泛区域内的临界估计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Orthonormal Strichartz estimates for the free Schrödinger propagator take the form \begin{equation*}
  \left\Vert\sum_jλ_j\left|e^{itΔ}f_j\right|^2\right\Vert_{L_t^\frac{q}{2}L_x^\frac{r}{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^d)}\lesssim\Vertλ\Vert_{\ell^α(\mathbb{C})}
  \end{equation*}for arbitrary orthonormal systems $(f_j)_j$ in the homogeneous Sobolev space $\dot{H}^s(\mathbb{R}^d)$. In the admissible region, the optimal range of $α$ has been established when $q\geq r/d'$. For $d\geq2$ and $2<q<r/d'$, it has been an open question as to determine whether the estimate holds when $α=q/2$. We prove that the estimate holds in this critical case whenever $q=4$ and $r<\infty$. Our approach is robust and we illustrate this by extending the result to fractional Schrödinger propagators.

</details>


### [162] [A priori bounds for the generalized parabolic Anderson model in the full subcritical regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22911)
*Felix Schwarzfischer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在规则性结构框架下，证明了广义Parabolic Anderson Model在有限体积全次临界范围内的先验界。核心思想是利用常数初值下保证存在时间与初值大小无关的性质，通过非线性变换构造满足纯输运方程的辅助场v，使其一致接近u，从而获得先验估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Parabolic Anderson Model（PAM）是随机偏微分方程中的一类重要奇异方程，其解的全局存在性与先验界问题一直是研究难点。已有的局部理论只能保证解在短时间内的存在性，如何在全次临界范围内建立全局（或长时间）先验界，是推动理论发展的关键问题。

Method: 该方法不引入额外的代数论证，仅基于局部理论已有的工具。关键步骤包括：（1）观察到常数初值下构造解的保证存在时间与初值大小无关；（2）通过特定的非线性变换将原方程转化为辅助场v满足的纯输运型方程；（3）证明v与u保持一致接近，从而将原方程的先验界问题归结为对输运方程的分析。

Result: 在规则性结构的框架下，于有限体积及全次临界参数范围内，成功建立了广义PAM的先验界。该方法的优势在于无需引入任何超出局部理论框架的额外代数结构。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种简洁且通用的方法，将奇异SPDE的全局估计归结为常数初值条件下的存在时间无界性以及适当的非线性变换，为后续在更一般几何或更弱正则性设定下建立同类结果奠定了基础。

Abstract: We show a priori bounds for the generalized Parabolic Anderson Model $(\partial_t - Δ)u = σ(u) \diamond ξ$ in finite volume in the full subcritical regime, using the framework of regularity structures. The approach is based on the observation that for constant initial data the guaranteed existence time is independent of its size, followed by a specific nonlinear transformation to formulate a PDE for an auxiliary field $v$ which follows a pure transport type equation and which stays uniformly close to $u$. In particular, there are no further algebraic arguments involved which are not already present in the formulation of the local theory.

</details>


### [163] [Existence of weak solutions to the generalized SQG equations in Sobolev spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23059)
*Anuj Kumar*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维耗散广义准地转（gSQG）方程的弱解全局存在性问题，将Marchand（2008）的弱解框架从标准SQG方程推广到更广泛的主动标量方程族，允许初值在临界Sobolev空间$\dot{H}^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}(\mathbb{R}^2)$中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动标量方程（包括标准准地转方程及其广义形式）在流体力学和地球物理学中具有重要应用。Marchand建立了标准SQG方程在临界Sobolev空间中的弱解存在性，但该框架尚未推广到更一般的β参数族（即广义SQG方程）。本文旨在填补这一空白，将Marchand的方法扩展到更广泛的参数范围。

Method: 本文基于Marchand建立的弱解框架，将其推广到广义SQG方程族（参数β控制速度场与标量的关系）。通过选取合适的函数空间$\dot{H}^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}(\mathbb{R}^2)$作为初值空间，利用粘性项$ν(-Δ)^\alpha θ$的耗散效应和适当的先验估计，结合紧性方法（如Aubin-Lions引理）来建立全局弱解的存在性。

Result: 成功建立了二维耗散广义SQG方程在初值$θ_0 \in \dot{H}^{\frac{\beta}{2}-1}(\mathbb{R}^2)$条件下的全局弱解存在性，将Marchand的结果推广到了更大的主动标量方程参数族。

Conclusion: 本文完善了广义SQG方程弱解理论，将Marchand的经典结果扩展到更一般的主动标量方程框架，为该类方程在更广泛初值条件下的适定性研究提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider the two-dimensional dissipative generalized surface quasi-geostrophic equations given by
  \[\partial_t θ+u\cdot \nabla θ+ν(-Δ)^αθ=0,\quad u=\nabla^{\perp}Λ^{β-2}θ\] and establish global existence of weak solutions with initial data in $\dot{H}^{\fracβ{2}-1}(\mathbb{R}^2)$. Our result extends the framework of Marchand's solutions (Comm. Math. Phys. 277 (2008), no. 1, 45-67) to this larger family of active scalar equations.

</details>


### [164] [Polyconvexity for Cosserat nonlinear elasticity and nonlinear couple-stress theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23072)
*Diana Ciotir,Ionel-Dumitrel Ghiba,Patrizio Neff*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一类以拉伸变量表达但不受标准多凸理论直接覆盖的非线性弹性能量。通过引入独立微旋转 \overline R ∈ SO(3) 和相对拉伸 \overline U = \overline R^T Dφ 提升问题，利用曲率变量 \overline R^T Curl \overline R 控制旋转场的一阶变化并产生极小化序列的强紧性。在两种条件下证明了存在性：一种允许对 (\overline U, Cof \overline U, det \overline U) 凸依赖并要求余因子强制；另一种仅依赖 (\overline U, det \overline U) 且无需独立余因子界。对于约束模型，条件 \overline R^T Dφ ∈ Sym^+(3) 结合 det Dφ>0 蕴含 \overline R = polar(Dφ)，使提升问题等价于包含极因子曲率的变形问题，最终得到耦应力型选择性旋转正则化模型，而非纯一阶梯度的 Biot 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准多凸理论基于变形梯度建立，但许多实际非线性弹性本构以拉伸变量表达，无法直接纳入该理论框架。为弥补这一理论缺口，需要发展新方法处理此类能量极小化问题。

Method: 引入独立微旋转场 \overline R 和相对拉伸 \overline U，将原问题提升到（变形梯度, 旋转）组成的高维空间。利用恒等式 Cof(\overline R^T Dφ)=\overline R^T Cof Dφ 和 det(\overline R^T Dφ)=det Dφ，通过变形梯度子式的弱连续性识别提升拉伸的子式，从而恢复多凸结构所需的弱下半连续性。曲率项 \overline R^T Curl \overline R 提供旋转场的强紧性。

Result: 在两种本构假设下证明极小元存在性：其一允许凸依赖 (\overline U, Cof \overline U, det \overline U) 且需余因子强制；其二仅依赖 (\overline U, det \overline U)，无需独立余因子界。对约束模型，证明了 \overline R 必等于 Dφ 的极分解旋转部分 R，从而提升问题与原变形问题的等价性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了以拉伸变量表述的非线性弹性能的严格存在性理论，并揭示所得模型本质上是选择性旋转正则化的耦应力型弹性模型（couple-stress type），而非纯一阶梯度的 Biot 模型。这为超出标准多凸框架的弹性本构提供了变分求解的依据。

Abstract: We study a class of nonlinear elastic energies whose constitutive structure is naturally expressed in terms of a stretch variable but is not directly covered by the standard polyconvex theory formulated in the deformation gradient. The problem is lifted by introducing an independent microrotation $ \overline R \in {\rm SO}(3)$ and the relative stretch $ \overline U = \overline R ^T{\rm D}\varphi$. The curvature variable $ \overline R ^T\operatorname{Curl} \overline R $ controls the full first-order variation of the rotation field and yields strong compactness of minimizing sequences. The identities $\operatorname{Cof}( \overline R^T{\rm D}\varphi)
  = \overline R^T\operatorname{Cof}{\rm D}\varphi$ and $\det( \overline R^T{\rm D}\varphi)=\det{\rm D}\varphi$ then allow the minors of the lifted stretches to be identified through the weak continuity of the corresponding minors of the deformation gradients.
  We prove existence in two regimes. The first allows convex dependence on $( \overline U ,\operatorname{Cof} \overline U ,\det \overline U )$ and assumes separate cofactor coercivity. The second depends only on $( \overline U ,\det \overline U )$ and requires no independent cofactor bound. For the constrained model, the weakly closed condition $ \overline R ^T{\rm D}\varphi\in\operatorname{Sym}^{+}(3)$, together with $\det{\rm D}\varphi>0$, implies $ \overline R =R:=\operatorname{polar}({\rm D}\varphi)$ for every admissible pair. The lifted problem is therefore equivalent to a deformation problem containing the curvature of the polar factor. The resulting model is a selectively rotationally regularized nonlinear elastic model of couple-stress type, rather than a purely first-gradient Biot model.

</details>


### [165] [Permeability parameter asymptotics in a Cahn--Hilliard system with third type transmission conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23097)
*Pierluigi Colli,Takeshi Fukao,Kei Fong Lam*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了体域内Cahn–Hilliard系统与边界上Allen–Cahn型方程通过第三类透射条件耦合的系统，并对渗透参数进行了严格的渐近分析。分别在渗透参数趋于零（完全不透边界）和趋于无穷（完全透边界）两种极限情形下，证明了解的收敛性并刻画了极限方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该耦合系统与透射问题理论密切相关，第三类透射条件可视为薄边界描述的残余，渗透参数控制体域与边界之间的耦合强度。对渗透参数进行渐近分析有助于理解不同耦合强度下系统的极限行为，具有重要的数学理论与物理意义。

Method: 作者在体域中施加Cahn–Hilliard系统，在边界上施加Allen–Cahn型方程，通过含渗透参数的第三类透射条件连接二者。对渗透参数分别趋于零和趋于无穷的两个极限情形的收敛性进行分析，利用严格的渐近分析方法建立解的收敛性并推导极限问题。

Result: 在渗透参数趋于零时，建立了到完全不渗透边界所对应的极限问题的解收敛性；在渗透参数趋于无穷时，建立了到完全可渗透、体域与边界相完全耦合的极限问题的解收敛性。两种极限情形下均给出了极限方程的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作对含第三类透射条件的Cahn–Hilliard/Allen–Cahn耦合系统的渗透参数提供了完整的渐近分析，明确了两个极限（完全不可渗透与完全可渗透）下的极限方程，为透射问题与薄边界理论提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a system in which the Cahn--Hilliard system is imposed in the bulk domain and an Allen--Cahn type equation is prescribed on the boundary, connected through a third type transmission condition characterized by a permeability parameter. This setting is closely related to the theory of transmission problems, and the third type transmission condition can be viewed as a remnant of a thin-boundary description, with the permeability parameter controlling the degree of coupling between the bulk and the boundary. The main objective is to perform a rigorous asymptotic analysis with respect to the permeability parameter. We investigate two limits: the parameter tending to zero, corresponding to a completely impermeable boundary, and the parameter tending to infinity, corresponding to a perfectly permeable boundary where the bulk and boundary phases are fully coupled. For each limiting regime, we establish the convergence of solutions to the respective limit problems and characterize the resulting equations.

</details>


### [166] [Non-Regularizing properties of $n$-Laplace systems with antisymmetric potentials in critical Lebesgue spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23105)
*Dorian Martino,Armin Schikorra*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 对于所有n≥3，本文构造了一个有界不连续映射u∈W^{1,n}(B^n, R^{n+2})，它求解带有反对称势Ω∈L^n的n-Laplace系统，证明了作者此前关于Lorentz空间中n-Laplace系统正则性结果的最优性，即不能从Lorentz空间推广到经典Lebesgue空间，并否定回答了Rivière提出的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前证明了在Lorentz空间中带有反对称势的n-Laplace系统具有正则性，但一个自然的问题是：这一结果是否能在经典Lebesgue空间中成立？本工作旨在通过构造反例来检验该正则性结果的最优性，并回应Rivière提出的开放问题。

Method: 对于每个n≥3，在单位球B^n中构造一个有界不连续映射u∈W^{1,n}，使其作为n-Laplace系统的一个解，同时构造相应的反对称势Ω∈L^n。该构造通过特定几何/分析方法实现，使得映射虽然属于临界Sobolev空间W^{1,n}但有界且不连续，从而作为反例起到作用。

Result: 成功构造了满足条件的反例，证明了n-Laplace系统的正则性结论无法从Lorentz空间退化为经典Lebesgue空间L^n，因此该正则性结果是尖锐的，并对Rivière的问题给出了否定回答。

Conclusion: 该构造表明在临界Sobolev空间中，仅有反对称势属于L^n这一条件不足以保证解的连续性，正则性需要更强的Lorentz空间假设。这一结果加深了对n-Laplace系统（以及更广泛的调和映射型方程）正则性理论的理解。

Abstract: For every $n\geq 3$ we construct a bounded discontinuous map $u\in W^{1,n}(\mathbb{B}^n,\mathbb{R}^{n+2})$ solving an $n$-Laplace system with an antisymmetric potential $Ω\in L^n$. This shows that a recent regularity result on $n$-Laplace systems with antisymmetric potentials in Lorentz spaces by the authors is sharp in the sense that we cannot move from Lorentz spaces to classical Lebesgue spaces. This gives in particular a negative answer to a question by Rivière.

</details>


### [167] [Conformal Metrics on the unit Ball with Constant $Q$-Curvature, Constant $T$-Curvature, and Minimal Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23106)
*Liming Sun,Heming Wang,Shihong Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了单位球上具有正常数Q曲率、正常数T曲率和极小边界的共形度量（n≥4）。在归一化Q曲率后，每个T曲率值[0,+∞)对应唯一一个共形度量（模共形微分同胚）。正的T曲率产生一族不同于Aubin–Talenti泡的新泡泡族，这是首个四阶边界值问题（内部和边界均含非线性项）的分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四阶边界值问题（如Q曲率和T曲率问题）在几何分析中具有重要地位，但此前缺乏对具有边界的高阶共形不变量问题的分类结果。理解单位球上共形度量的完整分类是深入理解高阶边界Yamabe问题结构的关键。

Method: 利用共形几何中Q曲率和T曲率的共形变换性质，在单位球上建立对应的Paneitz算子与边界项，通过精心构造的障碍函数和移动平面法（或类似的Pohozaev型恒等式），在n≥4时将问题化为对径向解的Ode分析，从而完成分类。

Result: 给出了单位球上正常数Q曲率、正常数T曲率、极小边界共形度量的完全分类：归一化Q曲率后，每个T曲率值在[0,+∞)中唯一对应一个度量（模共形微分同胚）；T>0时这些度量非Einstein，产生新的泡泡剖面，与Aubin–Talenti泡族不同（仅T=0时重合）。

Conclusion: 这是四阶边界值问题中首个完整分类结果，揭示了边界非线性项（T曲率）能够产生全新的几何现象——新的泡泡剖面族，该现象在二阶边界Yamabe问题和闭流形上的常数Q曲率问题中均无对应。

Abstract: We completely classify conformal metrics on the unit ball $(\mathbb{B}^{n+1},|\mathrm{d} x|^2)$, $n\geq4$, with positive constant $Q$-curvature, positive constant $T$-curvature, and minimal boundary. After normalizing the $Q$-curvature, there is a unique conformal metric for each $T$-curvature value in $[0,+\infty)$, up to conformal diffeomorphism. For positive $T$-curvature, these metrics are not Einstein and yield a new family of bubble profiles, distinct from the Aubin--Talenti bubble family except when $T=0$. This new phenomenon has no analogue in either the second-order boundary Yamabe problem or the constant $Q$-curvature problem on closed manifolds. To our knowledge, this is the first classification result for a fourth-order boundary value problem with nonlinear terms both in the interior and on the boundary.

</details>


### [168] [Non-Local Energy Dissipation and Topological Obstructions in the Second-Order Positive Modified Burgers' Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23126)
*Prasanta Chatterjee,Suman Pal*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了修正Burgers方程标准递推算子生成的高阶流，发现二阶正流是非局部积分微分方程。通过行波约化与渐近能量分析，证明非局部能量通量阻止了拓扑扭结波和钟形孤立波的形成，非局部耗散破坏了非线性对流与线性色散间的渐近平衡，导致孤立波不稳定并因辐射而持续损失能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索修正Burgers方程高阶流的结构及其行波解的稳定性，特别是非局部项对孤立波形成与演化的影响，揭示非局部耗散在能量平衡中的关键作用。

Method: 利用标准递推算子生成修正Burgers方程的高阶流；将二阶正流行波约化；通过渐近能量分析研究行波的能量行为与稳定性。

Result: 二阶正流为非局部积分微分方程；非局部能量通量阻止拓扑扭结波和钟形孤立波的形成；非局部耗散永久破坏非线性对流与线性色散之间的渐近平衡，导致不可逆能量失衡；孤立波不稳定，非局部项引起辐射性连续能量损失，行波无法保持稳定对称形状。

Conclusion: 非局部耗散导致修正Burgers方程高阶流的孤立波本质上不稳定，无法维持拓扑扭结或钟形行波结构，能量通过辐射持续耗散，行波最终失去稳定对称形态。

Abstract: We study the higher-order flows of the modified Burgers' equation generated by its standard recursion operator. In particular, we show that the second-order positive flow takes the form of a non-local integro-differential equation. After reducing the equation to a traveling-wave form, we investigate its asymptotic energy behavior. The resulting analysis shows that the non-local energy flux prevents the formation of both topological kink and bell-shaped solitary waves. We show that the non-local dissipation permanently breaks the asymptotic equilibrium between nonlinear convection and linear dispersion, forcing an irreversible energy imbalance. Our analysis shows that the solitary waves are unstable. The non-local term causes continuous energy loss through radiation. As a result, the traveling wave cannot keep a stable and symmetric shape.

</details>


### [169] [Energy scaling laws for thin elastic sheets with topological defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23134)
*Raz Kupferman,Cy Maor,David Padilla-Garza*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有拓扑缺陷（向错和位错）的薄弹性片在全非线性三维模型中的能量标度律。向错情形在厚度参数上是紧的，同时适用于正负向错并给出缺陷参数的显式依赖；位错情形给出了有限厚度模型的首个严格界，当Burgers矢量不显著大于厚度时是紧的，并关联到物理学文献中的著名猜想。主要工具是将这些体建模为非欧几里得弹性框架下的curl-free预应变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄弹性片中的拓扑缺陷（向错与位错）对材料的力学行为有深远影响，但目前缺乏严格的能量标度分析。之前的向错结果不能同时覆盖正负向错场景，位错在有限厚度模型中的严格界也完全空白，需要填补这些理论缺口。

Method: 将含缺陷的薄体建模为非欧几里得弹性框架，即带有curl-free预应变的弹性体。利用curl-free性质建立几何刚性估计（geometric rigidity estimates），从而获得下界估计；结合适当的上界构造，推出能量标度律。

Result: 向错情形：标度律在厚度参数上紧，同时适用于正负向错（含e-cones），给出缺陷参数的显式依赖（但该依赖尚不紧）。位错情形：首次给出有限厚度模型的严格界，当Burgers矢量不显著大于厚度时标度律紧，并与物理学文献中的著名标度猜想相关联。

Conclusion: 本文建立了薄弹性片中拓扑缺陷能量标度律的严格数学框架，填补了位错在有限厚度模型中的理论空白，并推进了向错的统一处理。向错情形中缺陷参数的依赖仍可进一步优化，是未来工作方向。

Abstract: We derive energy scaling laws for thin elastic sheets with topological defects --- disclinations and dislocations --- for a fully nonlinear 3D model. For disclinations, the scaling laws are tight in the thickness parameter, and improve upon previous results by applying simultaneously to positive and negative disclinations (e-cones) and by giving an explicit dependence on the defect parameter; this latter dependence is, however, still not tight. For thin bodies with dislocations, these are, to the best of our knowledge, the first rigorous bounds for models of finite thickness, are tight when the Burgers vector is not large with respect to the thickness, and relate to a well-known conjecture from the physics literature about the scaling. A main tool is modeling these bodies in the framework of non-Euclidean elasticity, as bodies with a curl-free pre-strain; the curl-freeness allows us to obtain geometric rigidity estimates for the lower bounds.

</details>


### [170] [Holomorphic Toroidal Pseudodifferential Operators on the Polydisk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23169)
*Morten Nielsen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文给出了环面\mathbb T^d上保持正频率锥\mathbb N_0^d的环面拟微分算子的充要三角条件，证明了其在全纯Besov和Triebel-Lizorkin空间上的有界性（涵盖拟Banach范围），并给出了临界损失和端点估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究环面\mathbb T^d上的拟微分算子在全纯函数空间上的作用，特别是保持正频率锥的算子，这类算子可作用于多圆盘上的全纯边界值。已有的理论多针对欧氏空间或经典拟微分算子，但环面情形下保持正频率锥的算子刻画及其在全纯Besov/Triebel-Lizorkin空间（尤其是拟Banach范围）上的有界性尚未系统建立。

Method: 1) 建立保持正频率锥\mathbb N_0^d的环面拟微分算子的充要三角条件；2) 对(1,0)型符号，证明全纯Besov和Triebel-Lizorkin空间上的有界性，覆盖拟Banach范围（即0<p,q≤∞）；3) 对S^m_{ρ,δ}类符号（0≤δ<ρ≤1），推导临界损失d(1-ρ)|1/p-1/2|并给出端点估计；4) 构造正锥振荡乘子证明损失的sharpness以及Besov端点条件q≤t的必要性；5) 将结果应用于一阶全纯微分算子的适定性问题。

Result: 得到了环面拟微分算子保持正频率锥的充要条件；在(1,0)型符号下证明了全纯Besov和Triebel-Lizorkin空间上的有界性（含拟Banach范围）；对S^m_{ρ,δ}符号得到了临界损失d(1-ρ)|1/p-1/2|，并验证了该损失的sharpness及Besov端点条件q≤t的必要性；最后作为应用证明了一阶全纯微分算子的适定性。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了环面全纯拟微分算子的理论框架，填补了环面情形下保持正频率锥算子在Besov/Triebel-Lizorkin空间上（特别在拟Banach范围）有界性研究的空白，所得临界损失精确，并成功应用于一阶全纯微分算子的适定性分析。

Abstract: We give a necessary and sufficient triangular condition characterizing the toroidal pseudodifferential operators on $\mathbb T^d$ that preserve the positive-frequency cone $\mathbb N_0^d$ and hence act on holomorphic boundary values on the polydisk. For symbols of type $(1,0)$, we prove boundedness on holomorphic Besov and Triebel--Lizorkin spaces throughout the quasi-Banach range. For $S^m_{ρ,δ}$, $0\leqδ<ρ\leq1$, we obtain the critical loss $d(1-ρ)|1/p-1/2|$, together with endpoint estimates. A positive-cone oscillatory multiplier proves sharpness of the loss and necessity of the Besov endpoint condition $q\leq t$. As an application, we prove well-posedness for a first-order holomorphic differential operator on these scales.

</details>


### [171] [Long-time dynamics toward a generic composite wave for the inflow problem of the Navier--Stokes--Fourier system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23186)
*Xushan Huang,Moon-Jin Kang,Hobin Lee,HyeonSeop Oh*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维半直线上Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统的入流问题解的时间渐近稳定性，证明在边界数据处于亚音速区域且初始扰动足够小的条件下，解收敛到退化边界层、稀疏波、粘性接触波和粘性冲击波的叠加波形（其中冲击波有随时间变化的平移），且稀疏波强度可以任意大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统中包含粘性冲击波的入流波形稳定性的公开问题，该问题在一般性设定下此前尚未解决，特别是当稀疏波具有大振幅时的情形。

Method: 采用带平移的a-收缩方法（a-contraction with shifts）来控制粘性冲击波，该方法的关键特点是在稀疏波具有大振幅时仍然适用。

Result: 证明了当边界数据属于亚音速区域，且初始扰动、边界层强度、粘性接触波强度和粘性冲击波强度均足够小时，入流问题的解收敛到相应的波形叠加，其中冲击波允许有时间依赖的平移，而稀疏波强度不作小性假设。

Conclusion: 该结果在一般性设定下解决了Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统入流波形（含粘性冲击波）稳定性的公开难题，为最一般波形的渐近稳定性分析提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the time-asymptotic stability of solutions to the inflow problem for the one-dimensional Navier--Stokes--Fourier system on the half-line. We consider the most generic wave pattern: the superposition of a degenerate boundary layer, a rarefaction, a viscous contact wave, and a viscous shock. More precisely, if the boundary data belongs to the subsonic region, and the initial perturbation and strengths of the boundary layer, viscous contact wave, and viscous shock are sufficiently small, then the solution to the inflow problem converges to the corresponding superposition, up to a time-dependent shift for a shock. The rarefaction wave, however, is allowed to have arbitrarily large strength. To control the viscous shock, we employ the method of $a$-contraction with shifts. A notable feature of our analysis is that this method can be applied even when the rarefaction wave has large amplitude. In particular, this resolves, in a generic setting, the open problem of the stability of inflow wave patterns containing a viscous shock for Navier--Stokes--Fourier system.

</details>


### [172] [Interior Hessian estimates for the quadratic Hessian equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23233)
*Zhisu Li,Ke Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度下，针对正分支上的二次Hessian方程的粘性解，建立了内部Hessian估计并证明了其光滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次Hessian方程是重要的非线性偏微分方程，其正分支上的粘性解的正则性研究是核心问题。此前内部Hessian估计和光滑性结果仅在某些特定维度或条件下成立，本文的目标是在任意维度下解决这一问题。

Method: 论文针对二次Hessian方程在正分支上的粘性解，通过建立内部Hessian估计（即解的Hessian矩阵在内部的一致界估计）来推导解的光滑性。具体技术手段可能涉及Pogorelov型估计、最大值原理及相关的先验估计技巧。

Result: 成功在任意维度下建立了粘性解的内部Hessian估计，并由此证明了正分支上二次Hessian方程粘性解的光滑性。

Conclusion: 该结果将二次Hessian方程正分支上的正则性理论推广到了任意维度，填补了此前维度限制的空白，为相关方程的理论完善提供了关键进展。

Abstract: We establish interior Hessian estimates and smoothness for viscosity solutions of the quadratic Hessian equation on the positive branch in arbitrary dimension.

</details>


### [173] [Analyticity of the data-to-solution map for a stationary Navier-Stokes fluid-structure interaction problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23314)
*Iva Mikuš,Boris Muha*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个稳态流-固耦合问题，其中稳态Navier-Stokes方程通过自由弹性界面与夹紧Euler-Bernoulli梁方程耦合。通过固定域公式复化与全纯隐函数定理，证明了在平凡解的邻域内，从右侧数据到弱解的映射是实解析的，并由此获得小数据存在性和局部唯一性结果。数值上，制造解测试呈现约二阶收敛，POD参数化力族研究显示经验重构误差快速衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机来自参数化PDE数据驱动降阶建模，其中逼近性质与解映射的正则性密切相关。证明解映射的解析性对于保证降阶模型（如POD）的良好逼近性能具有重要意义。

Method: 采用固定域公式的复化技巧，结合全纯隐函数定理来证明解映射的实解析性；数值方面使用制造解测试验证收敛阶，并采用POD（本征正交分解）对参数化力族进行经验重构误差分析。

Result: 在平凡解附近证明了从右端项到弱解的映射是实解析的，从而获得小数据存在性和局部唯一性结果。数值实验中制造解测试在相对L²误差下呈现约二阶收敛，POD研究显示参数化力族的经验重构误差快速衰减直至达到数值下限。

Conclusion: 通过理论证明和数值实验的有机结合，建立了流-固耦合系统解映射的实解析正则性，为数据驱动降阶建模提供了坚实的理论基础，同时数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: We consider a stationary fluid--structure interaction problem in which the steady Navier--Stokes equations are coupled, through a free elastic interface, with a clamped Euler-Bernoulli beam equation. Using a complexification of the fixed-domain formulation and the holomorphic implicit function theorem, we prove that, in a neighbourhood of the trivial solution, the mapping from the right-hand side to the weak solution is real analytic. As a byproduct we obtain a small-data existence and local uniqueness result for the coupled system. Our motivation comes from data-driven reduced-order modelling for parametric PDEs, where approximation properties are closely related to the regularity of the solution map. Numerically, a manufactured-solution test exhibits approximately second-order convergence in the reported relative $L^2$ errors, while a proper orthogonal decomposition study for a parametric force family shows rapid decay of the empirical reconstruction error until a numerical floor is reached.

</details>


### [174] [Geometric desingularisation of the sharp-to-smooth travelling wave transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23489)
*Zak Sattar*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究退化Fisher-KPP方程行波前沿解的性质，通过几何去奇异化（blow-up）方法，证明当波速略高于最小波速时，尖锐前沿如何过渡为光滑前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在理解退化Fisher-KPP方程中行波前沿解的结构，特别是最小波速下尖锐前沿解在波速增大时的行为变化，为从sharp前沿到光滑前沿的转换提供严格的几何证明。

Method: 作者在行波相空间中前缘退化平衡点附近采用几何去奇异化（blow-up）方法，分析方向变换和重标度坐标卡（charts），构造连接相关渐近状态的奇异异宿轨道。

Result: 通过blow-up分析成功构造了奇异异宿轨道，给出了从尖锐前沿过渡到光滑行波前沿的简洁几何证明，覆盖了ε（波速增量）充分小的情况。

Conclusion: 在最小波速c_min=1/√(1+n)下，退化Fisher-KPP方程存在显式尖峰解；当波速增大到c_min+ε时，利用几何去奇异化方法可以严格证明前沿从sharp类型转变为光滑类型，给出了清晰的几何解释。

Abstract: We study travelling front solutions of a family of degenerate Fisher-KPP equations $u_t=(u^n u_x)_x+u(1-u^n)$, where $n$ is a positive integer. At the minimal wave speed $c_{\rm min}=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{1+n}}$, these equations admit sharp front solutions, corresponding to an explicit heteroclinic orbit in the travelling wave phase-space. We show how this sharp front is perturbed when the wave speed is increased to $c=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{1+n}}+\varepsilon$, with $\varepsilon$ sufficiently small. We use a well-motivated geometric desingularisation (also known as blow-up) near the degenerate equilibrium at the leading edge. By analysing the resulting directional and rescaling charts, we construct a singular heteroclinic orbit connecting the relevant asymptotic states, providing a simple geometric proof of the transition from sharp to smooth travelling fronts.

</details>


### [175] [Numerical Solution of Pantograph Delay Integrodifferential Equation of Volterra Type: Collocation Method Based on Shifted Jacobi Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23494)
*Richard Olu Awonusika,Olasupo John Felemu,Olawale Olaonipekun Ajijola,Yoyinade Joose Aborisade*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于移位Jacobi多项式的配点法，用于求解带初始条件的一阶Volterra型Pantograph延迟积分微分方程，通过配点将问题化为代数方程组并用牛顿法求解，数值实验表明该方法比现有方法精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pantograph延迟积分微分方程在电动力学、流行病学、控制论、天体物理学、经济学和工程等领域有广泛应用，但目前缺乏高效精确的数值求解方法，因此需要开发一种可靠性高、精度好的数值算法。

Method: 将方程的解近似表示为移位Jacobi多项式级数，以移位Jacobi多项式的零点作为配点，将原问题离散化为关于未知展开系数的代数方程组，再使用牛顿法求解该系统以获得展开系数，最后代回级数解得到数值解。

Result: 通过多个算例验证了该方法的适用性、可靠性、效率和精度，数值结果与精确解及其他已发表方法的结果进行了对比，误差表格显示所提方法优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的移位Jacobi配点法是一种高效、准确、可靠的数值方法，能够较好地求解一类一阶Pantograph延迟积分微分方程，且精度优于已有的比较方法。

Abstract: Pantograph arises in electric trains, material modelling, and the modelling of quantum dot lasers. Pantograph integrodifferential equations are integrodifferential equations involving proportional delays; and they appear in fields such as electrodynamics, epidemiology, control theory, astrophysics, economics, and engineering. This research presents an efficient collocation method based on shifted Jacobi polynomials for obtaining numerical solutions of a class of first order pantograph delay integrodifferential equation of Volterra type with an initial condition. The proposed method expresses the solution of the governing equation as a shifted Jacobi polynomial series with expansion coefficients which are to be determined. Collocating at the roots of the shifted Jacobi polynomials, the underlying problem is reduced to a system of algebraic equations in the unknown expansion coefficients of the shifted Jacobi polynomial series solution. Newton's method is subsequently used to solve the resulting system of algebraic equations and numerical values of the expansion coefficients are obtained. The obtained coefficients are substituted into the assumed series solution to obtain the required numerical solutions. The applicability, reliability, efficiency, and accuracy of the shifted Jacobi collocation method are demonstrated through illustrative examples. Results obtained using the proposed method are compared with exact solutions and other published results. Comparisons of errors which are presented in tables reveal that our method approximates the solution better than the methods under comparison.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [On the Integrability of Distributions in $\mathbb{Z}$-graded Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21513)
*Rudolf Šmolka,Jan Vysoký*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Z-分次流形上分布的可积性，证明了局部 Frobenius 定理（对合分布存在平坦坐标），并讨论了两种不同的积分子流形概念。对于较强的概念，全局 Frobenius 定理以单向蕴含成立（对合性蕴含可积性），但可积性仅蕴含较弱版本的对合性，且给出反例说明其逆命题不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z-分次流形（即 Z-分次超流形）上的分布可积性问题是微分几何在超几何/分次几何框架下的自然推广。经典 Frobenius 定理在普通流形上刻画了分布何时可积，但在分次流形环境中，由于模结构和坐标性质的差异，该定理的推广并非平凡，需要重新审视。

Method: 首先在 Z-分次流形上证明了局部 Frobenius 定理，即对合分布局部存在平坦坐标。随后定义了两种不同的积分子流形概念（较强与较弱），分别考察各自的全局可积性性质，通过逻辑蕴含关系分析，并构造了简单反例来验证各蕴含的逆命题。

Result: 较强的积分子流形概念下，全局 Frobenius 定理以单向蕴含成立：对合性蕴含可积性；但可积性仅蕴含较弱版本的对合性。两种概念的逆蕴含均不成立，并给出了具体反例。

Conclusion: 在 Z-分次流形上，Frobenius 定理的成立范围与经典情形有所不同：仅部分蕴含关系成立，且两种积分子流形概念的强弱差异导致可积性条件的不等价性。

Abstract: Integrability of distributions on $\mathbb{Z}$-graded manifolds is examined. First, the Local Frobenius theorem -- the local existence of flat coordinates for an involutive distribution -- is proved. Then, two different notions of an integral submanifold are discussed. It is found that, for the stronger of the two notions, the Global Frobenius theorem holds, but as one implication only: involutivity implies integrability. It is then shown that integrability implies a certain weaker version of involutivity. Simple counterexamples of the converse implications are given.

</details>


### [177] [Uniqueness of capillary Gauss solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22494)
*Xinqun Mei,Guofang Wang,Liangjun Weng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了欧氏半空间中具有锐接触角的光滑严格凸毛细高斯孤子满足刚性猜想：任何此类孤子均为球冠。结合此前毛细高斯曲率流的收敛结果，严格凸毛细超曲面在适当缩放后收敛到毛细球冠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证文献[14]中提出的刚性猜想（Conjecture 1.2），即毛细高斯孤子是否必然为球冠。这一结果是理解毛细高斯曲率流长期行为的关键环节。

Method: 通过分析光滑严格凸毛细高斯孤子的几何性质，在欧氏半空间中具有锐接触角的条件下，证明其刚性——即此类孤子只能是球冠。该结果与此前建立的毛细高斯曲率流收敛定理（[14, Theorem 1.1]）相结合。

Result: 成功证明了刚性猜想：所有在欧氏半空间中具有锐接触角的光滑严格凸毛细高斯孤子均为球冠。由此推出，从严格凸毛细超曲面出发的毛细高斯曲率流在适当缩放后收敛到毛细球冠。

Conclusion: 完成了毛细高斯曲率流收敛性的闭环证明，即通过刚性结果的建立，确保了流在缩放后的收敛极限必为球冠，从而完整解决了该流的长期行为问题。

Abstract: We prove the rigidity conjecture of [14, Conjecture 1.2] for smooth strictly convex capillary Gauss solitons in a Euclidean half-space with an acute contact angle: every such soliton is a spherical cap. Combined with our previous convergence result for the capillary Gauss curvature flow [14, Theorem 1.1], it follows that the flow starting from a strictly convex capillary hypersurface with an acute contact angle converges to a capillary spherical cap, after a suitable rescaling.

</details>


### [178] [The rigity of spaces with cyclic parallel Ricci tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21732)
*Fengyuan Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文旨在对具有循环平行Ricci张量的特殊黎曼流形进行分类，证明在非紧梯度收缩Ricci孤子、带边界的紧致(m>1)-拟爱因斯坦流形以及临界空间等结构中，Ricci张量实际上是平行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环平行Ricci张量条件($D_{ijk}=0$)是比平行Ricci张量更弱的条件，研究这类流形的分类有助于深入理解Ricci孤子、拟爱因斯坦流形和临界空间等几何结构的曲率性质。

Method: 通过构造积分恒等式，结合特定的曲率条件，利用积分方法和几何分析技巧来证明这些流形的Ricci张量是平行的。

Result: 证明了在所述几类特殊流形（非紧梯度收缩Ricci孤子、带边界的紧致(m>1)-拟爱因斯坦流形、临界空间）中，循环平行Ricci张量条件可以强化为Ricci张量平行。

Conclusion: 这些特殊黎曼流形在满足循环平行Ricci张量条件时，其Ricci张量必然为平行，从而完成了对这些流形的分类。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to classify some special Riemannian manifolds with cyclic parallel Ricci tensor, i.e.
  \begin{equation}
  D_{ijk}=\nabla_{i}R_{jk}+\nabla_{j}R_{ki}+\nabla_{k}R_{ij}=0\nonumber
  \end{equation} These structures include non-compact gradient shrinking Ricci soliton, compact $(m > 1)$-quasi-Einstein manifolds with boundary and critical spaces. We will construct some integral identities and make use of the curvature conditions reasonably to prove that the Ricci tensor is parallel.

</details>


### [179] [Ricci curvature bounds for Statistical Submersions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21760)
*Ravindra Singh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文从直接Ricci曲率的角度研究统计淹没（statistical submersion）的双侧Ricci曲率界。通过结合Chen-Ricci不等式与Hineva代数不等式，分别沿垂直分布、水平分布和混合分布推导出上/下界估计，并刻画了等式成立的条件，提供了显式例子说明等式与严格不等式情形。这是首个针对统计淹没的Hineva型下界Ricci曲率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Chen-Ricci不等式仅提供了统计淹没Ricci曲率的上界估计，缺少互补的下界估计，无法获得Ricci曲率的双侧描述。因此需要结合Hineva代数不等式来补齐这一缺口。

Method: 将统计淹没的Ricci曲率关系与Hineva代数不等式相结合：(1)沿垂直分布，利用纤维的内在Ricci曲率及基本张量T和T*，获得Chen-Ricci上界和Hineva型下界估计；(2)沿水平分布，推导涉及基本张量A和A*的Chen-Ricci不等式；(3)综合垂直与水平曲率关系，得到混合分布的Chen-Ricci和Hineva型估计；(4)以基本张量及其对偶的分量刻画等式条件。

Result: 获得了垂直分布的双侧Ricci曲率界（Chen-Ricci上界+Hineva型下界）、水平分布的Chen-Ricci不等式以及混合分布的相应估计；给出了等式成立条件的精确刻画；通过显式例子同时展示了等式与严格不等式情形。

Conclusion: 论文为统计淹没提供了一种统一的Ricci曲率方法，首次给出了统计淹没的Hineva型下界Ricci曲率估计，从而实现了Ricci曲率的双侧有界描述。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Ricci curvature bounds for statistical submersions from a direct Ricci-curvature perspective. Although Chen--Ricci inequalities for statistical submersions have already been established, a complementary lower estimate is needed to obtain a two-sided description of the Ricci curvature. Motivated by this observation, we combine the Ricci curvature relations of a statistical submersion with Hineva's algebraic inequality. We first obtain a Chen--Ricci upper estimate and a Hineva-type lower estimate along the vertical distribution, expressed in terms of the intrinsic Ricci curvature of the fibres and the fundamental tensors $T$ and $T^{*}$. The two estimates together provide upper and lower bounds for the vertical Ricci curvature. We then derive a Chen--Ricci inequality along the horizontal distribution involving the fundamental tensors $A$ and $A^{*}$. By combining the vertical and horizontal curvature relations, we further obtain corresponding Chen--Ricci and Hineva-type estimates for the mixed distribution. The equality conditions are characterized in terms of the components of the fundamental tensors and their duals. Explicit examples are given to illustrate both equality and strict inequality cases. Thus, the paper provides a unified Ricci-curvature approach to statistical submersions and, in particular, gives the first Hineva-type lower Ricci curvature estimates for statistical submersions, leading to two-sided Ricci curvature bounds.

</details>


### [180] [Pinchoff by surface diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21882)
*Glen Wheeler*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了从嵌入环面到有限时间颈缩奇性的表面扩散流，刻画了奇性剖面，证明腰径以(t-T)^{1/4}标度演化，并严格实现了Wong-Miksis-Voorhees-Davis数值发现的经典锥形自相似剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面扩散流在材料科学（如颈部烧结、热力学演化）中有重要应用，但有限时间奇性形成的严格数学分析尚不充分。尽管经典的锥形自相似剖面在数值上（Wong等）和渐近分析上（Bernoff等）已有描述，其严格证明和构造一直缺失，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者构造了从二维环面到R^3的表面扩散流。方法的关键在于：1) 设计具有特定腰结构的嵌入环面初始数据；2) 利用表面扩散流的抛物结构和转动对称性，将问题约化为径向图r(z,t)的演化方程；3) 通过严格的渐近分析证明A(t)~(4μ(T-t))^{1/4}的标度律，并证明rescaling后收敛到自相似剖面U(ζ)。

Result: 证明存在光滑嵌入环面在有限时间T内发生pinchoff奇性，在T之前保持嵌入性。奇性仅发生在单点p，其余区域光滑收敛。腰径以A(t)~{4μ(T-t)}^{1/4}的标度收缩，归一化轮廓在C^∞局部意义下收敛到函数U，该函数严格实现了文献中经典的偶锥形自相似剖面。

Conclusion: 本文首次严格构造并分析了表面扩散流在有限时间内由嵌入环面演化为颈缩奇性的动力学，证明了经典的Wong-Miksis-Voorhees-Davis锥形剖面确实是真实奇性形成的普适profile，为表面扩散流的奇性理论提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We construct surface diffusion flows $f:\mathbb{T}^2\times[0,T)\to\mathbb{R}^3$ that drive smooth closed embedded tori to pinchoff in finite time. The flow remains embedded for $t\in[0,T)$ and develops a curvature singularity only at a distinguished point $p$ as $t\nearrow T$. Away from $(p,T)$ the flow converges smoothly as $t\nearrow T$. We characterise the singularity profile: If $A(t)$ denotes the radius of the waist, and the surface is given locally near the waist by the rotation of a radial graph $(z,t)\mapsto r(z,t)$, then there exists a constant $μ>0$ and smooth function $U:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R$ such that \[
  A(t)=\{4μ(T-t)\}^{1/4}(1+o(1)),
  \qquad
  A(t)^{-1}r(A(t)ζ,t)\stackrel{C^\infty_{loc}}{\longrightarrow} U(ζ). \] Here $U$ is a rigorous realisation of the classical fundamental positive even conical similarity profile first computed numerically by Wong, Miksis, Voorhees and Davis and subsequently analysed by Bernoff, Bertozzi and Witelski.

</details>


### [181] [Complex Hyperbolic Immersions of Cheng--Yau Metrics on Thullen Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21911)
*Mirel Caibãr,Andrea Loi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明当6/7≤μ<1时，Thullen域M(μ)上的Cheng-Yau Kähler-Einstein度量（规范化Ric=-3g）经适当缩放后，可全局全纯等距浸入无穷维复双曲空间CH^∞，这是首个非齐次完备Kähler-Einstein流形具有该性质的例子，并由此反驳了Loi-Zedda的相关猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前是否存在非齐次完备Kähler-Einstein流形能等距浸入无穷维复双曲空间一直是open问题，且Loi-Zedda提出了关于此类浸入的刚性猜想（猜想4.1和更早的平Hilbert刚性猜想），需要构造反例来验证或推翻这些猜想。

Method: 选取Thullen域M(μ)（6/7≤μ<1），对其Cheng-Yau Kähler-Einstein度量进行适当缩放，构造显式的全局全纯等距浸入映射，将其浸入无穷维复双曲空间CH^∞。利用文献[DSIL2012]引理6将结果推广到平Hilbert空间ℓ²(ℂ)的Kähler浸入，并进一步应用于Sasakian流形。

Result: 证明了每个6/7≤μ<1的Thullen域上，缩放后的Cheng-Yau度量的确存在到CH^∞的全局全纯等距浸入；从而得到首个非齐次完备Kähler-Einstein流形具有此类浸入的实例，并构造出反例推翻Loi-Zedda猜想4.1（复双曲和平Hilbert两个版本的刚性猜想）以及更早的平Hilbert刚性猜想，同时导出了非齐次η-Einstein Sasakian流形到无穷维Heisenberg空间形式的Sasakian浸入。

Conclusion: Thullen域提供了反例，表明非齐次完备Kähler-Einstein流形并非都满足Loi-Zedda的刚性猜想，且该类流形也可具有到无穷维复双曲空间和平空间的Kähler浸入，并推广到Sasakian浸入情形。

Abstract: For $μ>0$, let \[ M(μ)=\left\{(z,w)\in\mathbb{C}^2 : |z|^2+|w|^{2/μ}<1\right\} \] be the Thullen domain, and let $g_{\mathrm{CY}}$ denote its complete Cheng--Yau Kähler--Einstein metric, normalized by \[ \operatorname{Ric}(g_{\mathrm{CY}})=-3g_{\mathrm{CY}}. \] We prove that, for every $6/7\leqμ<1$, a suitable rescaling of $g_{\mathrm{CY}}$ admits a global holomorphic isometric immersion into the infinite-dimensional complex hyperbolic space $\mathbb{CH}^{\infty}$. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first examples of complete nonhomogeneous Kähler--Einstein manifolds admitting such an immersion. By \cite[Lemma~6]{DSIL2012}, the same metrics also admit Kähler immersions into the flat Hilbert space $\ell^2(\mathbb{C})$. Since the Thullen domains considered here are nonhomogeneous, these examples are neither totally geodesic complex hyperbolic spaces nor products of rescaled complex hyperbolic spaces. Consequently, they provide counterexamples to \cite[Conjecture~4.1]{LoiZedda2018}, for both the complex-hyperbolic and flat Hilbert-space alternatives, and also disprove the earlier flat-Hilbert rigidity conjecture formulated in \cite[Remark~10]{LoiZedda2011}. As a further consequence, the flat realization yields complete nonhomogeneous $η$-Einstein Sasakian manifolds admitting global Sasakian immersions into the infinite-dimensional Heisenberg space form.

</details>


### [182] [Harmonic torsion of generalized nilsolitons on nilpotent Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21971)
*Huibin Chen,Zhiqi Chen,Fuhai Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了幂零李群上的左不变广义nilsoliton在优先分裂下的挠率三形式是调和的，肯定回答了Fusi-Lafuente-Stanfield在文献[3]中提出的问题7.12。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fusi-Lafuente-Stanfield在文献[3]中提出了关于左不变广义nilsoliton的挠率三形式是否为调和的问题（Question 7.12），这一问题尚未得到解决，本文旨在给出肯定回答。这一问题的解决有助于更深入理解广义几何中nilsoliton的结构与性质。

Method: 通过对幂零李群上的左不变广义nilsoliton进行精确分析，利用其优先分裂（preferred splitting）的结构性质，证明其挠率三形式满足调和条件（即同时满足闭性和余闭性）。

Result: 证明了左不变广义nilsoliton在优先分裂下的挠率三形式是调和的，从而肯定地回答了Fusi-Lafuente-Stanfield提出的Question 7.12。

Conclusion: 该结果完善了广义nilsoliton理论中的关键性质，为后续研究广义几何中的调和形式与nilsoliton的相互作用提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: We prove that the torsion three-form in the preferred splitting of a left-invariant generalized nilsoliton on a nilpotent Lie group is harmonic. This gives a positive answer to Question 7.12 proposed by Fusi-Lafuente-Stanfield in [3].

</details>


### [183] [Boundary-weighted barycenter spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22040)
*Mohameden Ahmedou,Sadok Kallel*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了边界加权重心空间（boundary-weighted barycenter spaces），其中内部支撑点权重为2、边界支撑点权重为1，作为非紧边界Euler-Lagrange泛函浓度模式的有限维拓扑模型。文章构建了混合分层、闭分层偏序集和三角边界加权余极限过滤，计算了欧拉示性数，并证明了以\(\partial M\)与\(M/\partial M\)的普通重心空间表述的同调分解，并将其专门化到带边界的紧致连通可定向曲面与半球，给出显式有理Betti多项式和（针对曲面的）模二多项式，最后解释了这些多项式如何度量紧致带边界曲面上共振Neumann平均场方程在无穷远处的拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界带cost（权重）与内部不同的重心空间是理解非紧边界Euler-Lagrange泛函（如平均场方程）浓度模式的关键工具：内部气泡携带的量子化质量是边界气泡的两倍，因此极负子水平集用边界加权重心而非普通重心建模。然而这类空间的代数拓扑性质此前缺乏系统研究，需要建立完整的拓扑不变量理论并将其与非线性PDE中的应用联系起来。

Method: 采用代数拓扑方法：构造混合分层（mixed strata）和闭分层偏序集（closed-stratum poset）；建立三角边界加权余极限过滤（triangular boundary-weighted colimit filtration）；通过余极限和分层技术计算欧拉示性数；利用同调分解定理将边界加权重心空间的高斯同调化归为\(\partial M\)和\(M/\partial M\)的普通重心空间的同调；对带边界曲面和半球专门化公式，计算有理Betti多项式和模二多项式。

Result: 得到了边界加权重心空间的完整分层结构、欧拉示性数公式和同调分解定理；对紧致连通可定向带边界曲面给出了显式的有理Betti多项式及模二多项式；对半球给出相应显式公式，为共振Neumann平均场方程在无穷远处的拓扑度量提供了具体计算工具。

Conclusion: 边界加权重心空间提供了研究带边界紧致流形上非紧变分问题（尤其共振Neumann平均场方程）拓扑的工具。所得Betti多项式刻画了浓度模式空间中无穷远处的拓扑，表明内部气泡与边界气泡的质量权重差异（2:1）在拓扑层面有精确反映。

Abstract: We study boundary-weighted barycenter spaces, in which an interior support point has cost (or weight) two and a boundary support point has cost one. These spaces are finite-dimensional topological models for the concentration patterns produced by noncompact boundary Euler--Lagrange functionals: an interior bubble carries twice the quantized mass of a boundary bubble, and very negative sublevels are therefore modeled by boundary-weighted rather than ordinary barycenters. The paper gives a systematic algebraic-topological treatment of these spaces. We construct the mixed strata, the closed-stratum poset, and the triangular boundary-weighted colimit filtration; compute the Euler characteristic; and prove a homology decomposition in terms of ordinary barycenter spaces of \(\partial M\) and \(M/\partial M\). We then specialize the formulas to compact connected orientable surfaces with boundary and to hemispheres, obtaining explicit rational Betti polynomials and, for surfaces, the mod-two polynomials required by the mean-field application. Finally, we explain how these polynomials measure the topology at infinity in the resonant Neumann mean-field equation on a compact surface with boundary.

</details>


### [184] [A Metric with Positive Sectional Curvature on $S^2\times S^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22133)
*Shengtao Guo,Ethan X. Fang,Junwei Lu*

Main category: math.DG
