TL;DR: 本文通过奥丁自动AI研究代理发现了$S^2\times S^3$上具有正截面曲率的黎曼度量。作者将其视为$S^2\times S^2$上第一陈类为$(1,1)$的主$S^1$丛，通过小对角Cheeger形变构建非负曲率的连接度量，再利用全局水平复对称2-张量的旋转产生正的二阶项，经各向异性法Hessian估计和有限维约化控制所有邻近平面，最终通过足够小的扰动实现正截面曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明$S^2\times S^3$是否允许正截面曲率的黎曼度量是黎曼几何中长期未解决的问题。该问题属于正截面曲率流形分类的核心难题，此前对该空间是否存在正曲率度量一直悬而未决。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 将$S^2\times S^3$视为$S^2\times S^2$上第一陈类为$(1,1)$的主$S^1$丛；(2) 对基空间进行小对角Cheeger形变获得非负曲率；(3) 构造合适的连接度量，其零曲率平面构成Grassmann丛中干净的紧致子流形；(4) 构造全局水平复对称$S^1$-权2的2-张量，其在每个零平面上的限制是某个非零复零余向量的平方，通过沿纤维旋转产生Gauss方程中的正二阶项；(5) 利用各向异性法Hessian估计和有限维约化控制所有邻近平面，进行足够小的扰动获得正截面曲率。整个度量和证明由Odin自动AI研究代理发现。

Result: 成功证明了$S^2\times S^3$上存在具有正截面曲率的黎曼度量，解决了该空间正曲率度量的存在性问题。

Conclusion: 这项工作不仅给出了$S^2\times S^3$上正截面曲率度量的构造性证明，还展示了AI自动研究代理在发现复杂几何构造中的潜力，为其他闭流形上正曲率度量的构造提供了新的思路和工具。

Abstract: We prove that $S^2\times S^3$ admits a Riemannian metric with positive sectional curvature. We regard it as the principal $S^1$-bundle with first Chern class $(1,1)$ over $S^2\times S^2$ and begin with a small diagonal Cheeger deformation of the base. An adapted connection gives a nonnegatively curved connection metric whose zero-curvature planes form a clean compact submanifold of the Grassmann bundle. We then construct a global horizontal complex symmetric $2$-tensor of $S^1$-weight two whose restriction to every zero plane is the square of a nonzero complex null covector. Its rotation along the fibers produces a positive second-order term in the Gauss equation. An anisotropic normal Hessian estimate and a finite-dimensional reduction control all nearby planes, so a sufficiently small perturbation has positive sectional curvature. The metric and the proof are discovered by the Odin Automatic AI Research Agent.

</details>


### [185] [A note on the equality case of Bray's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22182)
*Xu-Qian Fan,Chengjie Yu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇短注，刻画了由Jiang-Li-Wang近期证明的Bray猜想中的等号情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bray猜想已被Jiang-Li-Wang证明，但原证明可能未完全刻画等式成立的条件，本文旨在补充并完整描述等号情形的刻画。

Method: 基于Jiang-Li-Wang的证明方法，进一步分析等式成立的必要和充分条件，给出完整的等号情形刻画。

Result: 给出了Bray猜想中等号情形的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 本文补充了Jiang-Li-Wang工作的等号情形的细节，完善了Bray猜想的结果。

Abstract: In this short note, we characterize the equality case of Bray's conjecture recently proved by Jiang-Li-Wang \cite{JLW}.

</details>


### [186] [Stable constant weighted mean curvature hypersurfaces in anti-Gaussian space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22225)
*Jinchuan Bai,Chao Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在反高斯空间（m≥2维）中，以原点为中心的球面是唯一具有常加权平均曲率且在加权体积保持变分下稳定的闭连通双侧浸入超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究反高斯空间中常加权平均曲率超曲面的稳定性分类问题，这是高斯空间相关几何分析的对称/对偶版本，旨在刻画该空间中最优的闭超曲面几何形状。

Method: 采用加权几何（weighted geometry）框架，通过加权体积保持变分的稳定性分析，结合反高斯空间的具体度量与加权函数结构，证明稳定性条件强加足以排除除原点为中心球面之外的所有候选超曲面。

Result: 在m≥2的反高斯空间中，封闭连通双侧浸入常加权平均曲率超曲面的稳定性唯一地刻画为以原点为中心的圆球面。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了反高斯空间中常加权平均曲率超曲面稳定性的完整分类，扩展了经典加权几何中关于高斯空间中常平均曲率超曲面的稳定性定理，为反高斯空间（Wulff流形/半群上的加权几何）提供了类似的刚性刻画。

Abstract: We prove that in the anti-Gaussian space $\bigl(\R^{m+1},\bar g_{\Euc},e^{|x|^2/4}\,dx\bigr)$, $m\ge2$, round spheres centered at the origin are the only closed, connected, two-sided immersed hypersurfaces with constant weighted mean curvature that are stable under weighted-volume-preserving variations.

</details>


### [187] [Componentwise Geometry and Monodromy of Generalized Lamé Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22243)
*Ting-Jung Kuo,Xuanpu Liang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文针对广义Lamé方程建立了逐分量的几何与单值理论，证明非偶分量谱曲线与经典Lamé谱曲线自然同构，从而每个非偶广义Lamé方程都单值等价于同一椭圆曲线上的唯一经典Lamé方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Lamé方程在椭圆曲线上具有奇点0和±p，其几何结构与经典Lamé方程的关系尚未被系统理解。作者旨在为其非偶分量建立完整的谱几何理论，并通过与经典Lamé方程的显式对应揭示其深层结构。

Method: 分解方程的log-free locus为偶与非偶两个不可约分量；基于偶分量的谱理论构造非偶分量的超椭圆谱曲线、Baker-Akhiezer函数及加法映射；证明度degσ_{n,p}^{(1)}=n(n+1)；通过对合T→-T取商，建立非偶谱曲线与经典Lamé谱曲线的自然同构，并给出显式对应关系B̃=T²-n(n+1)℘(p)。

Result: 证明了非偶分量谱曲线在取商后与经典Lamé谱曲线自然同构，且与加法映射和有理函数κ相容。每个非偶广义Lamé方程通过显式公式单值等价于唯一经典Lamé方程；固定B̃作为p变化时得到等单值族，并将谱几何、有限带结构、有限单值理论及平坦环面上的曲率方程全部转移到非偶分量。

Conclusion: 该对应关系为广义Lamé方程提供了逐分量的解释，结合偶分量的Painlevé VI变形，完整刻画了广义Lamé退化过程，建立了偶与非偶两个分量各自的谱几何框架及其相互联系。

Abstract: We develop a componentwise geometric and monodromy theory for the generalized Lamé equation on an elliptic curve with singularities at $0$ and $\pm p$. Its log-free locus decomposes canonically into two irreducible components, corresponding to the even and non-even symmetries of the potential.
  Building on the spectral theory of the even component, we construct the hyperelliptic spectral curve, Baker--Akhiezer functions, and addition map of the non-even component, and prove that \[
  \degσ_{n,p}^{(1)}=n(n+1).
  \]
  After quotienting by the involution $T\mapsto -T$, we show that the non-even spectral curve is naturally isomorphic to the classical Lamé spectral curve, compatibly with the addition map and the rational function $κ$. Consequently, every non-even generalized Lamé equation is monodromy equivalent to a unique classical Lamé equation on the same elliptic curve, via the explicit correspondence \[ \widetilde B=T^2-n(n+1)\wp(p). \]
  Fixing $\widetilde B$ yields an isomonodromic family as $p$ varies. The correspondence transfers the spectral geometry, finite-gap structure, and finite-monodromy theories, together with the curvature equations on flat tori, to the non-even component. Combining with the Painlevé VI deformation of the even component, we give a componentwise interpretation of the generalized Lamé degeneration.

</details>


### [188] [Stable Minimal Hypersurfaces in Positively Curved $4$-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22261)
*Han Hong,Gaoming Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维流形完全稳定极小浸入在四维流形中的刚性定理，证明当环境截曲率非负且数量曲率有正一致下界时，该极小曲面必须为全测地且法向Ricci曲率消失；同时构造了R^4上严格正截曲率度量下非全测地的反例，说明正曲率条件下刚性结论不再成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Schoen-Yau型刚性定理通常依赖弱有界几何假设或曲率上界限制。作者旨在取消这些额外条件，在更一般的完备非紧背景下建立稳定极小超曲面的刚性定理，同时探讨正截曲率环境下刚性结论的极限，为理解正曲率空间中极小超曲面的分类问题提供新视角。

Method: 证明部分采用三种核心工具的结合：(1)谱分裂理论分析极小超曲面的稳定性算子谱行为；(2)翘曲μ-泡构造以产生具有特定曲率性质的边界及区域；(3)调和函数水平集论证来刻画曲面结构与度量性质。反例构造通过对文献[CLS]中例子的紧致支撑形变实现。

Result: 得到两个主要结果：(1)刚性定理：完备连通双侧稳定极小浸入在非负截曲率且数量曲率具有正一致下界的环境中必为全测地超曲面且法向Ricci曲率为零，该结论不依赖弱有界几何假设和曲率上界；(2)反例：在R^4上构造了严格正截曲率完备度量，其上存在完备、嵌入、单端、非抛物、双侧稳定极小超曲面微分同胚于R^3但非全测地，表明刚性定理条件下不能去除或替代特定假设。

Conclusion: 本文在去掉弱有界几何和曲率上界假设后建立了更普适的刚性定理，同时通过精心构造的反例揭示了刚性结论的边界条件，证明正截曲率环境并不能保证极小超曲面的全测地性，为完备非紧正曲率空间中极小子流形的分类理论提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Let $M^3\to X^4$ be a complete, connected, two-sided stable minimal immersion. We prove that if the ambient sectional curvature is nonnegative and the ambient scalar curvature has a positive uniform lower bound, then $M$ is totally geodesic and its normal Ricci curvature vanishes. No weak bounded geometry assumption and no upper curvature bound are imposed. We also construct a complete metric of strictly positive sectional curvature on $\mathbb{R}^4$ admitting a complete, embedded, one-ended, nonparabolic, two-sided stable minimal hypersurface diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{R}^3$ which is not totally geodesic. The rigidity proof combines spectral splitting theory, a warped $μ$-bubble construction, and a harmonic function level set argument. The example is obtained by a compactly supported deformation of an example in \cite{CLS}.

</details>


### [189] [On the Rigidity of Closed CMC Hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{S}^5(1)$ with Constant Scalar Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22311)
*Qintao Deng,Yunjia Kou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究单位球面中具有常数量曲率和常三次幂和f_3的闭CMC超曲面M^4。证明若在某点恰有两个不同主曲率，则必为等参超曲面（Clifford环面）。在附加Willmore条件下，得到完整分类：所有具有常数量曲率的闭CMC Willmore超曲面均为等参的，具体包含全脐测地球面、极小与非极小Clifford环面以及Cartan极小超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面中CMC超曲面的等参分类是微分几何中的重要问题。已有的等参结果通常需要主曲率数量或符号条件限制，本文旨在在更一般的条件下（不假设数量曲率符号）推广分类结果，特别是结合Willmore条件获得完整刻画。

Method: 利用无迹局部张量恒等式、主曲率重数的代数分析，以及加权微分3-形式结合在主曲率重合集合附近的截断论证。通过分析f_3=常数与常数量曲率条件导出代数约束，从而限制主曲率结构。

Result: (1) 若M^4在某点恰有两个不同主曲率，则其为Clifford环面S^1(r)×S^3或S^2(r)×S^2；(2) 在Willmore条件下，所有闭CMC常数量曲率超曲面均为等参，完整分类为：全脐测地球面、极小Clifford环面S^2(1/√2)×S^2(1/√2)、非极小Clifford环面S^1(√3/2)×S^3(1/2)、或Cartan极小超曲面。

Conclusion: 在4维情形，Willmore条件与常数量曲率条件共同刻画了闭CMC超曲面的等参结构，无需数量曲率符号假设，从而推广了已有分类结果。所用方法（无迹张量恒等式、代数重数分析、加权3-形式与截断论证）可为高维或更一般情形的类似问题提供框架。

Abstract: Let $M^4\hookrightarrow\mathbb S^5(1)$ be a closed CMC hypersurface with constant scalar curvature and constant third power sum $f_3=\sum_{i,j,k}h_{ij}h_{jk}h_{ki}$. We prove that if $M^4$ has exactly two distinct principal curvatures at some point, then it is isoparametric. More precisely, it is a Clifford torus of the form $\mathbb S^1(r)\times\mathbb S^3(\sqrt{1-r^2})$ or $\mathbb S^2(r)\times\mathbb S^2(\sqrt{1-r^2})$, where $0<r<1$. Under the additional Willmore condition, we obtain a complete classification: every closed CMC Willmore hypersurface $M^4\hookrightarrow\mathbb S^5(1)$ with constant scalar curvature is isoparametric. Consequently, it is congruent to a totally umbilic geodesic sphere, the minimal Clifford torus $\mathbb S^2(1/\sqrt2)\times\mathbb S^2(1/\sqrt2)$, the nonminimal Clifford torus $\mathbb S^1(\sqrt3/2)\times\mathbb S^3(1/2)$, or a Cartan minimal hypersurface. The proofs combine trace-free local tensor identities, an algebraic analysis of the possible principal curvature multiplicities, and a weighted differential $3$-form together with a cut-off argument near the set where principal curvatures coalesce. No sign condition on the scalar curvature is imposed.

</details>


### [190] [Weighted decomposition of vector fields, $X$-ADM mass, and higher-dimensional mass-charge inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22395)
*Francesca Oronzio*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意维度n≥3的完备单端渐近平坦黎曼流形上的X-ADM质量。通过加权梯度-散度自由分解构造共形度量，建立了X-ADM质量与共形度量ADM质量之间的精确分解，包含一个非负缺陷项，实现正性定理的分解证明与完整性刚性刻画。并推广到多矢量场系统，得到高维多电荷质量不等式，以及三维电-磁不等式的高维类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于类空超曲面引力的物理动机，X-ADM质量（由任意矢量场X定义的推广ADM质量）的正性与分解结构尚未完全理解，尤其是在任意维度下。作者希望建立一种分解方法，将X-ADM质量分解为标准ADM质量加非负项，从而给出更直接的正性证明与刚性刻画，并将此理论推广到多矢量场系统以处理电-磁质量不等式的高维情形。

Method: (1) 对矢量场X进行加权梯度-散度自由分解，构造一个共形度量，其标量曲率由临界修正标量曲率R_X^{(1-n)}控制；(2) 利用该共形度量得到X-ADM质量的精确分解：X-ADM质量 = 共形度量的ADM质量 + 非负缺陷项；(3) 通过显式反例阐明修正标量曲率R_X^{(k)}中参数k的取值范围的锐性；(4) 将X正质量定理应用于多矢量场系统，推导高维多电荷质量不等式；(5) 对磁数据β（高维时为2-形式）引入张量磁通量，证明当dβ∈L^1时该通量消失，并在临界渐近假设下构造非平凡张量磁荷，归约为X正质量定理。

Result: 获得了X-ADM质量的分解公式，给出分解型正性证明及完整刚性（当且仅当X为梯度时缺陷项为零）；通过显式负质量例子证明参数k范围的锐性；得到多矢量场系统的高维多电荷质量不等式，在等号情形下具有刚性和全局对齐；恢复经典三维电-磁不等式及其高维类比；证明特定张量通量在dβ∈L^1时消失，并在临界渐近假设下定义非平凡张量磁荷。

Conclusion: X-ADM质量正性定理可以通过共形分解方法给出统一的证明，且该方法自然处理刚性情形。多矢量场系统推广为高维电-磁质量不等式提供了理论框架，其中高维磁荷需通过张量通量来定义，并可在临界渐近条件下归约为X正质量定理。

Abstract: We study the $X$-ADM mass on complete, one-ended asymptotically flat Riemannian manifolds without boundary in arbitrary dimension $n\geqslant 3$. Starting from a weighted gradient--divergence-free decomposition of the vector field $X$, we construct a conformal metric whose scalar curvature is governed by the critical modified scalar curvature $\mathrm{R}_{X}^{(1-n)}$ associated with $X$. This yields an exact decomposition of the $X$-ADM mass into the ADM mass of the conformal metric plus a nonnegative defect term, which vanishes precisely when $X$ is a gradient, thereby giving an alternative decomposition-based proof of positivity with a full rigidity statement. Explicit negative-mass examples show that the range of parameters $k$ in the modified scalar curvature $\mathrm{R}_{X}^{(k)}$ condition is sharp. We then apply the $X$-positive mass theorem to systems of vector fields, obtaining higher-dimensional multi-charge mass inequalities with rigidity and global alignment in the equality case. This yields the classical three-dimensional electric-magnetic inequality and its higher-dimensional analogue. In higher dimensions, the magnetic datum $β$ is a $2$-form and does not have a canonical scalar charge on the asymptotically flat end. We prove that a selected tensorial flux vanishes when $dβ\in L^1$. Under a prescribed critical asymptotic ansatz, this flux defines a non-trivial tensorial magnetic charge and we formulate a conditional reduction to the $X$-positive mass theorem.

</details>


### [191] [On ideal lemniscates, butterflies, and circular waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22396)
*Shinya Okabe,Glen Wheeler*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了长度惩罚理想能量（length-penalised ideal energy）存在无穷多个类双纽线（lemniscate-like）、类蝴蝶（butterfly-like）和圆波（circular-wave）临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究长度惩罚理想能量的临界点结构，特别是寻找具有不同几何形态（双纽线、蝴蝶、圆波）的临界点，以理解该能量泛函的丰富解空间。

Method: 采用两种方法：(1) 对双纽线和蝴蝶族使用分阶段直接极小化过程（staged direct minimisation）；(2) 对圆波族使用边界层分析和自适应打靶法（adapted shooting method）。边界层分析揭示了临界点位于两个Fresnel相位附近：3π/4模2π对应双纽线，7π/4模2π对应蝴蝶。圆波族从多重覆盖圆（multiply-covered circles）意义上分叉而来。

Result: 成功证明了长度惩罚理想能量存在无穷多个临界点，包括双纽线型、蝴蝶型和圆波型三类；确定了双纽线和蝴蝶分别对应的Fresnel相位，并建立了圆波族从多重覆盖圆分叉的存在性。

Conclusion: 该能量泛函具有极为丰富的临界点结构，不同类型临界点分别对应不同的Fresnel相位，且可通过直接极小化和打靶法等不同手段加以构造。

Abstract: We prove the existence of infinitely many lemniscate-like, butterfly-like, and circular-wave critical points for the length-penalised ideal energy. For the lemniscate and butterfly family, we use a staged direct minimisation process to establish existence. A boundary-layer analysis reveals critical points near two Fresnel phases: $3π/4$ modulo $2π$, corresponding to lemniscates, and $7π/4$ modulo $2π$, which are the butterflies. The family of circular waves bifurcates, in a sense, from multiply-covered circles. We use an adapted shooting method to establish their existence.

</details>


### [192] [Scalar curvature of blow-ups of compact Kähler manifolds along complex submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22410)
*Zeqing Miao,Bo Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在复维数至少3的紧致Kähler流形中，沿余维数至少2的复子流形做blow-up后，所得流形上存在一列Kähler度量，其数量曲率按C^0范数收敛到原度量数量曲率的拉回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brown近期建立了blow-up于一点情形下Kähler度量数量曲率收敛的结果。本文将这一结果推广到沿更高余维子流形的blow-up情形，因为子流形情形在几何与分析上更具挑战性，构造更复杂。

Method: 证明基于构造极值Kähler度量的粘合方法（gluing method），并引入新的分析工具以及对现有方法的多处修改，以处理沿余维数至少2的复子流形blow-up时出现的额外技术困难。

Result: 在复维数至少3、子流形余维数至少2的条件下，blow-up流形上存在一列Kähler度量，其数量曲率在C^0范数意义下收敛到原度量数量曲率经由blow-up映射的拉回。

Conclusion: 该结果将Brown的点blow-up结果推广至子流形blow-up情形，为blow-up上Kähler度量的构造与分析提供了更广泛的工具和理论支持。

Abstract: Let $(M,ω)$ be a compact Kähler manifold with its scalar curvature $S(ω)$, Assume that $M$ has complex dimension at least $3$ and contains a complex submanifold $X$ of complex codimension at least $2$. Let $σ: Bl_{X} M \rightarrow M$ denote the blow-up of $M$ along $X$. We show that $Bl_{X} M$ admits a sequence of Kähler metrics $\{\widetildeω_{i}\}_{i \geq 1}$ whose scalar curvatures $S(\widetildeω_i)$ converge to $σ^{\ast} (S(ω))$ in the $C^0(Bl_{X} M)$ norm. Our work is motivated by a recent result of Brown, who established the corresponding result for blow-ups at a point. The proof is based on the gluing method for constructing extremal Kähler metrics on blow-ups, together with new analytic tools and several modifications needed in our setting.

</details>


### [193] [Ricci-flat string algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22455)
*Agnaldo A. da Silva,Henrique N. Sá Earp,Bernardo S. Vieira*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究由4-形式定义的几何结构，统一涵盖带有挠率的几乎Hermitian、G₂、Spin(7)及几乎四元/超Hermitian等几何。在此框架下，4-形式自然地确定出流形上的弦代数胚（string algebroid）几何数据，并引出规范瞬子的规范概念，从而将这些几何与广义几何及异质弦论中的规范理论结构联系起来。文中进一步证明这些数据诱导广义Ricci平坦度量，并考察了文献中的主要例子，构造了若干显式实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同类型的带挠几何结构（如近Hermitian、G₂、Spin(7)、四元/超Hermitian等）长期被分别研究，缺乏统一的框架来理解它们之间的内在联系及其与弦论中代数胚、瞬子和广义Ricci平坦性的关联。

Method: 以4-形式为统一出发点，证明其自然确定弦代数胚结构，并利用该结构定义瞬子概念；随后证明这些数据诱导广义Ricci平坦度量，最后通过文献中的主要例子和显式构造加以验证。

Result: 建立了由4-形式统一描述的带挠几何框架，证明了该框架与弦代数胚、瞬子及广义Ricci平坦度量之间的内在联系，并给出了若干显式例子。

Conclusion: 4-形式为理解多种带挠几何结构及其在弦论和广义几何中的作用提供了统一且自然的框架，为后续研究瞬子与广义Ricci平坦度量提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate geometric structures defined by 4-forms, encompassing important classes of geometric structures with torsion including almost Hermitian, $\mathrm{G}_2$, $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$, and almost quaternion/hyper Hermitian. Within this unified framework, the underlying 4-form naturally determines geometric data that define a string algebroid over the manifold. In particular, it gives rise to a canonical notion of instanton, thereby linking these geometries to gauge-theoretic structures in generalized geometry and heterotic string theory. We further prove that these data induce generalized Ricci-flat metrics. Finally, we examine the main examples in the literature and construct several explicit ones.

</details>


### [194] [Wishart Matrices and Quantum Geometry: Foundations and Applications in Quantum Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22604)
*Noémie C. Combe*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将Wishart矩阵嵌入Monge-Ampere几何中研究，并证明所有Wishart分布构成一个对称幺半范畴，其张量积编码块对角耦合，braiding由块置换给出，公理保证了Monge-Ampere函子性、可加性和凸对偶性。该框架应用于量子纠错领域，用于建模相关噪声、优化纠错代价、捕捉独立误差信道以及支撑熵驱动的解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wishart矩阵作为多元高斯数据协方差结构的核心模型，其性质已被广泛研究，但缺乏一个统一的数学框架将几何、代数范畴论和实际应用整合起来。特别是将Wishart分布嵌入微分几何（Monge-Ampere几何）以及用范畴论语言刻画其结构性质，可以为量子信息等交叉领域提供新视角。

Method: 本文将正定锥嵌入Monge-Ampere几何，使其获得Hessian流形结构，具有仿射不变度量与体积形式。在此几何下，Wishart密度被视为自然几何流的孤子解。随后构造对称幺半范畴（W），对象为Wishart分布，态射为线性映射，张量积编码块对角耦合，braiding由块置换实现，并通过Legendre变换建立凸对偶关系。

Result: 成功建立了Wishart分布的对称幺半范畴结构，验证了Monge-Ampere函子性、可加性和凸对偶性等公理。在量子纠错应用中，Wishart律用于建模相关噪声，Wasserstein测地线优化纠错代价，张量结构捕捉独立误差信道，Legendre对偶支撑熵驱动解码。

Conclusion: 本文的框架为Wishart矩阵理论提供了统一的几何-范畴论视角，不仅加深了对Wishart分布内在结构的理解，还为量子纠错中的噪声建模、代价优化和解码策略提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We present a unified framework for the study of Wishart matrices (W_p(n,Σ)), which generalize the chi-squared distribution to matrix-variate settings and model the covariance structure of multivariate Gaussian data. After recalling their defining properties - additivity under independent summation (W_1 + W_2 \sim W_p(n_1+n_2,Σ)), equivariance under linear maps (A W A^T \sim W_q(n,AΣA^T)), and their role as sample covariance matrices - we embed the positive-definite cone (S_p^+) within Monge-Ampere geometry. Here (S_p^+) acquires a Hessian manifold structure with affine-invariant metric and volume form (ω= \det(Σ)^{-(p+1)/2},dΣ), under which the Wishart density acts as a soliton of natural geometric flows. We then show that the collection of Wishart distributions forms a symmetric monoidal category (\mathcal{W}), whose objects are (W_p(n,Σ)) and whose morphisms are linear maps (A:\mathbb{R}^p\to\mathbb{R}^q). The tensor product encodes block-diagonal coupling, with braiding given by block permutation, and the axioms enforce Monge-Ampere functoriality, additivity, and convex duality via the Legendre transform. Applications to quantum error correction are discussed: Wishart laws model correlated noise, Wasserstein geodesics optimize error-mitigation cost, tensor structure captures independent error channels, and Legendre duality underpins entropy-driven decoding.

</details>


### [195] [Semistable $J$-equation on Kähler threefold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22674)
*Junbang Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究三维紧致Kähler流形上的J-流，证明了当Kähler类对(α,β)是J-半稳定时，其J-零轨迹构成X的一个子簇，并给出了基于具有解析奇点的Kähler流形的解析刻画。作为应用，证明了J-流在J-零轨迹之外的光滑收敛性，从而为半稳定J-方程弱解提供了更高阶的部分正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J-流是Kähler几何中一类重要的抛物几何流，其收敛性与稳定性条件密切相关。对于J-半稳定的情形，需要深入理解J-零轨迹的结构及其几何性质，以建立流在奇点以外的收敛性，并提升已有弱解的正则性结果。

Method: 利用具有解析奇点的Kähler流形理论，类比Collins-Tosatti定理，对J-零轨迹给出解析刻画；在此基础上，证明J-流在J-零轨迹补集上的光滑收敛性，进而获得半稳定J-方程弱解的高阶部分正则性。

Result: 证明了三维紧致Kähler流形上J-半稳定条件蕴涵J-零轨迹为X的子簇；给出了不依赖于代数几何的解析刻画；实现了J-流在J-零轨迹外的光滑收敛，为半稳定J-方程弱解建立了高阶部分正则性。

Conclusion: 该工作将J-流理论从代数几何框架拓展到解析框架，深化了对半稳定情形下J-方程解的正则性理解，为进一步研究高维与一般情形的J-流动力学奠定了基础。

Abstract: For a $3$-dimensional compact Kähler manifold $X$ with a pair of Kähler class $(α,β)$ which is $J$-semistable, we show that the $J$-null locus of $(α,β)$ forms a subvariety of $X$. We also give an analyic characterization of the $J$-null locus using Kähler currents with analytic singularities analogous to the theorem of Collins-Tosatti \cite{CT15}. As an application, we show the smooth convergence of the $J$-flow off the $J$-null locus. It gives higer order partial regularity for weak solutions of semistable $J$-equations obtained in \cite{M26b}.

</details>


### [196] [Height functions on singular surfaces parameterized by smooth maps $\mathcal{A}$-equivalent to $H_k$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22712)
*Masaru Hasegawa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由与Mond分类中H_k型映射胚A-等价的C^∞映射芽参数化的R^3中奇异曲面上的高度函数的奇点，以及高度函数族的versal性，并进一步探讨了这些高度函数奇点与奇异曲面抛物轨迹（parabolic locus）之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异曲面的高度函数在微分几何和奇点理论中具有重要地位，但此前对于由A-等价的H_k型奇异映射芽参数化的曲面上高度函数的奇点结构及其与抛物轨迹的关系尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 利用Mond关于奇异映射芽的A-等价分类理论，考察H_k型奇异曲面的高度函数族，分析其奇点的类型与结构，研究该高度函数族的versal性，并通过奇点理论工具建立与曲面上抛物轨迹的联系。

Result: 系统地刻画了H_k型奇异曲面上高度函数的奇点类型，确定了高度函数族的versal性条件，并揭示了高度函数奇点与曲面抛物轨迹之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文为H_k型奇异曲面的高度函数奇点分析提供了完整理论框架，深化了对奇异曲面几何结构的理解，并为后续相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study singularities of height functions on singular surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$ parameterized by smooth map-germs $\mathcal{A}$-equivalent to $H_k$ in Mond's classification, and the versality of the family of the height functions. We also study relations of the singularities of the height functions with the parabolic locus of these singular surfaces.

</details>


### [197] [Transforms of holomorphic maps from flag manifolds into Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22886)
*Oscar Macia,Yasuyuki Nagatomo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文基于广义do Carmo-Wallach理论，构建了旗流形到Grassmannians和二次曲面的全纯映射的函子框架，通过Penrose型变换建立了满足规范条件的完全全纯映射之间的函子，并完整刻画了Einstein-Hermitian映射的模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于旗流形全纯等距嵌入的研究缺乏统一的范畴化框架，且目标流形从射影空间推广到Grassmannians和二次曲面时，模空间的几何结构尚不清晰。作者旨在通过函子化的Penrose变换统一并推广do Carmo-Wallach理论。

Method: 基于广义do Carmo-Wallach理论，利用直接像层及其逆构造Penrose型变换，使定义域和靶空间均可变化；这些变换为满足半正齐次向量丛规范条件的完全全纯映射之间的函子。利用Einstein-Hermitian映射在变换下的稳定性，通过模空间的识别（Grassmannian目标对应r元非正整数组模对称，二次曲面目标获得完整几何描述）来刻画模结构。

Result: 对Grassmannian目标，旗流形上Einstein-Hermitian映射的模空间被识别为r元非正整数组的对称模类；对二次曲面目标，给出模空间的完整几何描述，其中心对应射影空间中的Einstein-Hermitian映射，变换塔将所有中间模空间与底层的全纯等距嵌入模空间统一起来。

Conclusion: 研究提供了旗流形全纯等距嵌入的统一范畴与几何描述，确立了Einstein-Hermitian映射作为变换下自然子范畴的地位，并保持均曲率算子L²范数的最小性。

Abstract: Building on the generalized do Carmo-Wallach theory, we construct a functorial framework for holomorphic maps from flag manifolds into Grassmannians and quadrics. Through the direct image sheaf and its inverse, we construct Penrose-type transforms that allow both the domain and target to vary. These transforms provide functors between categories of full holomorphic maps satisfying the gauge condition for semi-positive homogeneous bundles. Within this framework, Einstein-Hermitian holomorphic maps form a natural subcategory, stable under all transforms, and the minimality of L^2-norm of the mean curvature operator is preserved.
  For Grassmannian targets, the moduli of Einstein-Hermitian maps from a flag manifold are identified with r-tuples of non- positive integers modulo symmetry, where r is the rank of the group of holomorphic isometries. For quadric targets, we obtain a complete geometric description of the moduli space. The center of this moduli space corresponds to the Einstein-Hermitian map into the projective space, and the tower of transforms identifies all intermediate moduli spaces with the moduli of holomorphic isometric embeddings at the bottom level. The results yield a unified categorical and geometric description of holomorphic isometric embeddings of flag manifolds.

</details>


### [198] [Bounded Harmonic Functions on Products with a Parabolic Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22942)
*Ruotong Jia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了：若 M 是连通完备的抛物黎曼流形，N 是连通完备的随机完备黎曼流形，则 M×N 上的任何有界调和函数都独立于 M 变量。等价地，第二投影的拉回诱导从 N 上有界调和函数空间到 M×N 上有界调和函数空间的等距同构。由此推出两个抛物流形的乘积具有有界 Liouville 性质，肯定地回答了 Grigor'yan 综述中的问题 16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界 Liouville 性质（即所有有界调和函数均为常数）是黎曼流形上调和分析的核心问题之一。Grigor'yan 在其综述中提出问题 16，即两个抛物流形的乘积是否具有有界 Liouville 性质。本文旨在回答该问题。

Method: 关键分析工具是抛物流形上热核的全变差"记忆丢失"性质。作者通过证明时间一热核定义了一个非周期的 Harris 递归转移核，然后应用 Jamison 和 Orey 的行合并（row-merging）定理来建立该性质，最终证明乘积流形上有界调和函数与 M 变量无关。

Result: 若 M 为抛物流形、N 为随机完备流形，则 M×N 上的有界调和函数仅依赖于 N 变量；特别地，两个抛物流形的乘积具有有界 Liouville 性质，肯定地回答了 Grigor'yan 的问题 16。

Conclusion: 本文通过热核的遍历性质（Harris 递归性与行合并定理）建立了抛物流形上热核的全变差记忆丢失特性，从而解决了乘积流形上有界调和函数的分类问题，完善了抛物流形与随机完备流形乘积的调和函数理论。

Abstract: We prove that if $M$ is a connected complete parabolic Riemannian manifold and $N$ is a connected complete stochastically complete Riemannian manifold, then every bounded harmonic function on $M\times N$ is independent of the $M$-variable. Equivalently, pullback by the second projection induces an isometric isomorphism from the space of bounded harmonic functions on $N$ onto that on $M\times N$. In particular, the product of two parabolic manifolds has the bounded Liouville property, thereby answering Problem~16 of Grigor'yan's survey in the affirmative. The key analytic input is a total-variation memory-loss property of the heat kernel on a parabolic manifold. We establish this property by showing that the time-one heat kernel defines an aperiodic Harris recurrent transition kernel and then applying the row-merging theorem of Jamison and Orey.

</details>


### [199] [Positive Mass Theorem with Arbitrary Ends and Noncompact Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22997)
*Caiyan Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有非紧边界、一个渐近平坦半空间端以及有限多个无预定渐近性的额外端的完备黎曼流形的正质量定理，并给出了等号刻画及尖端的黎曼Penrose不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论和几何分析中，正质量定理是核心结果之一。此前的工作大多针对无边界或紧边界的流形，对于具有非紧边界的流形（如半空间模型），尤其是带有多个额外端的更一般情形，缺乏完整的质量正性及等号刻画结果。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明策略结合了多重技术：在指定端附近进行密度变形（density deformation）、沿非紧边界做对折（doubling）、局部光滑化（local smoothing）以及共形修正（conformal correction）。通过这些步骤将问题归约为已有正质量定理可处理的情形。对于Penrose不等式的证明，则利用紧致最外极小超曲面将指定端与其余端分离，并构造相应的外部区域进行比对。

Result: 在3≤n≤7、数量曲率R_g≥0且边界平均曲率H_{∂M}≥0的条件下，质量m(M,g,ℰ)≥0；等号当且仅当(M,g)等距于欧氏半空间。进一步得到尖端的黎曼Penrose不等式，其等号情形恰为Schwarzschild半空间外部区域。

Conclusion: 本文建立了具有非紧边界和多端结构的完备黎曼流形的正质量定理及其等号刚性结果，并推广了对应的Penrose不等式，为广义相对论中具有边界的孤立系统提供了更一般的几何约束。

Abstract: We prove a positive mass theorem for complete Riemannian manifolds with noncompact boundary, a distinguished asymptotically flat half-space end, and finitely many additional complete ends with no prescribed asymptotics. If $3\leq n\leq7$, $R_g\geq0$, and $H_{\partial M}\geq0$, then $ \mathfrak m(M,g,\mathcal E)\geq0$. Moreover, equality holds if and only if $(M,g)$ is isometric to the Euclidean half-space. The proof combines a density deformation near the distinguished end with doubling across the noncompact boundary, local smoothing, and a conformal correction. We also obtain the sharp Riemannian Penrose inequality when a compact outermost minimal hypersurface separates $\mathcal E$ from all the remaining ends; equality holds precisely when the exterior region is a Schwarzschild half-space exterior.

</details>


### [200] [Hopf type theorem for surfaces of constant weighted mean curvature in Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23036)
*Jiaming Chen,Shanze Gao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究三维光滑度量测度空间中的紧致浸入零亏格曲面，引入新的加权平均曲率概念，证明了在满足特定Ricci曲率与权函数f条件时，具有常加权平均曲率的曲面必为全脐曲面，并将该结果应用于对偶Christoffel-Minkowski问题的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光滑度量测度空间中，加权几何（如测度曲率概念）已被广泛研究，但关于常加权平均曲率的零亏格紧致浸入曲面的分类问题尚待解决。同时，该分类结果与对偶Christoffel-Minkowski问题的唯一性密切相关。

Method: 引入新的加权平均曲率定义，利用Ricci曲率与权函数f之间的条件约束，通过几何分析（如极大值原理、稳定性分析等）证明常加权平均曲率的紧致浸入零亏格曲面必为全脐曲面，并将该几何结论应用于对偶Christoffel-Minkowski问题的唯一性证明。

Result: 建立了常加权平均曲率的紧致浸入零亏格曲面在特定曲率条件下的全脐性分类定理，并由此推导出对偶Christoffel-Minkowski问题的唯一性结果。

Conclusion: 本文在加权几何框架下推广了经典的全脐曲面分类定理，给出了其对偶Christoffel-Minkowski问题的唯一性应用，为加权测度空间中的几何分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We consider compact immersed surfaces of genus zero in a three-dimensional smooth metric measure space \((M^3,g,e^{-f}\mathrm{d}V_{g})\). We introduce a new weighted mean curvature and prove that any such surface whose weighted mean curvature is constant must be totally umbilical, provided that a condition relating the Ricci curvature of $M$ and the weight function $f$ is satisfied. We also give an application of this result to the uniqueness of the dual Christoffel-Minkowski problem.

</details>


### [201] [Semilinear overdetermined problems, a divergence formula, and geometric inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23184)
*Benedito Leandro,Ilton Menezes,Rafael Novais*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文针对满足Serrin型问题的n维紧致带边黎曼流形，证明了边界面积的锐利上下界；提出了一个特殊向量场的散度公式，并应用于V-次静态、静态和静电流形，证明了Minkowski型不等式和面积-电荷不等式，最终推广了Boucher-Gibbons-Horowitz经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立静态及相关度量（V-静态、次静态、静电磁形）上的几何与分析不等式，推广经典结果并统一处理带边流形的边界面积估计问题。

Method: 通过构造特殊向量场并推导其散度公式，结合Neumann边界条件和Serrin型问题的几何结构，运用比较几何与PDE方法证明边界面积的锐利估计、Minkowski型不等式及面积-电荷不等式。

Result: 获得了边界面积的锐利上下界；建立了V-次静态流形在不同Neumann边界条件下的Minkowski型不等式；证明了紧致与非紧致静电磁形上的面积-电荷不等式；推广了Boucher-Gibbons-Horowitz定理，即满足零收敛条件的静态时空的渐近双曲空间必为双曲空间。

Conclusion: 该散度公式是统一处理静态及推广静态几何中刚性问题的关键工具，所得的Minkowski型和面积-电荷不等式以及广义BGH结果具有重要几何与物理意义。

Abstract: Considering an $n$-dimensional compact Riemannian manifold with a boundary that satisfies a Serrin-type problem, we prove sharp upper and lower bounds for the area of such a boundary. Then, we present the main result of this paper: a divergence formula for a special vector field on a given Riemannian manifold. This divergence formula is closely related to sub-static manifolds and related metrics, e.g., $V$-static, static, and electrostatic manifolds. We show Minkowski-type inequalities for $V$-sub-static manifolds under different Neumann boundary conditions. Moreover, we prove an area-charge inequality for compact (and noncompact) electrostatic manifolds. The main application of the divergence formula presented in this work generalizes the classical result of Boucher--Gibbons--Horowitz: we prove that an asymptotically hyperbolic space of a static spacetime satisfying the null convergence condition must be the hyperbolic space.

</details>


### [202] [Convex functions with symplectic Hessian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23236)
*Jose Rafael Santiago Arellano*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了实Monge-Ampère方程det Hess(u)=1在R^{2m}上，且Hess(u)每点位于辛群Sp(2m,R)中的凸解的三阶导数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Nitsche对R^2上实Monge-Ampère方程Bernstein定理的经典证明进行几何解释并推广到更高维度，从而在辛几何约束下获得三阶导数估计。

Method: 采用Nitsche经典方法的几何诠释与推广，利用辛群约束条件将二维情形扩展到2m维，并对m=1情形优化常数。

Result: 获得了满足辛约束的凸解的三阶导数估计；在m=1时改进了Nitsche常数及Calabi的相关估计，并构造出具有有趣几何行为的解的例子。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典的Bernstein型估计推广到具有辛结构约束的高维Monge-Ampère方程，并给出了更优的常数与新的几何实例。

Abstract: We prove a third-order derivative estimate for convex solutions to the real Monge-Ampère equation ${\rm det}\,{\rm Hess}(u) = 1$ on an open set in $\mathbb{R}^{2m}$ under the additional assumption that ${\rm Hess}(u)$ lies in ${\rm Sp}(2m,\mathbb{R})$ at every point. Our method is a geometric interpretation and extension to higher dimensions of Nitsche's classical proof of the Bernstein theorem for the real Monge-Ampère equation on $\mathbb{R}^2$. For $m = 1$ we also improve Nitsche's constant as well as some estimates due to Calabi, and we construct examples of solutions with interesting geometric behavior.

</details>


### [203] [Fixed Point Homogeneous Orbifolds with Positive Sectional Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23364)
*Jan Nienhaus,Dennis Wulle*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对具有正截面曲率且不动点齐次的黎曼轨形进行了分类，分类直到等变微分同胚。作为推论，得到了具有最大对称秩的正曲率轨形的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正截面曲率的黎曼流形分类是几何学中的经典问题，轨形（orbifold）作为流形的推广在几何和拓扑中具有重要地位。对称性是理解高曲率空间结构的关键工具，研究最大对称秩的正曲率轨形有助于深入理解正曲率空间的几何与拓扑性质。

Method: 通过分类"不动点齐次"（fixed point homogeneous）的黎曼轨形，利用等变微分同胚作为等价关系进行分类。固定点齐次是指轨形上等距群作用的不动点集具有某种齐次性条件，这是一种与技术性假设，使得可以用类似于流形情况的分类方法处理轨形。

Result: 得到了固定点齐次、正截面曲率黎曼轨形的完整分类（直到等变微分同胚），并由此推导出正曲率且具有最大对称秩的轨形的分类结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将正曲率流形的分类结果推广到了轨形的范畴，为理解正曲率轨形的对称结构提供了完整的刻画。

Abstract: We classify fixed point homogeneous Riemannian orbifolds with positive sectional curvature up to equivariant diffeomorphism. As a corollary we obtain a classification of positively curved orbifolds with maximal symmetry rank.

</details>


### [204] [Demailly-Kollár continuity on klt pairs, and applications to alpha and delta invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23505)
*Tamás Darvas,Kewei Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在法正规复解析klt对上建立了关于适应测度的Demailly-Kollár型连续性定理，并以此为工具证明了紧致正规Kähler klt对上alpha与delta不变量的解析版本与除子版本相等，同时证明了孤立log terminal奇点处两种局部alpha不变量相等且共同值等于Li的归一化体积的n次方根。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成由第二作者发起的程序：建立alpha与delta不变量在解析版本（基于plurisubharmonic函数）与除子版本（基于代数几何）之间的统一性，并解决局部奇点处alpha不变量的代数刻画问题。

Method: 首先建立Demailly-Kollár型连续性定理，处理关于适应测度的plurisubharmonic函数的连续性问题；利用该定理将全局与局部的不变量联系起来，证明解析与除子版本的相等性。

Result: 证明了两类主要恒等式：(1) 紧致正规Kähler klt对上alpha和delta不变量解析版本与除子版本相等；(2) 孤立log terminal奇点的两种局部alpha不变量（Guedj-Trusiani引入）相等且等于Li归一化体积的n次方根。

Conclusion: 这些恒等式具有几何推论：delta不变量的相等性给出大上同调类中扭曲Kähler-Einstein方程可解性的Yau-Tian-Donaldson型除子判据（无需扭曲的半正定假设）；局部alpha恒等式代数地确定了孤立log terminal奇点附近正曲率KE度量存在的临界指数，证实了Guedj-Trusiani猜想。

Abstract: We establish a Demailly-Kollár type continuity theorem for plurisubharmonic functions with respect to adapted measures on normal complex analytic klt pairs.
  As applications, we prove the equality of the analytic and divisorial versions of the alpha and delta invariants on compact normal Kähler klt pairs, thereby completing a program initiated by the second author. We further show that the two local alpha invariants introduced by Guedj and Trusiani for an isolated log terminal singularity coincide and that their common value is the nth root of Li's normalized volume.
  These identities have geometric consequences. The equality for the delta invariant yields a Yau--Tian--Donaldson type divisorial criterion for the solvability of twisted Kähler-Einstein equations in big cohomology classes, without a semipositivity assumption on the twist. The local alpha identity determines algebraically the critical exponent governing the existence of positively curved KE metrics near an isolated log terminal singularity, thus confirming a conjecture of Guedj and Trusiani.

</details>


### [205] [A Lean Formalization of Hamilton's Three-Manifold Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21502)
*Bennett Chow,Yuan Liao,Ziyang Qin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Hamilton 1982年关于正Ricci曲率闭连通三维流形定理的Lean形式化验证。该开发包含Ricci流的短时存在性定理以及大量几何分析基础设施（黎曼张量微积分、Levi-Civita联络、Ricci流演化方程、最大原理、三维曲率代数、Ricci pinching保持及改进估计）。形式化采用了替代的blow-up路径而非Hamilton原始的规范化流证明，并集成了时间一致短时存在性、极大延续、无局部塌缩及Cheeger-Gromov-Hamilton紧致性等配套开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Hamilton的经典微分几何定理（正Ricci曲率三维闭流形为球面空间形式）在证明助手Lean中完全形式化，以检验该领域几何分析论证在形式化环境中的可机器验证性，并建立可供后续工作复用的严格数学基础设施。

Method: 采用Lean证明助手进行形式化验证。核心方法包括：(1) 对Ricci流建立短时存在性定理；(2) 开发丰富的几何分析基础设施，包括黎曼张量微积分、Levi-Civita联络、演化方程、最大原理等；(3) 利用三维曲率代数结构实现Ricci pinching的保持和改进估计；(4) 采用替代的blow-up路由而非Hamilton原始的规范化流证明路径；(5) 集成了时间一致短时存在性、极大延续、无局部塌缩和Cheeger-Gromov-Hamilton紧致性等配套管线。

Result: 成功在Lean中形式化验证了Hamilton定理（正Ricci曲率三维闭连通流形），所有主要组件均被记录状态与来源。形式化采用blow-up替代路径完成，配套的紧致性与延续性管线已包含在工件中，Hamilton论证所使用的接口和推论已记录，而完整构造细节将见第二作者的博士论文。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了大规模几何分析定理在证明助手中形式化的可行性，为Ricci流及相关几何分析结果的可验证形式化奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We describe a Lean formalization of Hamilton's 1982 theorem on closed, connected three-manifolds with positive Ricci curvature. The development contains a short-time existence theorem for Ricci flow and substantial geometric-analysis infrastructure: Riemannian tensor calculus, the Levi--Civita connection, Ricci-flow evolution equations, scalar and tensor maximum principles, three-dimensional curvature algebra, preservation of Ricci pinching, and Hamilton's improved pinching estimate. The formalization follows an alternative blow-up route, rather than Hamilton's original normalized-flow proof. Its time-uniform short-time existence, maximal continuation, no-local-collapsing, and Cheeger--Gromov--Hamilton compactness pipelines have been formalized and are included in the artifact, while we give only a brief account of these companion developments and record the interfaces and consequences used by the Hamilton argument; a detailed exposition of their full constructions is deferred to the second author's forthcoming thesis. We interweave representative Lean declarations with their mathematical meaning and record the status and provenance of every major component. All source-level status claims are tied to the source release identified below.

</details>
